# Live Theater Campaign Planner Implementation Plan

**Produced-by:** stage G planning agent (Claude Opus 5.5) in worktree `.worktrees/campaign-planner`, branch `feat/campaign-planner`; revised 2026-10-15 for crit round 1 (verdict on `b9a36dc`, findings F1-F17).

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Use superpowers:test-driven-development for every pure-logic change (red first). Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Implement roadmap stage G (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-campaign-planner-design.md`, binding, including its "Vanilla and W&L model" and "Corrections" sections). Each AI alliance gets a daily, fog-honest, per-theater campaign plan built from live vanilla state: believed enemy armies from vanilla's own campaign fog grid, commander profiles from the live vanilla commander records, sub-theater scoring with a time-decayed historical prior and hysteresis, a supply-reach gate, active reconnaissance (cavalry screens, pickets, an advance guard and belief-driven probes placed where beliefs are oldest or approaches unwatched — vanilla never scouts), a force allocation over every AI formation, competence-plus-prior commander appointments, and idempotent executors that steer vanilla's own campaign AI (offensive target pointers, `followedcampaignobjective`, `theaterposition`, availability, capital pledges, transfers, `MoveUnitTo`, depots, forts, railroads). The phased-plan CIC, the probe/package layer, the army-area anchors and their patches are deleted.

**Architecture:** New folder `src/WhiskeyRealism/Strategic/Planner/`. Pure contracts, snapshot, belief model, profiles, sectors, planner, logistics gate, allocator, appointment planner, mover/depot/railroad state machines are in the test csproj; runtime readers and executors (`*Runtime.cs`) are not. Two calls per alliance in the daily strategic review (`CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewPlan` builds the snapshot and runs beliefs → profiles → plan → allocation; `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewExecute` runs appointments and executors); executors write vanilla state only from that review. Harmony patches read the published `CampaignPlanStore` and steer vanilla's own inputs (Postfix pointers, availability Postfixes, snapshot-restore filters, argument edits); the only new Prefix-skip is the railroad line choice, approved by the user 2026-10-15 (spec C11, P14).

**Tech stack:** C# netstandard2.1, BepInEx 5.4 + HarmonyX, Unity 2021.3.16f1 Mono x64. Console harness `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests` (net8.0, auto-discovered `internal static class *Tests { public static void Run() }`).

**Base:** branch `feat/campaign-planner` (worktree `.worktrees/campaign-planner`, `refs` symlinked), cut from `main` `163e681`. Stage E (need-driven policy) merged to `main` at `99bf4eb` (2026-10-15). Stage F merged to `main` at `9aa71cf`, and this branch merged `main` `93f6a82` (2026-10-15). G0 starts once `fix/campaign-perk-ids` has merged (cross-stage gates below); merge `main` again then. Each workstream works on `feat/campaign-planner-<gN>` cut from `feat/campaign-planner` after G0 merges, and merges back in the order below. `fix/campaign-perk-ids` merged to `main` at `c4b9f34`; this branch merged `main` `4ba3805` at `c66db8b` before G0 (2026-10-15). `main` `742d7e4` (BC9 #80, logistics fixes #81-#91 incl. `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` = BUG-LOG-001..014, W&L economy #92-#99) merged after the G0 landing; `fix/campaign-terrain-bugs` claims #100 and up and `fix/arms-bugs` follows it; stage G ordinals are assigned at G7.

**Inputs (read before your workstream):** the spec (all of it; the "Vanilla and W&L model" section is the anchor set); `docs/wl-player-model.md`; `docs/vanilla-vision-and-movement.md` § Campaign vision / Campaign movement; `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-wl-chain-of-command-design.md` §D-§E; root `AGENTS.md`; `src/WhiskeyRealism/{Strategic,Patches}/AGENTS.md`; `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/AGENTS.md`. Decompile `/tmp/gt_src/asm/Assembly-CSharp.decompiled.cs` (build 14703111; regenerate per `docs/findings.md` if `/tmp` was wiped).

## Global Constraints

- **Vanilla first.** Before code that reads or writes vanilla state, read the full vanilla body and its callers. Cite anchor lines in the file header comment and in the commit message. Every task below names the anchors it rests on; code not grounded in them is rejected in review.
- **Never throw from a patch.** Reflection is cached in static fields, wrapped in try/catch, and logged once (`OnceLog.Warning("campaign-planner:<static key>", …)`). Snapshot-restore filters restore in a `[HarmonyFinalizer]`.
- **`TelemetryPerf.Scope` + `PatchFirstFire.Mark` on every patch entry.** `using (TelemetryPerf.Scope("campaign.patch.<short-name>", TelemetryLayer.Campaign, TelemetryCategory.Performance, 2.0))` and `PatchFirstFire.Mark("campaign.patch.<short-name>")` with a literal id (`tools/smoke-check.py` collects literals only). Situational ids go into `tools/smoke-situational.txt` with a reason.
- **Static OnceLog keys** (no instance id or name in a key).
- **Scoped state.** Every static per-campaign cache registers `CampaignScope.Register("<name>", Reset)` from its owner's static constructor (`Util/ScopedState.cs`). No hand-wired resets in `StrategicCoordinator.ResetAll`.
- **Patches read, the review writes.** Planner state (beliefs, plan, allocation, task ledgers) is written only inside `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewPlan`/`ReviewExecute` (daily review) and the battle-result event handler. Patches read `CampaignPlanStore` through read-only accessors. Executor bookkeeping (issued-move tracker, depot task state) is written by the executors inside the review.
- **W&L.** One classification for the whole stage: stage F's `WlStrategicOrderBridge.ChainClassOf(int allianceId, Regiment unit)` → `WlChainClass { None, CicSide, PlayerCommand, PlayerMapGroup, CarryingGroup }`, read through `WlChainRules.MayMove` (moves, stops, list changes, pledges: `None`/`CarryingGroup` only), `WlChainRules.OrderOnly` (`PlayerMapGroup`: a W&L dispatch through `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue`), `WlChainRules.MayMove` (who may be pledged or hold a must-hold role: `None`/`CarryingGroup`) and `WlChainRules.CountsTowardCoverage` (who counts in the planner's hold sums: those plus the map-group player's group, by position; see the decision record in § G4). `PlayerCommand` folds `dlcw_isundercommander`, `DLC_WL.IsMovedByPlayer` (45797) and the units inside the player's command; it is never moved, stopped, re-staged, re-parented or given a cavalry order. Never appoint/displace `dlc_chosencommander` or a commander with `IsServingUnderPlayer()` (60695). CIC player → the planner stands down for that alliance; vanilla's job-machine predicate (11368: `dlc_scenarioactive && GetPlayerCommandHierarchy() == -1 && !IsCommanderInChief()`) → the planner's executors pause for every alliance, as vanilla's job machine does; only bookkeeping runs (spec C5).
- **Alliance bounds.** The planner runs for alliances 0 and 1 only. Faction index ≠ alliance id: map through `AIFaction.GetAIFactionIDFromAllianceID` (10413) by reflection, bound-check every per-faction array (`AIArea` arrays are sized by alliance count but indexed by faction index, 10679-10692).
- **Fog-honest.** Only `EnemyArmyModel` beliefs (via `CampaignSnapshot.Beliefs`) feed planner, allocator and the migrated ledgers. Only `EnemyDetectionRuntime` reads raw enemy positions. The snapshot copies no vanilla enemy aggregate (`AIArea.points`, `distancepoints`, `enemycampaignunitsstrength*`, `enemyavgcampaignunits`, `avgenemytroopmoraleinzone`, `positiondeficit.enemystrength`, `recruitmentheatmapenemy`: all built from every enemy, `UpdateAreaPoints` at its destination, 10535-10552, 10582-10594). Gate `UnitIsHidden` on `GameVars.usefow` (101188 NRE).
- **All-on config.** Every new bool flag defaults `true` with a truthful description. A flag is bound in the slice that first reads it (config-audit's UNREAD finding is a gate) and removed only in G6; `Plugin.cs` edits are minimal and integrator-merged. `python3 -I tools/config-audit.py .` → 0.
- **No dead code.** `python3 -I tools/orphan-sweep.py .` → 0 and `--include-test-only` lists nothing this stage added, at the end of every workstream for its files and repo-wide at G7.
- **Tests.** Each workstream owns one test file `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/<Name>Tests.cs` (`internal static class <Name>Tests { public static void Run() }`), adds a `<Compile Include="..\..\src\WhiskeyRealism\Strategic\Planner\<File>.cs" Link="<File>.cs" />` per new pure file and removes the entry of every deleted file. Assertions use `TestHarness.AssertEqual/AssertTrue/AssertFalse`. Write the failing test first, see it fail (exit 134 on the first failing assertion), then implement.
- **Pure means no Unity or vanilla types.** Positions are `float X, Z`. Stage F's `WlChainClass` (an `internal` enum, so contract members of that type are `internal`), `Theater`, `DirectorPosture`, `PersonalityVector`, `BattleHistoryRecord` (pure strategic types) are allowed.
- **Exclusive file ownership.** Each workstream edits only its listed files plus its own test file and csproj lines. Shared files (`StrategicCoordinator.cs`, `PersistenceDto.cs`, `SidecarDtoCodec.cs`, `Plugin.cs`, `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs`, `docs/patch-catalog.md`, `docs/handoff.md`) are edited only where this plan says so, minimally; stage F edits some of them and several files this stage also edits (listed under shared-file exceptions), and the integrator merges.
- **Gates before each commit:** `./build.sh` 0 warnings; test build 0 warnings; `dotnet run --project tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests.csproj` exit 0 + PASS count; `python3 -I tools/config-audit.py .` 0; `python3 -I tools/orphan-sweep.py .` 0 (`--include-test-only` adds nothing); `python3 -I tools/test_smoke_check.py` OK. `git log --oneline -10 && git status` before each commit. Messages `<type>(campaign-planner): <summary> [G<n>]`, then the anchors, then `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **No new Transpilers.** No new Prefix-skip other than `RailroadLineSteeringPatch` (spec C11; approved by the user 2026-10-15).
- **Patch ordinals** are assigned by the integrator at G7 in merge order (stages E/F take ordinals in parallel; next free on `main` `742d7e4` is #100, claimed by `fix/campaign-terrain-bugs`, then `fix/arms-bugs`). Workstreams refer to new patches by class name.

## Plan decisions (resolving the spec's open points)

| # | Point | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| P1 | Where the planner runs | Two calls per alliance per review in `RunStrategicReviewCore`, both inside `if (operationalRuntimeReady)`: `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewPlan(int alliance, int day, int month, int year, DirectorPosture tempo)` right after the published `UpdateFiscalIntent` call (`StrategicCoordinator.cs:434` on `main` `93f6a82`, after stages E and F) and its policy step, before `UpdateFrontLedger` (`:439`), so the front and defense ledgers read today's beliefs; `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewExecute(int alliance)` right after `UpdateDefenseIntent` (`:443`), so the executors see units the defense runner just committed as busy. Both return immediately for the CIC player's alliance, before `aifaction` exists, and for alliances ≥ 2. While vanilla's job-machine predicate holds (11368; spec C5), `ReviewExecute` returns immediately for **every** alliance and `ReviewPlan` runs bookkeeping only (snapshot, detection, beliefs, ledger aging; no replan, no publish). **Mirror rule:** while vanilla's predicate holds, vanilla's return at 11368 freezes the job machine of **every** faction, so the planner's executors pause for **every** alliance: no `MoveUnitTo`, no W&L orders, no cavalry orders, no appointments, no objective/`theaterposition` writes, no depot wishes, and the railroad Prefix lets vanilla's own roll run (vanilla keeps rolling railroads on the alliance tick, 81741). The enemy never moves while vanilla's own enemy AI is frozen. Non-executing bookkeeping keeps running for both alliances: the snapshot, detection and belief updates, and ledger aging (recon mission days, depot task timeouts, appointment protection windows); the plan and allocation are not recomputed (hysteresis counters do not advance), so the last published plan simply ages and the stale-plan rule retires it from the patches after 2 review days. The coordinator's state-only ledgers keep running for both alliances (era, capital threat, director posture, fiscal, policy, construction); the ledgers that issue moves or mirror job-machine work skip for both (defense intent, front, formation directives, the old probe/army-area path until G6). (Integrator decision 2026-10-15.) **Belief lag:** `UpdateCapitalThreat` and `PublishDirectorPosture` run before `ReviewPlan` (`:413-414`) and deliberately read the previous review's beliefs (a one-day lag; `PublishDirectorPosture` is the `tempo` input of today's `ReviewPlan`); on the first review after a load they read the sidecar-restored model (G6), and on the first review of a new campaign the model is empty, so capital threat reads no believed enemy for that one day. During the pause beliefs keep updating from detections (bookkeeping), so the state-only ledgers that keep running read current beliefs. Until G6 the old CIC/probe path runs alongside (P10). Scopes `campaign.planner.review` and `campaign.planner.execute` (4.0 ms). |
| P2 | Snapshot cost | One `CampaignSnapshot` per alliance per review, built from one pass over `BattleUnits.campaignunitlist` (79974-79994) plus the faction's lists and `aiarea`. Pooled lists, rebuilt in place. Budget ≤ 2 ms per alliance at 20x for the snapshot and ≤ 4 ms per alliance for the whole review (G7 smoke 1.7). There is no deferral path: a review over budget is a defect to fix, because deferring an alliance would run its executors outside the review. |
| P3 | Score scale | Every planner score term is normalized to [0, 1] before weighting; the total is a weighted sum with weights as `public const` in `TheaterPlanner` (pinned by tests): `ValueWeight 0.30`, `BalanceWeight 0.25`, `ReachWeight 0.15`, `DistanceWeight 0.10`, `ExposureWeight 0.10`, `CapitalThreatWeight 0.10`. Prior: `PriorLambda 0.08`, so the prior's contribution is ≤ `PriorMaxContribution = 0.08` and any live margin above 0.08 wins (harness-tested). |
| P4 | Hysteresis | Switch main effort only when `best − current > SwitchMargin (0.12)` for `SwitchDays (3)` consecutive reviews, or immediately on a trigger: decisive battle in/near the current target (existing `BattleReplanQuery` rule), believed capital threat ≥ `CapitalThreatTrigger (0.6)`, current objective accomplished or unavailable. **Amended at G3 verify r1 (integrator):** the capital trigger fires on onset only, latched in `PlannerState` until the threat falls below `CapitalThreatRelease (0.4)`, and while it stands the margin rule applies; the odds trigger has a deadband: the current main effort is dropped when its odds fall below `RequiredRatio − OddsDeadband (0.10)` or stay below `RequiredRatio` for `SwitchDays` consecutive reviews. A trigger keeps the current main effort while it is within `Epsilon` of the best. |
| P5 | Randomness | Softmax over candidates within `Epsilon (0.04)` of the best, temperature `0.02`, `System.Random(seed)` with `seed = CampaignSeed ^ (alliance * 7919) ^ DecisionSerial`; `CampaignSeed` = 64-bit FNV-1a over the UTF-8 bytes of `SceneManagement.currentcampaignscenario` (36382, set when a campaign loads, 36576; stage F's `ComplianceCareerKey` keys on the same field) + `"|"` + the first review's date `yyyy-mm-dd`, computed once at that first review and persisted (never `string.GetHashCode`, which differs between runtimes); a v1/v2 sidecar without it computes it at its first review after the load; `DecisionSerial` increments per main-effort decision and is persisted. |
| P6 | Belief dynamics | `EnemyArmyModel`: visible → confidence 1; unseen → `confidence = 0.5^(days / 7)`; uncertainty radius `= seenRadius + DriftPerDay(unitTyp) × days`, where `DriftPerDay(unitTyp)` is vanilla's **maximum campaign speed** for that unit type in map units per game day (corrected 2026-10-15, G0 landing: the earlier road-march bound `unitbasicspeed[t,1] × unitbasicspeed[15,1] × basicunitspeed / terrainspeccost[7] / 365.25` was too low by the campaign factor ×30, the 2× of cost-1 movement and every speed-up). Vanilla moves a campaign group by `num33 = num25 × num11 × num28 × num9 × speedadjustmentduetotransportcap × commanderspeedadjustment × num30` (134100) with `num25 = Δcurrenttimefromstart` in floating years, clamped to 1 (133891-133896; campaign time 30171: a time step, not a normalization) and `num9 = generalcampaignunitspeedfactor` on the campaign (134083; campaignprefs 215-216 = 30, loaded 55336). The bound is the product of each term's maximum: `unitbasicspeed[t, 1]` (unittyp ≥ 14 always the mounted column, 133350-133354) × `unitbasicspeed[15, 1]` (`groupspeed`'s ceiling: it starts there and only falls to the slowest member, 120179, 120438-120440; applied 133991) × `max(skilled cartographers)²` (own and parent army, 133369-133376; `movementspeedskilledcartographers`, campaignprefs 2380-2385, max 1.2) × `max(flying column)²` (133377-133384; `movementspeedflyingcolumn`, 2388-2393, max 1.25) × `max(1, doublequickspeedfact[t], runningspeedfact[t])` (forced march, 133509-133516) × `max(foot cavalry)²` (133516-133525; `forcedmarchspeedfootcavalry`, 2402-2407, max 1.3; with `Fix Perk Effect Scope` only on a forced march, which the bound includes anyway) × `max(max(river expedition)², railroadspeedadj)`: the two cost-1 modes exclude each other — River Expedition applies only on terrain 3/4/5 (133482-133491; `rivermovementspeedriverexpedition`, 2516-2521, max 2.0), `railroadspeedadj` only on a train (`currentmodeltype == 0`, 133401-133404; `1 + project 101 level × project_militaryrailroadtransportspeed`, 213217; the runtime reads the believed enemy alliance's live value, a scalar, never a position) × `basicunitspeed` (133535) × `1 / min_k terrainspeccost[k]` (= 1: Rivers, River Crossings, Sea and the railroad areas cost 1, campaignprefs 724, 734, 764, 774; `GetTerrainSpeedAdjustment` 132888-132900 maps a land unit on terrain ≥ 9 to the road cost 2 unless it is on a train) × `generalcampaignunitspeedfactor` (134083) × 2 (the downhill slope term `num31 ≤ 2`, 134013, multiplied into `num28` at 134070) × `max(1, speedfactorretreat, speedfactorfallback × debug_aicheat_fasterfallbackspeed)` (134050-134062; retreat and fallback are the only `num28` terms above 1; 53208-53210) × `max(1, speedfactorretreat)` (G1 as built: the safety-zone retreat term at 134062 can stack on mode 5) / 365.25. Every other term only shrinks it (`commanderspeedadjustment` is 1 on the campaign, 132838-132841; `aiarrivalfactor = distance / max distance ≤ 1`, 8850-8856; wounded, state support, readiness, training, weather 133414-133475; the night stop 133958-133961; land torpedoes 133385-133398). Each array factor is the array's maximum over perk levels. The bound is deliberately loose: the uncertainty radius widens fast, and a recon sighting (G1b) is what narrows a belief again. The runtime passes it as `Func<int, float>` to the pure model; expire at 30 days or when confidence < 0.05; an enemy-held objective town is public (ownership), but its fort garrison's strength is known only once the side has seen or contacted that garrison (G1 detection rule; G0 landing F2): the snapshot carries `GarrisonStrength` for own and allied forts only. |
| P7 | Capital floor | Floor = max(vanilla's own requirement, `CapitalFloorRatio 1.0 ×` believed enemy strength within relocation range weighted as vanilla's 11712-11741 formula). Vanilla-need part: rewritten #4 pre-pledges into `groupstodefendcapital` only while vanilla's `num2 > 0` (unchanged rule). Excess: `CapitalGuard` role, held by the mover and withheld by the availability Postfixes (spec C9). |
| P8 | Write budget | Per alliance per review: ≤ `MaxMovesPerDay 6` `MoveUnitTo` calls, ≤ 1 appointment, ≤ 1 depot wish, ≤ 2 `theaterposition` writes per formation per week. A move is confirmed on the next review when the formation's last waypoint changed toward the target or it moved ≥ 25% of the way; else `Failed`, one retry at the next review, then the allocator reassigns the role (`GaveUp`). |
| P9 | Player reliability | `ForceAllocator` takes `float playerReliability01` from stage F's `PlayerComplianceRuntime.Reliability` (1 outside a career or with F's ledger off; else `PlayerComplianceLedger.Reliability`, `Strategic/PlayerComplianceLedger.cs:54` on `main` `93f6a82`) and discounts a `PlayerMapGroup` formation's strength with F's `PlayerComplianceLedger.StrengthWeight(bool playerOwned, float reliability)`. G0 starts after F merges, so no interim value exists. Thresholds: `UnreliableBelow 0.5`, `NonComplianceDays 5`. |
| P10 | Running beside the old path until G6 | G0 adds `Enable Campaign Planner` (planner review + telemetry) and `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` (all planner writes). Until G6 deletes the old path, G5's executors check that the old writer for the same surface is gone or stand down for that surface: G5 rewires #2, #3, #4 in place (one writer per surface), and the mover refuses formations held by `CoordinatedOperationRuntime.IsPackageLocked` until G6 deletes it. G6 removes the second flag's stand-down branches. Both flags stay as rollbacks after G7 (planner off = vanilla campaign AI plus the kept ledgers). |
| P11 | Sub-theater sectors (spec C15; default confirmed 2026-10-15) | The planner's own `SectorModel`: AI areas grouped into sectors by a deterministic flood fill over area adjacency (`GetClosestAreaDistance` ≤ `aimaximumdistancetosearchforunitrelocations`, 14835) seeded at each live objective, each sector tagged with the existing `Theater` of its centroid (`TheaterClassifier.FromPosition`). `FrontSectorLedger` stays 6-bucket and switches to beliefs (G1). Re-keying the ledger and its readers is out of scope. |
| P12 | Historical prior | `HistoricalPriorTable` (pure) is `HistoricalOperationCatalog`'s profiles reduced to `(alliance, objectiveIds[], windowStart, windowEnd, priority)`; weight 1 inside the window, `0.5^(monthsOutside / 3)` outside, × `priority / 3`. The per-phase machinery (phases, dynamic rules, tempo presets) is deleted. `HistoricalArmyAreaRegistry` → `HomeTheaterPrior` (formation/commander name → home `Theater`), used by the allocator as a 0.05 tie-break toward the home theater. |
| P13 | Appointment protection | A planner appointment is protected for `ProtectionDays 60`: #6's Prefix sets `alliance.worstcommanderidperlevel[r] = -1` while that slot holds a protected commander and records which slots it cleared; its Finalizer writes the saved id back into exactly those slots and touches no other slot. Vanilla's loop skips a slot whose worst id is −1 (17017), so it never writes a slot the Prefix cleared, while the slots vanilla consumed (both ids set to −1 by vanilla, 17027-17028) stay as vanilla left them. Vertical replacement (17662) and the CIC re-pick (81315) are not blocked (spec C16, C18). |
| P14 | Railroad steering (spec C11) | **Approved by the user 2026-10-15** (option a): the Prefix-skip on `UpdateRailroadConstruction` keeps vanilla's exact per-line probability and number of starts and only chooses which startable line starts; a success on a line vanilla would not start starts nothing (G5c rule). Built in G5c, behind `Enable Railroad Steering`. |
| P15 | W&L career transfer fix (spec C1) | **Decided yes (integrator, 2026-10-15):** a vanilla bug fix (BUG-WL-013), argument-only, never for player-owned units. Built in G5b behind `Enable W&L Career Transfer Fix`. |
| P16 | Vertical replacement on the player's side in a career (BUG-WL-014) | **User 2026-10-15: leave as vanilla (intended, won't fix).** Vanilla's `CheckVerticalReplacement` never runs for `playeralliance` (17664). The appointment planner must not add what vanilla withholds: for the W&L player's alliance it never seats a commander over a command that contains the commander's own current command (no subordinate-replaces-superior swaps); it still appoints idle commanders (job-24 style), which vanilla does run on that side. |

## Decisions recorded (2026-10-15; no slice awaits a decision any more; the merge gates are under Dependency order)

1. **C1 — fix vanilla's dead W&L-career transfers** with a `MoveUnit` argument edit scoped to `CheckTransferOfUnits`: **yes** (integrator decision; vanilla bug fix, argument-only, never for player-owned units) → G5b.
2. **C11 — railroad line steering** via a Prefix-skip on `UpdateRailroadConstruction`: **approved by the user**, with vanilla's exact per-line probability and number of starts; only which startable line starts is chosen, and a success on a line vanilla would not start starts nothing. Justification: spec § Corrections, "Prefix-skip justification (C11)" → G5c.
3. **C15 — sub-theater sectors**: the default — the planner's own sector model from AI areas; `FrontSectorLedger` stays at 6 buckets and reads beliefs → G3.
4. **BUG-WL-014 — no vertical replacement on the player's side in a career**: **user: leave as vanilla** (intended / won't fix); the appointment planner adds no vertical swaps there (P16) → G2.

**Campaign chapters (research `scratchpad/research/chapters.md`, 2026-10-15; user decisions by AskUserQuestion 2026-10-15, recorded verbatim; anchors re-derived for this plan, decompile build 14703111):**

5. **Tempo — "Keep chapter pacing (Recommended)".** The planner launches each new main effort only when vanilla's chapter tempo roll allows: the pure `TempoProbability` moves out of #38's code into the planner before G6 deletes #38; the first offensive move of each new main effort (its MainEffort formations, the planned mass) is gated in G5a, and a Support move into enemy-held ground waits for that release (no piecemeal attack ahead of the main effort; Support moves in own or neutral ground are not held, and the W&L map-group player's Support order follows the same rule); faction factors as vanilla 14339 and the per-scenario `aiagressiveness` values. Vanilla's roll (`CheckOffensiveMovements` 14316-14350): `tval = aiagressiveness[CurrentChapter] × timediff × 10 × aiagressivenessoffensiveoperationfaction[alliance] × usedcampaignagressiveness × winter + clamp01(idle³)`, ×`dlcwl_careerincreasedaggressiveness` in W&L (once more when the player's group is in the package), skipped by the 001 Union forced-invasion flag (14303-14306) or `debug_permanentaimovement` (14350); `aiagressiveness` per chapter 001 = 0.00/0.75/1.50/2.00 (`Config/campaignprefs.txt:1417-1420`, header 1416; 001/A has no own prefs), 002 = 0.00/1.15/1.75/2.25 (`Campaigns/002/A/campaignprefs.txt:1417-1420`), faction Union 1.0 / CSA 0.5 (`Config/campaignprefs.txt:1509-1510`, header 1508). The planner's roll replaces vanilla's for the planned mass and is not added to it: G5a withholds every MainEffort member of the mass from vanilla's offensive candidate path (offense only; vanilla's defensive operations may still take it), so only the mover launches it. Support formations are outside the mass; until their decision is released (`TempoReleasedDecision == DecisionSerial`, set by the planner's roll or, when no roll is needed, before the first MainEffort dispatch) they are withheld from vanilla's offensive the same way (never from its defence; never the map-group player), so vanilla's own roll cannot send them ahead of the main effort. → G5a (gate), G6 (deletion order).
6. **1861 targets — "Add the mapping (Recommended)".** A candidate source maps scenario 001's abstract objectives (capitals 2/3/4 and the rest; `campaignobjectives.dat` has empty town lists for ids 0-28) to their towns, still gated by vanilla's `ObjectiveChapters`/`IsDeactivated` (178905-178950); G3's candidate list uses it. G6 re-points the kept readers of the catalog's 001 coordinates (`FrontSectorRuntime` and the formation-directive target, both through `ObjectiveAdapter`) at the published plan; `ObjectiveCatalog` and `ObjectiveAdapter` are deleted in G7's post-smoke step, after Session E has shown a main effort on mapped ground. → G3c, G6, G7.
7. **Late war — "Follow the game's chapter (Recommended)".** Whiskey's era stage follows vanilla's chapter: `TotalWar1864` starts with chapter 3 (211663-211667: Lincoln re-elected), never if he loses. That change lands on `main` in a separate fix slice (`fix/chapters`), not here. Wherever this plan reads the era it means the chapter-driven era from `fix/chapters`: the planner's tempo input `DirectorPosture` (`CampaignPaceLedger`'s LateWarPressure, whose calendar Union runway `input.Year >= 1864` at `CampaignPaceLedger.cs:66` is replaced there), G6's kept era readers (policy, projects, defense, fiscal; spec C17) and the sidecar's `currentEra`. `CampaignPaceLedger`'s two other calendar reads, rule 1 (`TooFastCollapse`, `input.Year <= 1863` at `:58`) and the CSA collapse floor (`IntentBlockedFromPreserve`, `input.Year >= 1864` at `:51`), stay on the calendar by integrator decision (kept so on `fix/chapters`): they are historical timing priors ("history is a weight"), not the late-war stage. `fix/chapters` (on `main` since `3ecda40`) also starts the catalog's `union-late-pressure` window in May 1864, so the merge of `main` that brings it into this branch moves `HistoricalPriorTable`'s `union-late-pressure` row (`HistoricalPriorTable.cs:42`, today `1, 1864`) to `5, 1864` in the same commit (`PriorTableMatchesCatalog`, `TheaterPlannerTests.cs:178`, pins it). The planner reads all three rules only through `DirectorPosture` and adds no calendar-year or `EraStage` test of its own (pinned in G5a: `PlannerReadsNoCalendarEra`).
8. **Pre-war gate (research gap 3; integrator, with the decisions above).** While scenario 001 is at chapter ≤ 0 (vanilla refuses every campaign move, `SetWaypoint` 91366, and its offensive aggression is 0), the planner issues no move, order or steering (G2's appointments still run, since 91366 refuses waypoints only) and `CampaignPaceLedger` does not report `TooQuiet`. → G5a (where `ReviewExecute` runs the executors).

## Dependency order and parallelism

The graph follows the real type dependencies. Two small pure helpers that several slices need, `EnemyBeliefQuery` and `ViewerCoverage`, are G0 contracts (with their tests), and so are `SectorModel` and `LogisticsGate`'s reach functions (`Evaluate`, `ClampToReach`): all four read only G0 snapshot types, and putting them in G0 is what lets G1, G1b, G2 and G3 start in parallel. A slice "builds against G0" only when every type it names is in G0; each remaining edge below names the type that forces it.

```
G0 contracts + snapshot + SectorModel + reach + belief query + flags + review hook + W&L pause gate
   (integrator, sequential; WAITS for fix/campaign-perk-ids to merge to main, then merges main; stages E and F merged, 99bf4eb and 9aa71cf)
  ├─ G1  EnemyArmyModel + detection + ledger migration to beliefs                       ─┐
  ├─ G2  CommanderProfiles + appointments + #6 guard                                     │ start in parallel
  ├─ G3  HistoricalPriorTable + TheaterPlanner + depot tasks (pure files)                 │ against G0
  └─ G1b ReconPlanner + ReconMissionLedger + directive cleanup (pure files)              ─┘
        G1b runtime edits (ReadLocalForceBalance, OperationalProbeRuntime via EnemyDetectionRuntime.BeliefsFor) need G1 merged
        G3 runtime step (CampaignPlannerRuntime: profiles → plan) needs G2 merged (it passes CommanderProfiles.ForSector)
        G1b runtime step (recon tasks after the plan) needs G3 merged (TheaterPlan is produced there)
  G4  ForceAllocator — needs G1b merged (ReconPlanner.PickFormation)
G5b capital floor + replacement flow + recruit routing + career transfer fix — after G4; after stage F (rewrites DefensiveOpsPatch, which F edits)
G5a mover + objective/importance/theater/availability executors + chapter tempo gate + pre-war gate — after G4 and G3c; after stage F and fix/campaign-logistics-bugs
G3c 001 objective ground (mapped candidate source) — after G3 and G4 (merged); before G5a (both edit CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs and CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs: G3c first); in parallel with G5b
G5c construction steering (depots, forts, railroads) — after G5a (the depot builder uses G5a's mover and CampaignWithholdView); after fix/campaign-logistics-bugs
G5d recon executor (moves, scouting orders, recall; old probe stands down) — after G5a (MoveTracker)
G6 removals, migrations, sidecar v3, coordinator rewiring (integrator, sequential)
G7 integration, read census, docs, smoke (integrator)
G8 garrison manning beyond the capital (after G7)
G9 ordnance: re-arming priority by allocation role + scored weapon purchase (after G8; needs G4 roles, G1 beliefs, G5a MoveTracker; WAITS for fix/arms-bugs)
```

Merge order into `feat/campaign-planner`: G0, G1, G2, G3, G1b, G4, G3c, G5b, G5a, G5c, G5d, G6, G7, then G8, then G9. G3c lands before G5a starts, because both edit `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` and `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (G3c: `ReadObjectives` and `LogFirstReview`; G5a: the own-formation read and the executor step); G5b is disjoint from G3c and may be built beside it. `ObjectiveCatalog`/`ObjectiveAdapter` are deleted in G7's post-smoke catalog-removal step, never in G6, so no slice waits for a later one. `fix/chapters` (decision 7) landed on `main` on its own (`3ecda40`) and reaches this branch with the next merge of `main`, which also moves the `union-late-pressure` prior row to May 1864 (decision 7); no planner slice waits for it. G5b and G5a touch disjoint files and may be built in parallel after G4; G5c and G5d may be built in parallel after G5a.

Cross-stage gates: **G9 waits for `fix/arms-bugs`** (vanilla arms bugs F1, F2, F4, F5 and the AI's researched-but-unproduced orders F3 of `research/armament.md`, plus the replacement of Whiskey's industry-need signal): G9 scores models over the per-model stock that F2's `CombineUnits` leak inflates, and shares the arms quality measure that the industry-need replacement introduces; G9 reuses that branch's measure and does not re-fix its bugs. **G0 does not start until stage F (`feat/wl-chain-campaign`; merged at `9aa71cf`, satisfied) and `fix/campaign-perk-ids` have merged to `main`**; `main` is then merged into `feat/campaign-planner` again. F is a G0 gate because the snapshot classifies every own formation with F's `WlStrategicOrderBridge.ChainClassOf` (one W&L classification for the stage) and the allocator reads F's reliability; `fix/campaign-perk-ids` because `SightRadiusFormula` takes its `Fix Perk Id Mismatches` flag. Stage E (need-driven policy, which edits `StrategicCoordinator`'s fiscal region and the sidecar DTO) merged at `99bf4eb`: satisfied. **G5a and G5c wait for `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs`** (merged to `main` at `efd74ef`, merged into this branch with `742d7e4`: satisfied; G5a/G5c re-read its patches #81-#91 before building on them) (zone supply-capacity freeze, job 19 depot branches, raid search gate; the branch has no commits yet at this revision) to merge to `main`; rebase before starting them. **G1b's raid-dependent text** (component 4b: supply-base raids stay with vanilla raid forces) is a behaviour claim that holds only once `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` repairs the raid search gate; G1b's code does not depend on it, so G1b is not gated.

Shared-file exceptions (sequential by merge order, disjoint regions):
- `StrategicCoordinator.cs`: G0 adds the `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewPlan`/`ReviewExecute` calls and the W&L pause change (spec C5: the early return and the per-alliance skip); G3 adds the battle-event forward (`RecordBattleOutcome` → `CampaignPlannerRuntime.OnBattleRecorded`, one line after `BattleHistory.Add`, `:1475` after G0) with its reader (moved from G0, see G0 as built); G1 rewrites `UpdateCapitalThreat`'s enemy read only; G1b `ReadLocalForceBalance`'s enemy sum only; G6 does the removals.
- `Strategic/WlCareerStartGate.cs` and `Patches/MonthlyTickHookPatch.cs` (`:51`, the startup gate): G0 (the pause change); G6 later edits `MonthlyTickHookPatch`'s `CIC` reference (`:24`), a different line.
- `Patches/FortConstructionGovernorPatch.cs`: G1 switches its enemy read (`:301-304`) to beliefs; G5c adds the axis site preference.
- `Patches/DefensiveOpsPatch.cs`: stage F adds its chain-class pledge guard; G5b rewrites the file and keeps that guard (the rewrite pledges only formations `WlChainRules.MayMove` allows); its enemy read moves to beliefs there, not in G1.
- `Strategic/DefenseIntentRuntime.cs`, `Strategic/CoastalDefenseCustomOrderRunner.cs`: stage F edits both (chain class); G1 changes only their enemy-read lines; integrator-merged.
- `Patches/RecruitmentPatch.cs`: G1 (`SafeStrengthRatio` only), G5b (an `internal static bool InZoneRecruiting` / `ActiveAifaction` accessor over the existing `_zoneDepth`/`_activeAifaction` thread-statics, `:16-17`, nothing else), G6 (`BuildIntent` migration).
- `StrategicCoordinator.ReadLocalForceBalance`, `Strategic/OperationalProbeRuntime.cs`: G1b moves their enemy inputs to beliefs; G5d adds the probe stand-down; G6 deletes both. Stage F also edits `OperationalProbeRuntime.cs` (chain class): integrator-merged.
- `Strategic/FormationDirectiveLedger.cs`, `Patches/PerkSelectionPatch.cs`: G1b changes the directive cases / the `:134` mapping; G6 removes `ApplyOperationalProbe` / migrates the plan reads.
- `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs`: created by G5a; G5c adds the depot-task hold, G8 the garrison role.
- `Plugin.cs`: each slice binds the flags it reads (G0, G1, G1b, G2, G5b, G5c, G8), G6 removes. `TelemetryTagPolicy.cs`: G0 registers every new event name of G0-G7 (list in G0); G8 registers its own two.

## G0 — Contracts, snapshot, primitives, flags, review hook, W&L pause (integrator, sequential)

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSnapshot.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/SightRadiusFormula.cs` (pure: `Campaign(int unitTyp, float los, bool hasPerk8, float perk8Factor, float climate105, float rainSnowInfluence, bool liedown, float liedownFactor, float incident63Factor, bool fixPerkIdMismatches)` = the sight radius as painted once `fix/campaign-perk-ids` lands: `maximumlineofsight[unittyp]`, times the Expert Scouts factor `perk_expertscoutsvisibilityrangefactor[GetPerkLevel(8)]` only when unittyp ≤ 13 and `HasPerk(8)`, times the weather term (campaign `1 − climate(105) × influenceofrainsnowonvisibilitycampaign`, battle `lineofsightreduction`), then × `fow_lineofsightreduction_layndown` when lying down and × the W&L incident-63 multiplier for the player's command; with `Fix Perk Id Mismatches` off it computes vanilla's unfixed 101296-101309 radius (the factor on any `HasPerk(8)`). The flag value is an input. Read by the snapshot runtime and G1's viewer check), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyBeliefQuery.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/ViewerCoverage.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/SectorModel.cs` (pure, P11), `Strategic/Planner/LogisticsGate.cs` (pure: `Evaluate`, `ClampToReach`; G3 adds `AddDepotTasks` in `LogisticsGate.DepotTasks.cs`), `Strategic/Planner/PlayerCommandClassifier.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlanStore.cs` (pure, CampaignScope), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (runtime), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (runtime); modify `Plugin.cs` (flags), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs` (event names), `StrategicCoordinator.cs` (two call sites, P1; the W&L pause change below), `Strategic/WlCareerStartGate.cs` (two pure predicates), `Patches/MonthlyTickHookPatch.cs` (`:51` startup gate only); test `CampaignSnapshotTests.cs`.

**Vanilla to read:** `SeparateUnits` 10458-10515; `campaignunitlist` 79974-79994; `GetUnitVisibilityStateOnCampaignMap` 82934-82974; `GetCampaignGroup` 82991-83020; `CheckCurrentOrderUpdate` 8251-8275 (`flag2` 8271); `GetPlayerCommandHierarchy` 44397-44427; `IsCommanderInChief` (47475); the job-machine pause 11368; `IsMovedByPlayer` 45797; `IsUnitUnderCommander` 91168-91223; `GetAIFactionIDFromAllianceID` 10413; `AIArea` fields 10679-10692 (`zoneedges`, `averageposition`, `importancevalues`, `ownpoints`, `supplycapacity`) and `GetClosestAreaDistance` 14835-14860 (the sector adjacency test); `UpdateAreaPoints` 10517-10609 (why `points`/`distancepoints` are not copied); `UpdateSupplyCapacity` 10772-10802; `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` 14080-14158 (busy lists); `FortConstructionOrder.UnitAlreadyConstructing`, `SeaInvasionForce.GetSeaInvasionForceReference`, `RaidForce.IsRaidUnit`; embarkation `SetEmbarkmentMode` 116338-116369 (`embarkmentstarttime` > 0 while embarking, 116348-116360; movement blocked, 133304) and the afloat model types 116383-116417 (`currentmodeltype` 1 sea, 2 river; vanilla's own afloat test 80724); `groupstrengthdirect` 120391-120407; `GetGroupSupplyState` 14063-14066; campaign `commanderrange` 125830-125838; objectives `CampaignObjective.allcampaignobjectives` (178548; `UniqueObjectiveID` 178490, `ObjectiveAlliance` 178505, targets `objectives` 178542 as `Town`/`IIP`), `GetAvailableObjectives(int allianceid, …)` 178827-178866 (own alliance's objectives, scenario match, not deactivated, not accomplished, at least one target held by an enemy; vanilla quirk: the IIP counter is not reset per objective), `IDAccomplished` 178805-178825; `Railroad` fields and `StartConstruction(int, bool checkonly)` 77818-77845; IIP `GetTransportCapacity` 104652-104668, `allianceowner`; `CBuilding` supply depot fields (`Condition`, `constructiontimer`, `Owner`); unit speed for `DriftPerDay` (P6, read in G1; G0 does not implement it): `RegimentMove` 133340-133545 and 134083-134100, `GetTerrainSpeedAdjustment` 132888-132900, `groupspeed` 120179/120438-120440/133991, campaign time 30171. **Interfaces — Produces (every later slice consumes these):**
```csharp
namespace WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.Planner {
  public enum PlayerCommandClass { NotCareer = 0, NoCommand = 1, CommanderInChief = 2, MapGroup = 3, BelowMapLevel = 4, ArmyGroupCommander = 5 }
  [System.Flags] public enum FormationFlags : uint { None = 0, InBattle = 1, OnRetreat = 2, InOffensiveOps = 4, InDefensiveOps = 8,
    CapitalDefense = 16, VanillaDepotBuilder = 32, FortBuilder = 64, SeaInvasion = 128, RaidForce = 256, InTransfer = 512,
    Embarked = 1024, FightingForce = 2048, HasPath = 4096, MoveQueued = 8192 }
  // Embarked = embarkmentstarttime > 0 || currentmodeltype == 1 || currentmodeltype == 2 (116348-116360, 116383-116417, 80724).
  public struct FormationRecord { public int InstanceId; /* in-session lookup only, never persisted */ public string UnitKey; /* name + ":" + commander, the sidecar convention (FormationDirectiveRuntime.UnitKey); persisted identity */
    public int UnitTyp; public float X, Z, WaypointX, WaypointZ;
    public float Strength; /* groupstrengthdirect */ public float Morale; public float Supply01; /* RAW 0..1: max(avg(groupsupplystate[0..3]), supplystate) = vanilla GetGroupSupplyState × 2 (14063-14066 halves both terms, so vanilla's value never exceeds 0.5) */
    public int ReadinessStep; public float CommandRange; public float BugleRange; public int CommanderId;
    public bool HasTheater; public float TheaterX, TheaterZ; public int AreaIndex; public FormationFlags Flags; public string Name;
    internal WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.WlChainClass Chain; /* internal: F's enum is internal (CS0052 otherwise); stage F's ChainClassOf; None outside a W&L career and on the enemy alliance */
    public float CavalryShare; public float SightRadius;
    public int ParentInstanceId; /* smallest containing own top group by allattachedunits (the battle core's D5 rule), -1 root; replaces transform.parent reads */ }
  public struct AreaRecord { public int Index; public float X, Z; /* averageposition */ public float[] EdgeX, EdgeZ; /* zoneedges */
    public float Importance; /* importancevalues[f] */ public float OwnPoints, SupplyCapacity; } // no enemy aggregate (spec C25)
  [System.Flags] public enum ObjectiveRole { None = 0, Capital = 1, RailHub = 2, Port = 4, Industry = 8 }
  // One record per target (Town or IIP) of every active campaign objective of either alliance.
  public struct ObjectiveRecord { public int ObjectiveId; /* UniqueObjectiveID */ public float X, Z; /* the target's live position */ public int HolderAlliance;
    public bool Available; /* objective is in GetAvailableObjectives(snapshot alliance) */ public bool Accomplished; /* IDAccomplished */
    public ObjectiveRole Roles; public float Importance01; public float GarrisonStrength; /* town garrison groupstrengthdirect, 0 for none */ }
  public enum SupplySourceKind { Iip = 0, Depot = 1 }
  public struct SupplySourceRecord { public int Id; public SupplySourceKind Kind; public int Owner; public float X, Z;
    public float TransportCapacity, Condition; public bool Built; public bool Invisible; }
  public struct RailLineRecord { public int Index; public bool Startable; /* StartConstruction(a, checkonly:true) */
    public bool UnderConstruction; public float Progress; public float AX, AZ, BX, BZ; }
  public enum DetectionSource { FogGrid = 0, OwnSideWithinFog = 1, Contact = 2, NoFog = 3, ObjectiveGarrison = 4 }
  [System.Flags] public enum EnemyTraits { None = 0, Embarked = 1, CavalryMajority = 2, OnOwnSoil = 4, NearOwnCoast = 8 }
  // Only what a sighting shows; never a destination, plan state or target. UnitKey is the persisted identity, InstanceId the in-session one.
  public struct EnemyDetection { public int InstanceId; public string UnitKey; public int UnitTyp; public float X, Z, Strength, Guns; public DetectionSource Source; public EnemyTraits Traits; }
  public struct EnemyBelief { public int InstanceId; public string UnitKey; public int UnitTyp; public float X, Z, Strength, Guns; public int LastSeenDay;
    public float Confidence01, UncertaintyRadius; public DetectionSource LastSource; public EnemyTraits Traits; }
  public struct CommanderRecord { public int Id; public int Rank; public float Initiative, Leadership, Administration, Average; /* GetAverageAttributeValue 59175 */
    public bool Available, Navy, Dummy, RefreshCommand, ServingUnderPlayer, IsPlayer; public int CommandInstanceId; public int CommandUnitTyp; public string Name; }
  public enum TheaterPosture { Hold = 0, MainEffort = 1, Support = 2, EconomyOfForce = 3, Screen = 4, Recover = 5 }
  public enum FormationRole { Hold = 0, MainEffort = 1, Support = 2, Reserve = 3, CapitalGuard = 4, DepotBuilder = 5, Recover = 6, Recon = 7 }
  public enum ReconKind { Screen = 0, Picket = 1, AdvanceGuard = 2, Probe = 3 }
  public struct ReconTask { public ReconKind Kind; public int SectorId; public float FromX, FromZ, HoldX, HoldZ; public float Priority; public string Reason; }
  public sealed class SectorPlan { public int SectorId; public WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.Theater Theater; public TheaterPosture Posture;
    public int TargetObjectiveId; public float TargetX, TargetZ; public float RequiredRatio; }
  public sealed class DepotTask { public float X, Z; public int SectorId; public float Priority; }
  public sealed class TheaterPlan { public int Alliance; public int DaySerial; public int MainSectorId = -1; public int MainObjectiveId = -1;
    public float MainRequiredRatio; public System.Collections.Generic.List<SectorPlan> Sectors = new System.Collections.Generic.List<SectorPlan>();
    public float CapitalFloorStrength; public System.Collections.Generic.List<DepotTask> DepotTasks = new System.Collections.Generic.List<DepotTask>();
    public string SwitchReason; public string PriorId; }
  public struct CommanderProfile { public int CommanderId; public float RequiredOddsShift; /* (initiative - 0.5) * -0.5: bold commanders accept worse odds */
    public float AttackBias; /* initiative - leadership*0.5, clamped [-1,1] */
    public float SupplySlack; /* administration * 0.25 of commandrange */ }
  public struct RankBand { public int UnitTyp; public int MinRank, MaxRank; } // GetMin/MaxRankForUnittyp (67003-67058) per unittyp for this alliance
  public struct Assignment { public int InstanceId; public FormationRole Role; public int SectorId; public float TargetX, TargetZ; public string Reason; public int ReconMissionId; /* -1 none */
    public bool Withheld; /* kept out of vanilla's offensive pool: Reserve, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon, Recover, and Hold at sector need (G4) */ }
  public sealed class Allocation { public int Alliance; public System.Collections.Generic.List<Assignment> Assignments = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Assignment>();
    public bool TryGet(int instanceId, out Assignment a);
    public System.Collections.Generic.List<int> Shortfalls = new System.Collections.Generic.List<int>(); // instance ids, worst first
    public System.Collections.Generic.List<int> Surpluses = new System.Collections.Generic.List<int>(); }
}
public sealed class CampaignSnapshot {
  public int Alliance { get; } public int FactionIndex { get; } public int DaySerial { get; }
  public PlayerCommandClass Player { get; } public int PlayerCommandInstanceId { get; } /* -1 none; G2's no-vertical-swap check */
  public bool UseFog { get; } public DirectorPosture Tempo { get; } /* MassRatioModifier → G3 required ratio, MinimumHoldRatioModifier → G4 hold ratio */
  public float CapitalX, CapitalZ; public bool HasCapital; public float EnemyCapitalX, EnemyCapitalZ; public bool HasEnemyCapital;
  public float RelocationRange; /* GamePrefs.aimaximumdistancetosearchforunitrelocations */ public float TheaterHalfExtentX, TheaterHalfExtentZ; /* GamePrefs.theatersize is a Vector2 (49166): x for x, y for z (17105) */
  public float OffensiveDominance; /* GamePrefs.aidominanceforoffensiveoperations */ public float MinStrengthForMovement, MinMoraleForMovement; /* aiminimumstrength/moraleformovement */
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<FormationRecord> Own { get; } public System.Collections.Generic.List<AreaRecord> Areas { get; }
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<ObjectiveRecord> Objectives { get; } public System.Collections.Generic.List<SupplySourceRecord> Supply { get; }
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<RailLineRecord> Rails { get; } public System.Collections.Generic.List<CommanderRecord> Commanders { get; }
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<RankBand> RankBands { get; }       // 10..18, for CheckSuitableRank-style checks in pure code
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<EnemyDetection> Detections { get; } // filled by G1's EnemyDetectionRuntime; empty until then
  public System.Collections.Generic.List<EnemyBelief> Beliefs { get; }       // filled by G1's EnemyArmyModel
  public int IndexOfOwn(int instanceId); public int IndexOfKey(string unitKey); // IndexOfKey: sidecar rebinding (G6)
}
public static class EnemyBeliefQuery { // pure; G1 migrations, G3 balance, G1b, G5b, G8
  public static float StrengthWithin(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> b, float x, float z, float radius); // confidence-weighted
  public static int Nearest(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> b, float x, float z, out float distance);
}
public static class ViewerCoverage { // pure: an enemy counts as seen on the shared fows[1] only near an own or allied viewer (spec C14)
  public static bool Covers(float enemyX, float enemyZ, float[] viewerX, float[] viewerZ, float[] viewerRadius, int count);
}
public sealed class SectorModel { public static SectorModel Build(CampaignSnapshot s); // P11 flood fill over GetClosestAreaDistance adjacency, deterministic
  public int SectorOfArea(int areaIndex); public int SectorAt(float x, float z); public int Count { get; }
  public WhiskeyRealism.Strategic.Theater TheaterOf(int sector); public void Centroid(int sector, out float x, out float z); }
public struct ReachResult { public bool InReach; public float Slack01; public int NearestSourceId; public float NearestSourceDistance; }
public static partial class LogisticsGate { // G3 adds AddDepotTasks in its own partial file
  public static ReachResult Evaluate(CampaignSnapshot s, float targetX, float targetZ, float formationCommandRange, float supplySlack);
  public static bool ClampToReach(CampaignSnapshot s, float fromX, float fromZ, ref float toX, ref float toZ, float formationCommandRange, float supplySlack); // pulls a hold point back along the axis to the farthest in-reach point; false when none
}
public static class PlayerCommandClassifier {
  public static PlayerCommandClass Classify(bool scenarioActive, int chosenCommander, bool isCommanderInChief, int hierarchy, bool hasCommand, bool commandIsMapGroup);
  public static bool PlannerStandsDown(PlayerCommandClass c, int playerAlliance, int alliance); // CIC, this alliance only
  public static bool ExecutorsPaused(PlayerCommandClass c);                                   // NoCommand (vanilla 11368): every alliance, as vanilla's job machine
}

  public static bool TryGetAllocation(int alliance, out Allocation allocation);

```
`DirectorPosture`, `Theater`, `BattleHistoryRecord` are existing pure types; `WlChainClass` is stage F's (`Strategic/WlStrategicOrderBridge.cs`, compiled into the test csproj by F). `PlayerCommandClassifier.Classify`: not a career → `NotCareer`; CIC → `CommanderInChief`; hierarchy −1 → `NoCommand` (exactly vanilla's 11368 predicate, since hierarchy −1 already excludes the CIC and army-group commanders, 44397-44427); no `currentcommand` with hierarchy 18 → `ArmyGroupCommander` (planner runs; every unit of his army group is `PlayerCommand` per F); otherwise `MapGroup` or `BelowMapLevel` by the map-group test (8271).

**W&L pause change (spec C5; G0 makes it, on this branch, because it is the planner's gate):**
- `WlCareerStartGate` gains `CareerNotStarted(bool dlcScenarioActive, int chosenCommanderId)` = `active && chosen < 0` and `VanillaJobMachinePaused(bool dlcScenarioActive, int hierarchy, bool isCommanderInChief)` = `active && hierarchy == -1 && !isCommanderInChief` (11368). `ShouldDeferStrategicReview` (no command) keeps its meaning and its callers through `WlCareerStartPending()`: `WlCareerStartSelectionRetryPatch:63` (the start-selection popup retry needs "no command"), `ArmyGroupManagementPatch:49` (#16) and `ArmyAreaTheaterPatch:36` (#15, deleted in G6). Its `WlCampaignTests.cs:116-119` assertions stay as they are; the new predicates are tested in `CampaignSnapshotTests` (stage F rewrites `WlCampaignTests.cs`, so G0 does not touch it).
- `StrategicCoordinator.RunStrategicReviewCore`: the early return (`:343` on `93f6a82`) uses `CareerNotStarted` instead of `WlCareerStartPending()`, with the same once-per-pending log. Inside the alliance loop, for **both** alliances while `VanillaJobMachinePaused` holds: run era, capital threat, director posture, fiscal, policy and construction, and `ReviewPlan` in bookkeeping mode; skip the CIC/probe path, front, formation directives, army areas, defense intent and `ReviewExecute` (`ReviewPlan`/`ReviewExecute` also check `ExecutorsPaused` themselves; the railroad Prefix and every planner patch read the same predicate). Log once per pause: `[Coordinator] W&L player has no command (hierarchy -1): planner executors and order-issuing ledgers paused for every alliance, as vanilla's job machine`.
- `MonthlyTickHookPatch:51`: `runtimeReady` uses `CareerNotStarted` instead of `WlCareerStartPending()` (otherwise the review would never be notified while the player is between commands).

**Flags (all `true`, section `[Campaign Planner]`; bound in the slice that reads them):**
- `Enable Campaign Planner` — "Default ON. Runs the live theater campaign planner in the daily strategic review for each AI alliance (snapshot, fog-honest enemy beliefs, commander profiles, theater plan, allocation) and logs it. OFF = no planner; vanilla campaign AI and the kept strategic ledgers only."
- `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` — "Default ON. Lets the planner steer vanilla: offensive target pointers, campaign objective, theater boxes, availability, capital pledges, transfers, MoveUnitTo, depots, forts, railroads. Never moves or orders the W&L player's own command. OFF = plan and telemetry only."
- `Enable Campaign Fog-Honest Beliefs` — "Default ON. Enemy armies are known only when vanilla's campaign fog shows them to the side (FogOfWar.UnitIsHidden, 127225 arguments; fows[1] for the non-player AI, iswithinfog for the W&L player's AI side) or by contact; unseen armies are remembered and decay. OFF = vanilla's omniscient enemy lists."
- `Enable Commander Appointment Planner` — "Default ON. Each AI alliance puts its best available commanders (vanilla competence: GetAverageAttributeValue and the live attribute getters) on its main-effort commands, at most one appointment a day; the historical succession events add a bonus when their conditions hold; appointees are shielded from vanilla's job-24 swap for 60 days. OFF = vanilla job 24 only and no succession swaps."
- `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance` — "Default ON. Each AI alliance places viewers where its picture of the enemy is oldest or an approach is unwatched: cavalry screens (with the vanilla scouting order, never on a group carrying the W&L player), town pickets, one advance guard and belief-driven probes ahead of the main effort, recalled on contact. Vanilla never scouts on the campaign map. Never the W&L player's own command. OFF = no recon missions; beliefs only decay." (bound in G1b)
- `Enable Supply Depot Steering` — "Default ON. When the planned advance would outrun supply, a small AI formation marches to a depot site on the axis, places a vanilla supply-depot construction wish there and stays until it is built (or 20 days pass). Vanilla's own depot logic is unchanged. OFF = vanilla depots only."
- `Enable Fort Steering` — "Default ON. Vanilla fort construction prefers scenario fort sites on planned axes and threatened sectors when one is eligible; vanilla's one-fort-at-a-time rule, builders and placement are unchanged. OFF = vanilla site choice (the fort governor's saturation caps still apply)."
- `Enable Railroad Steering` — "Default ON. When vanilla's yearly railroad roll succeeds for an AI alliance on a line vanilla would start, the line that starts is the startable one that best serves the planned axes (same probability and number of starts as vanilla). OFF = vanilla's random line." (G5c; user-approved Prefix-skip, P14)
- `Enable W&L Career Transfer Fix` — "Default ON. Fixes vanilla's W&L-career transfers, which never happen because MoveUnit refuses non-player units in a career (93099): inside CheckTransferOfUnits only, and never for a player-owned unit. OFF = vanilla (no AI transfers in careers)." (G5b, P15)
G0 binds only `Enable Campaign Planner` (read by `ReviewPlan`) and `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` (read by `ReviewExecute` as its gate); `Enable Campaign Fog-Honest Beliefs` is bound in G1, `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance` in G1b, `Enable Commander Appointment Planner` in G2, the steering flags in G5b/G5c, `Enable Garrison Manning` in G8, each in the slice whose code reads it.

**Steps:**
- [x] Tests first (`CampaignSnapshotTests`): `CampaignSightIgnoresPerkEightAndSixteen` (campaign viewers are top units, unittyp 14-16/18, so no perk changes campaign sight; weather and lie-down terms as 101296-101309), `SightRadiusFlagOffKeepsPerkEightFactor` (rollback: with `Fix Perk Id Mismatches` off a `HasPerk(8)` viewer gets the factor, as unfixed vanilla), `ClassifierNotCareerWhenScenarioInactive`, `ClassifierCicBeatsEverything`, `ClassifierNoCommandOnHierarchyMinusOne`, `ClassifierArmyGroupCommanderWithoutCommandIsNotPaused` (hierarchy 18, no `currentcommand`), `ClassifierMapGroupWhenCommandIsItsOwnCampaignGroup` (a top-unit division, hierarchy 14), `ClassifierBelowMapLevelForBrigadeInCorps`, `StandsDownOnlyForCicPlayersOwnAlliance`, `AllAlliancesExecutorsPauseWithVanillaJobMachine` (with the predicate true `ExecutorsPaused` holds for both alliances and the bookkeeping steps still run; with it false neither pauses), `CareerNotStartedOnlyWithoutChosenCommander`, `VanillaJobMachinePausedMatches11368` (CIC or army-group player with no command → not paused), `IndexOfOwnMinusOneForUnknown`, `IndexOfKeyFindsByUnitKey`, `SnapshotListsResetOnReuse`, `AllocationTryGetFindsAssignment`, `PlanStorePublishReplacesAndResetClears` (CampaignScope reset), `ViewerCoverageUsesEachViewersOwnRadius`, `CoversFalseWithoutViewerInRadius`, `StrengthWithinIsConfidenceWeighted`, `NearestIgnoresExpired`, `SectorsDeterministicAcrossRebuilds`, `SectorTheaterFromCentroid`, `SectorAdjacencyUsesZoneEdges` (14835-14860), `ReachTrueWithOwnIipInCommandRange`, `EnemyOwnedIipDoesNotSupply`, `ClampToReachPullsBackToLastInReachPoint`, `ClampToReachFalseWhenNothingInReach`, `AreaRecordCarriesNoEnemyAggregate` (source pin, word-bounded so the legitimate `ownpoints` read passes: `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` has no match for `\bpoints\b`, `\bdistancepoints\b`, `\benemyavgcampaignunits\b` or the prefix `\benemycampaignunitsstrength\w*`).

Vanilla's `GetGroupSupplyState` (14063-14066) halves both terms, so its result never exceeds 0.5: vanilla's 0.25 thresholds (depot trigger, fort builders, raid abort 9092) mean 50 % real supply and its 0.75 `minimumsupplyoffensivetargets` gates (raid search 9135, the dead micro-move gate 14019) can never pass. Every Whiskey threshold is stated on the raw metric; test `SupplyMetricIsRawNotVanillaHalved` (raw 0.45 ↔ vanilla 0.225).

Until G1-G4 merge, `ReviewPlan` publishes the sectors with an empty plan and allocation, which no patch reads before G5. Scopes `campaign.planner.review` / `campaign.planner.execute` (4.0). "campaign-planner:first-review:0" : "campaign-planner:first-review:1", "[CampaignPlanner] first review alliance=<a> formations=<n> areas=<n> sectors=<n> objectives=<n> supply=<n> rails=<n> commanders=<n> player=<class>")`.

### G0 as built (2026-10-15)

Commits on `feat/campaign-planner` (the landing commit after `d9cbd56` is described under "G0 landing" below): `c66db8b` (merge of `main` `4ba3805`: `fix/campaign-perk-ids`, patches #71-#79, flag `Fix Perk Id Mismatches`) and the G0 commit `feat(campaign-planner): contracts, snapshot, primitives, flags, review hook and W&L pause [G0]`. Harness 2141 PASS (2104 on `main` + 37 in `CampaignSnapshotTests`), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (110 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new patch, no new first-fire id.

Files: new `Strategic/Planner/{CampaignPlannerContracts, CampaignSnapshot, SightRadiusFormula, EnemyBeliefQuery, ViewerCoverage, SectorModel, LogisticsGate, PlayerCommandClassifier, CampaignPlanStore}.cs` (pure, in the test csproj), `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs`, `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (runtime); edited `WlCareerStartGate.cs`, `StrategicCoordinator.cs`, `Patches/MonthlyTickHookPatch.cs` (`:51` only), `Plugin.cs` (two flags, `[Campaign Planner]`), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs`; test `CampaignSnapshotTests.cs`.

Where the build differs from the text above (each checked against the decompile):
- **`TheaterHalfExtentX/Z`** instead of one `TheaterHalfExtent`: `GamePrefs.theatersize` is a `Vector2` (49166) and `IsWithinOperationsTheater` tests `|dx| < theatersize.x` and `|dz| < theatersize.y` (17105). The contract block and census row above are updated; G5a's box writer uses both.
- **`PlannerReviewMode { Full, Bookkeeping, StandDown }`** and `PlayerCommandClassifier.ReviewMode(class, playerAlliance, alliance)` are added: the one decision both `ReviewPlan` and `ReviewExecute` read (stand-down for the CIC player's own alliance, bookkeeping for every alliance while `ExecutorsPaused`).
- **`CampaignSnapshotRuntime.Build(alliance, factionIndex, daySerial, player, in WlChainContext, tempo, reuse)`**: the runtime passes what it already computed for its gates instead of re-reading it.
- **Pure helpers inside the owned files:** `SnapshotRules` (in `CampaignSnapshot.cs`: `UnitKey`, `RawSupply01`, `IsEmbarked`, `IsFightingForce`, `CavalryShare`, `ObjectiveRoles`, `Clamp01`) and `AreaGeometry` (in `SectorModel.cs`: vanilla's `GetClosestAreaDistance` point and area overloads, 14835-14860 and 14863-14900, including the area overload's `area2.zoneedges[i]`-to-`area1` centre cross term and its lack of a centre-to-centre term; `AreaAt` = the closest area by the point overload). The adjacency is the area overload (the plan's `14835` is the point overload); `SectorAt` uses the point overload.
- **`FightingForce`** mirrors `UnitIsFightingForce` (16867-16890) without its `UpdateGroupStrength(overridecalc: true, ...)` call for armies (16882): the snapshot writes nothing vanilla.
- **Rules the plan left open, fixed here:** `ObjectiveRecord.Roles` — Capital = `Town.IsCapital`; Port = IIP `IIPType == IIP.id_harbor` (1, 103717); RailHub = an IIP listed by two or more lines' `ConstructionSymbol.IIPsForTransportCapacityUpdate` (188134), or a town with IIPs of two or more lines within `radiustowncentertocapture`; Industry = a factory/foundry/ironworks/manufactory (`CBuilding` 5/8/10/12, 95107-95113) whose `closesttown` is the town. `Importance01` = vanilla's own per-target term for the snapshot's faction (`SnapshotRules.TargetImportance`, `UpdateImportanceValues` 14905-14995; corrected at the landing, F1): town population × `aiimportancepopulation` or IIP transport capacity × `aiimportancetransportcapacityweight`, × `num` (1 when the holder is at war with the faction, else `nonwarparticipantsobjectimportanceweighting` 0.35, campaignprefs 1400-1401; so own targets count 0.35), + `aitownweightfactorobjective` (unscaled) when the target is part of the faction's `followedcampaignobjective` (`IsPartOfObjective`), + `aiimportancevaluescapital` (unscaled) only for the capital of the alliance whose id equals the faction index (14970-14976 adds at index `l` of a faction-indexed array: a vanilla index quirk, mirrored), over the snapshot's largest. `GarrisonStrength` = own and allied fort garrisons only (`CBuilding.garrisonunit`, 81985-82093; towns have no garrison link; F2) summed onto the fort's `closesttown` and its `parentiip` (else `closestiip`). `EnemyBeliefQuery`: a belief with `Confidence01 <= 0` is expired. Supply sources: every IIP (owner kept; `LogisticsGate` accepts own and not invisible, 114277-114281) and only own or allied supply depots (114296-114306), so the gate's depot rule is built and `Condition >= 0.75`. `LogisticsGate.Evaluate`: range = `commandRange × (1 + supplySlack)`; `Slack01 = 1 − d / range`; a target whose closest area has `OwnPoints > SupplyCapacity` is not in reach (10983, 10941-10962). `ClampToReach` samples the axis every quarter range (≤ 256 samples). Sectors: seeds in area-list order, multi-source Dijkstra on centre distance over adjacent areas, ties to the lower sector, unreached components become their own sectors.
- **`CampaignPlannerRuntime.OnBattleRecorded` and the `RecordBattleOutcome` forward are not in G0.** In G0 it would have no reader (P4's decisive-battle trigger is G3's, and `TheaterPlanner.Plan` already takes the battle list); G3 adds the forward with its reader (one line after `BattleHistory.Add`, `StrategicCoordinator.cs:1475` after G0), and G6 routes the deleted `DirtyPlanIfBattleInvalidates` through it.
- **`CampaignPlanner` is registered with `AddCampaignAndMainLog`** (category State), so the first-review smoke line reaches `LogOutput.log` under every logging profile (two lines per campaign, the #69 precedent); the other eleven events are `AddCampaign` as listed.
- **No sidecar change in G0.** Sidecar v3 is G6's; G0 only fixes the identity contract (`UnitKey`, `IndexOfKey`).
- **`Enable Campaign Planner Executors` gates an empty executor list until G2/G5** (P10). It is bound and read now because the plan fixes the review hook in G0; the branch is not merged to `main` before the executors exist.
- **Coordinator pause wiring:** `jobMachinePaused` (`WlVanillaJobMachinePaused()`, direct `DLC_WL` reads) is evaluated once per review. While it holds, for both alliances: era, capital threat, director posture, fiscal, policy, `ReviewPlan` (bookkeeping) and construction run; succession checks/swaps, `ReviewPlanWithTruth`/`Replan`, front, defense intent, `ReviewExecute`, formation directives, the probe and army areas are skipped. The CIC object is still built (`PublishDirectorPosture` and the heartbeat read it). The pause line logs once per pause episode. The early return uses `WlCareerNotStarted()`; `WlCareerStartPending()` keeps its three callers. `[DailyOps:Perf]` gains `plannerMs`.
- **Telegraph starts during the pause (decided by the integrator at the landing):** a telegraph start is an executor write (`AddConstructionWish`), so `UpdateConstructionIntent` skips `TelegraphConstructionRuntime.TryStartTelegraph` while `jobMachinePaused`; the construction ledger itself keeps running (see G0 landing).
- **P6 `DriftPerDay` corrected in this landing (coordinator request):** the road-march formula was too low; P6 now states vanilla's maximum campaign speed with every term anchored, and G1's test is `DriftIsCampaignSpeedUpperBound`. G0 does not implement `DriftPerDay`.

TEST-ONLY members added by G0 (orphan-sweep `--include-test-only` 29 → 72), each read by the later slice the census names: `CampaignPlanStore.TryGetPlan/TryGetAllocation/TryGetSectors` (G5a patches, `CampaignWithholdView`); `DetectionSource.NoFog/ObjectiveGarrison`, `EnemyTraits.CavalryMajority/OnOwnSoil/NearOwnCoast` (G1); `EnemyBelief.LastSeenDay/UncertaintyRadius/LastSource` (G1b, G6); `FormationRole.CapitalGuard/DepotBuilder` (G4, G5a/b/c); `ReconKind.Picket/AdvanceGuard`, `ReconTask` and `ReconTask.HoldZ` (G1b, G4); `TheaterPlan.MainSectorId/MainObjectiveId/MainRequiredRatio/CapitalFloorStrength/DepotTasks/SwitchReason/PriorId` (G3, G5a/b/c); `CommanderProfile` and its three fields (G2, G3); `Assignment.ReconMissionId/Withheld`, `Allocation.Shortfalls/Surpluses` (G4, G5a/b/d); `CampaignSnapshot.IndexOfKey` (G6); `EnemyBeliefQuery`/`StrengthWithin` (G1); `LogisticsGate`/`ClampToReach` (G1b, G3); `SectorModel.SectorOfArea/SectorAt/TheaterOf/Centroid` (G3, G4, G5a); `ViewerCoverage`/`Covers` (G1, G1b).

Mutation plants (scratch copy, one at a time, each turns the named test red): sight radius ignores the flag → `SightRadiusFlagOffKeepsPerkEightFactor`; hierarchy −1 not `NoCommand` → `ClassifierNoCommandOnHierarchyMinusOne`; `ExecutorsPaused` always false → `ClassifierNoCommandOnHierarchyMinusOne`; `Covers` always true → `ViewerCoverageUsesEachViewersOwnRadius`; unweighted `StrengthWithin` → `StrengthWithinIsConfidenceWeighted`; store reset leaves the plan → `PlanStorePublishReplacesAndResetClears`; pause ignores CIC → `VanillaJobMachinePausedMatches11368`; `ClampToReach` true with nothing in reach → `ClampToReachFalseWhenNothingInReach`; halved supply → `SupplyMetricIsRawNotVanillaHalved`; adjacency without edge pairs → `SectorAdjacencyUsesZoneEdges`; `CareerNotStarted` ignores the chosen commander → `CareerNotStartedOnlyWithoutChosenCommander`; `Nearest` keeps expired beliefs → `NearestIgnoresExpired`.

#### G0 landing (verify-g0 pass on `d9cbd56`, items F1-F6 and the queued terrain amendments)

- **F3, pause wiring pinned.** `CampaignSnapshotTests.CoordinatorPauseGuardsOrderIssuingSteps` (BattleWriteGateTests-style source pin) fails when `Succession.CheckEvents`, `SwapOfficer`, `ReviewPlanWithTruth`, `cic.Replan`, `UpdateFrontLedger`, `UpdateDefenseIntent`, `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewExecute`, `UpdateFormationDirectiveLedger`, `UpdateOperationalProbe` or `UpdateArmyAreaLedger` is reachable in `RunStrategicReviewCore` outside a `!jobMachinePaused` guard, or when `ReviewPlan`, `UpdateFiscalIntent` or `UpdateConstructionIntent` is put behind it; `CoordinatorPauseGatesAreTheVanillaPredicates` pins the `WlCareerNotStarted()` early return, the 11368 predicate with its CIC term and the `MonthlyTickHookPatch` gate; `PlannerReviewGatesPinned` pins `ReviewPlan`'s flag, stand-down and bookkeeping-before-publish and `ReviewExecute`'s flags and Full-only gate. The verifier's 8 surviving runtime plants plus 6 more (telegraph, succession, capital index, enemy garrison, cache key, non-war weight) each turn the named test red (scratch copy, `evidence/mutation-results-landing.txt`).
- **Telegraph wishes pause (integrator decision on open point 8).** `UpdateConstructionIntent(…, jobMachinePaused)`: the ledger runs, `TelegraphConstructionRuntime.TryStartTelegraph` (its `AddConstructionWish`, `TelegraphConstructionRuntime.cs:68`) is skipped while paused; pinned by the same test.
- **F1 `Importance01`** mirrors vanilla exactly (rule above); `ImportanceMirrorsVanillaTerms` on fixture prefs.
- **F2 garrisons** own and allied only (`SnapshotRules.SideKnowsGarrison`); `FortGarrisonReadOnlyForOwnOrAlliedForts` pins every `garrisonunit` read behind the check; spec § Campaign fog records the decision; P6 and G1's detection rule updated.
- **F4 performance.** `SectorModel`'s adjacency matrix is cached per campaign (CampaignScope `campaign-planner-sector-adjacency`), keyed by a 64-bit FNV-1a over the area count, relocation range and every area's index, centre and edges (`SectorAdjacencyCacheFollowsGeometry`). `ReadCommanders` reads the live getters once per alliance and review day (CampaignScope `campaign-planner-snapshot-cache`). net8 micro-benchmark (Release, scratch project over the pure files; 300 own formations, 60 areas with 4 edges, 120 objective targets, 450 supply sources; 200 runs): `SectorModel.Build` cold mean 0.98 ms / median 0.90 ms, cached mean 0.29 ms / median 0.15 ms; `AreaAt` for 300 formations median 0.28 ms; `LogisticsGate.Evaluate` for 300 formations median 0.47 ms. Against P2 (snapshot ≤ 2 ms, review ≤ 4 ms per alliance): the pure parts fit with room; Mono's JIT in game is slower than net8, and the vanilla-reading parts (commander getters with `UnitIsInsideRadius`, `GetTransportCapacity`, `StartConstruction(checkonly)`) cannot run outside the game, so G7 smoke item 4 remains the budget proof.
- **Static map facts per campaign (perf review P1, 2026-10-15).** The G0 snapshot re-read static map facts on every review: each town target walked every IIP of every railroad line (`RailLinesNear`, one `transform.position` per IIP), each line's endpoints were an O(n²) position search, and the area edges, crossings and town/IIP/depot positions were copied again. They are now read once per campaign and shared by both alliances (CampaignScope `campaign-planner-snapshot-cache`): `RailGeometry` (per-line IIP ids and positions, endpoints, lines per IIP, and lines within `radiustowncentertocapture` of a town memoized per town and radius), area shapes (index, centre, edge arrays; only `importancevalues`/`ownpoints`/`supplycapacity` are read daily), crossings, and a position cache for towns, IIPs, depots and capitals. List-backed caches rebuild when their vanilla list is replaced or changes size (`StaticSourceKey` on `BattleUnits.railroad` 82506-82516, `AICampaign.aiarea` 11272, `rivercrossingpositions` 26628-26638); no cached fact holds ownership, construction or importance, so an ownership change invalidates nothing. Daily as before: formations, ownership, at-war, importance, available/accomplished, garrisons, industry towns, rail `Startable`/progress, supply owner/condition/capacity, commanders. New scope `campaign.planner.snapshot` (2.0) around `Build`. Tests: `StaticSourceKeyRebuildsOnNewListOrCount`, `RailGeometryMatchesDirectScan` (5 seeded fixtures, cached vs the G0 scans kept in the test as the uncached reference: lines near 60 towns twice, lines per IIP, endpoints per line), `RailGeometryMemoFollowsRadiusAndRebuild`, `SnapshotStaticFactsReadOncePerCampaign` (source pin: every cache cleared on reset, keys checked, no per-review rail walk, the only `transform.position` reads are the position cache's miss and a moving unit's climate cell). 11 plants, 10 red, 1 equivalent (`Clear` keeping the town memo: the NaN radius it sets clears the memo on the next query); verify r1's surviving plants are killed below (r2 plants: V5, V6, V8, V9, V11, V12 rail progress, V13 IIP condition red in the harness; V7 a moving unit through the cache does not compile, CS1503; V10 equivalent). Benchmark (net8 Release, scratch; 80 town + 40 IIP targets, 60 lines listing 1,041 IIP slots from 450 IIPs, 60 areas, 2,000 crossings; 200 runs): native `transform.position` reads per alliance review G0 95,257, P1 530 on the first review of a campaign and 0 after (at the review's 0.1-1 µs per native call: ≈ 9.5-95 ms → ≈ 0.05-0.5 ms once per load → 0); pure part G0 mean 0.25 ms / median 0.25 ms, P1 cache build mean 0.29 ms / median 0.26 ms, P1 later review mean 0.023 ms / median 0.020 ms. Still read daily and still costly per the review (≈ 5-6 ms per alliance, not measurable outside the game): commander live getters for every commander (G2 owns `ReadCommanders`), the army descendant scan before the strength test, `GetTransportCapacity` for the unread `SupplySourceRecord.TransportCapacity`, and per-formation reads; the in-game `maxMs` gate below is the proof. The position cache is reachable only through `PositionOf(Town)`, `PositionOf(IIP)` and `PositionOf(CBuilding)`, so a moving unit cannot be cached (it does not compile).
