## G1 — EnemyArmyModel, detection, ledgers on beliefs

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/EnemyArmyModel.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyDetectionRuntime.cs` (runtime); modify `Strategic/FrontSectorRuntime.cs` (`:37` enemy read), `Strategic/DefenseIntentRuntime.cs` (`:140`, `:456`; stage F edits other lines of this file), `Patches/FortConstructionGovernorPatch.cs` (`:301-304` enemy read only), `Strategic/CoastalDefenseCustomOrderRunner.cs` (`:102`; stage F edits other lines), `StrategicCoordinator.UpdateCapitalThreat` (`:672` enemy loop only), `Strategic/FormationDirectiveRuntime.cs` (`PopulateLocalPressure` / `BuildAreaIndex`), `Strategic/Construction/TelegraphConstructionRuntime.cs` (`EnemyNearby`, `EnemyNearPosition`; the `:151` own-unit candidate scan keeps its `completeunitlist` fallback with an `// own side` annotation, since `UnitEligible` filters `unit.alliance != alliance` at `:316`), `Strategic/DefenseIntentRuntime.cs` sea-invasion and raid threat inputs (with `DefenseIntentTypes.cs` / `DefenseThreatSignature.cs` as needed), `Patches/RecruitmentPatch.cs` (`SafeStrengthRatio` only), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (insert step 1); test `EnemyArmyModelTests.cs`. (Source line numbers below are on `main` `93f6a82` (stages E and F merged; this branch merged it at the crit round 2 landing).)

**Vanilla to read:** `CreateFOWObjects` 116505-116517; `AssignUnitToFOW` 116520-116534; `UpdateViewRange` 101274-101365 (campaign radius 101296-101307); `DrawVisibility` 101057-101132 (campaign concealment 101116-101128); `UnitIsHidden` 101179-101229; `CheckRegimentVisibility` 127205-127239 (argument order 127225); `InitFOW` 100755-100795 (`usefow`); `PositionIsWithinFog` 101246-101263; `SeparateUnits` 10458-10515; `CheckEnemyContactCampaign` 115620+ (bugle-range contact); the campaign speed bound for `DriftPerDay` (P6, every term anchored there: `RegimentMove` 133340-133545, 134013, 134050-134100; `GetTerrainSpeedAdjustment` 132888-132900; `groupspeed` 120179/120438-120440/133991; campaign time in floating years 30171); `IsAtWarWith`; `EuropeanUnits` 15761-15800; for the non-`enemyunits` readers: `GetStrengthRatio` 12366-12404, `SeaInvasionForce` 9188+ (state machine; Whiskey must not read it), `RaidForce` 8869-9186, `Regiment.GetClosestEnemyUnitReg`; the embarked state of a seen enemy group (the G0 `Embarked` predicate: `embarkmentstarttime > 0` while embarking, 116348-116360, or `currentmodeltype` 1/2 afloat, 116383-116417, vanilla's own afloat test at 80724).

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public sealed class EnemyArmyModel {
  public const int ExpireDays = 30; public const float HalfLifeDays = 7f; public const float MinConfidence = 0.05f;
  public void Update(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyDetection> seen, int daySerial,
                     System.Func<int, float> driftPerDay /* by unittyp, P6 uncertainty */,
                     System.Func<int, float> marchPerDay /* by unittyp, identity plausibility (G1 as built) */); // review only
  public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> Beliefs { get; }
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<EnemyBelief> beliefs); // sidecar (G6): keyed by UnitKey, InstanceId rebound on the next detection
}
internal static class EnemyDetectionRuntime {
  internal static void Detect(CampaignSnapshot s, int playerAlliance, bool fogHonest);  // fills s.Detections
  internal static EnemyArmyModel ModelFor(int alliance);                               // CampaignScope store
  internal static System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> BeliefsFor(int alliance); // for migrated ledgers
}
```

**Identity (spec component 2):** a belief is keyed by `UnitKey` (`name:commander`, the sidecar convention), never by `InstanceId`, which is in-session only. `Update` matches a detection to a belief by `UnitKey`; when no belief has the key and a remembered belief has the same unit name (the part before `:`) and `UnitTyp`, that belief is re-keyed (its commander changed, e.g. after an appointment) instead of a second belief being created. So a save/load, which changes every instance id, and a commander change never count one army twice. **Collisions:** `name:commander` is assumed unique per live group but not trusted: when two live detections in one review carry the same `UnitKey`, neither is merged into the other; each keeps its own belief keyed `UnitKey#InstanceId` for the session (persisted under that composite key, rebound by the same rule after a load) and `OnceLog.Warning("campaign-planner:unitkey-collision", …)` logs it once. **Renames:** vanilla renames groups (`UpdateUnitNameDefault` sets the GameObject name after a combination, 17159), so a renamed army arrives under a new key with a new name and the re-key rule cannot match it; instead, a remembered belief that was not matched this review and has the same `UnitTyp` is retired (not re-keyed, not merged) when a new-key detection lies inside its uncertainty radius, and the detection becomes a fresh belief. A rename therefore leaves no ghost, and two armies that are both seen are never merged. **Plausibility radius (integrator decision 2026-10-15, verify-g1 F2):** re-key and rename-retire both require the detection within the belief's *plausibility* radius = a normal road march for its unit type (`CampaignSpeedBound.RoadMarchPerDay`: `unitbasicspeed[t,1] × unitbasicspeed[15,1] × basicunitspeed / terrainspeccost[7] × generalcampaignunitspeedfactor / 365.25`, 133350-133354, 120179/133991, 133535, 132888-132900, 134083) × days unseen, clamped to [1, `PlausibleDaysCap` = 7]; rename-retire also requires the same `UnitTyp`. The P6 radius stays the planning uncertainty only.

**Detection rule (exact; spec component 2):** for each enemy campaign group `e` (alliance at war with the side, active, visibility state ≠ 0, not a fleet), visible when:
- `!GameVars.usefow` or `!fogHonest` → `NoFog` (omniscient, telemetry `mode=NoFog`/`ConfigOff`);
- else side ≠ `playeralliance`: `!ownUnit.fow.UnitIsHidden(e.GetPosition(), GameVars.commander[e.commander].GetCommanderCunning(), (float)e.strength / GamePrefs.unittypesize[e.unittyp], e.liedown, e.coverobject, e.covervalue, e.unittyp, e)` (bound-check `commander` and `unittyp`, pass −1 when out of range, as the battle core does) **and** some own or allied viewer (visibility state ≠ 0, active, not routed) lies within its campaign radius (`SightRadiusFormula`, the same radius that paints the grid) of `e` → `FogGrid`;
- else side == `playeralliance` (W&L): `!e.iswithinfog` → `OwnSideWithinFog`;
- always: `e.inbattle` against the side, or `e` within an own formation's `buglerange` → `Contact`;
- an enemy-held objective town → one `ObjectiveGarrison` detection at the town only when its fort garrison (`CBuilding.garrisonunit`, 81985-82093) passes the same rule (seen on the grid or in contact); strength = that garrison's `groupstrengthdirect`. The town's ownership is public, its garrison strength is not (G0 landing F2; spec § Campaign fog).
- **W&L intelligence incidents are the player's, never the AI side's:** while incident 60, 61 or 64 is active `UnitIsHidden` returns false for every unit (101183-101185), which would hand the enemy AI side the player's intelligence. Rule (b) masks those incidents for its own probe exactly as the battle side does on `fix/campaign-terrain-bugs` (`BattlePerceptionProbe.MaskPlayerIntelIncidents`: a `FieldValueSwap` of `DLC_WL.Incident.launchedtime` to not-launched for the probe call, restored in a `finally`; `BattlePerception.PlayerIntelIncidents`/`MasksPlayerIntelIncidents` are reused once that branch merges, else ported verbatim). Rule (c) (the W&L player's own AI side, `iswithinfog`) keeps the incidents: they are that side's intelligence.
Strength = `groupstrengthdirect`, position = `GetPosition()` (never `GetLastTransmittedPathPos`). Traits: `Embarked` by the G0 predicate; `CavalryMajority` from `groupstrengthperunittypcampaigndirect[1]` over the sum; `OnOwnSoil` when `BattlefieldSetup.GetStateOfField(position)` is a state of the side; `NearOwnCoast` when an own port (IIP harbour) lies within the side's relocation range.

### G1 as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g1` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g1`), cut from `feat/campaign-planner` `dbc3a6f`. Harness 2349 PASS after verify-g1 r3 (2299 − 2 deleted `CampaignMilitaryFixTests` sea-invasion tests + 51 in `EnemyArmyModelTests` + 1 calendar test in `CampaignLifecycleFixTests`; 2333 at the first build, 2343 after r1, 2348 after r2), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (131 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 72 → 60 (G1 adds no test-only member and gives a src reader to 12 G0 rows: `DetectionSource.NoFog/ObjectiveGarrison`, `EnemyTraits.CavalryMajority/OnOwnSoil/NearOwnCoast`, `EnemyBelief.LastSeenDay/UncertaintyRadius/LastSource`, `EnemyBeliefQuery`/`StrengthWithin`, `ViewerCoverage`/`Covers`), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new patch and no new first-fire id.

Files: new `Strategic/Planner/EnemyArmyModel.cs` (pure: `EnemyArmyModel`, `EnemyBeliefStore`, `CampaignSpeedPrefs`/`CampaignSpeedBound`, `DetectionRules`), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyBeliefReadings.cs` (pure: the migrated ledgers' halves; a second pure file instead of one large one), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyDetectionRuntime.cs` (runtime); edited `CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`EnemyBelief.LastEmbarkedDay`), `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (step 1), `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (`SightRadiusOf` extracted so the snapshot and the viewer check share one radius; `FixPerkIds`/`ResolveWeather` internal), `FrontSectorRuntime.cs`, `DefenseIntentRuntime.cs`, `DefenseIntentInput.cs`, `DefenseThreatSignature.cs`, `DefenseIntentLedger.cs`, `CampaignMilitaryGates.cs` (sea-invasion strength helpers deleted with their only reader), `CoastalDefenseCustomOrderRunner.cs`, `FormationDirectiveRuntime.cs`, `Construction/TelegraphConstructionRuntime.cs`, `StrategicCoordinator.cs` (`UpdateCapitalThreat` and the one `CollectSifSignatures` line), `Patches/FortConstructionGovernorPatch.cs`, `Patches/RecruitmentPatch.cs` (`SafeStrengthRatio` only), `Plugin.cs` (one flag); tests `EnemyArmyModelTests.cs` (new), `StrategicDefenseTests.cs`, `WlCampaignTests.cs` (one initializer), `CampaignMilitaryFixTests.cs`, csproj (two pure files).

Where the build differs from the text above (each checked against the decompile):
- **P6 bound gains a second retreat factor.** `num28` takes `speedfactorretreat` for movement mode 5 (134058) **and** again when `lastpathsetoutofsafetyzone && unittyp > 13` (134062), so a campaign group can carry it twice; the bound is `… × max(1, retreat, fallback × cheat) × max(1, retreat)`. Inferred, not measured: the block catch-up factor `GetBlockDifferenceSpeedAdjustment` (134373+) is 1 for the lead block of a one-block campaign group, and the reinforcement `timeofarrival` rescale (133536-133541) is a battle-arrival path; neither is in the bound. `railroadspeedadj` is the largest among the alliances at war with the side (a scalar per alliance, 213217). Test `DriftIsCampaignSpeedUpperBound`.
- **Identity, as built (revised at verify-g1 r1, F2):** match order exact key → re-key (same unit name = text before the last `:`, same `UnitTyp`, among beliefs unmatched this review, **within the plausibility radius**) → rename-retire (the nearest unmatched belief of the same `UnitTyp` **within the plausibility radius**) → new. The plausibility radius is a normal road march × days unseen in [1, 7] (`CampaignSpeedBound.RoadMarchPerDay`, see Identity above); the first build used the P6 radius, which is ≈ 61× a road march per day (verify-g1 F2) and let any new group's first sighting retire the nearest remembered one. `Update` gains `marchPerDay` (from `EnemyDetectionRuntime.MarchFor`). Tests `NewGroupFarAwayDoesNotRetireRememberedOne`, `SameNameBeyondPlausibleMarchIsAnotherGroup`, `RoadMarchIsTheNormalDayBelowTheBound`; the re-key test checks continuity (`LastEmbarkedDay` carries over). Decay and radius are absolute from `LastSeenDay` (`EnemyArmyModel.SeenRadius = 0`: a sighting is the true `GetPosition()`), which is what makes a second update on one day a no-op. **An `ObjectiveGarrison` belief does not drift** (a fort does not move; its confidence still decays).
- **Detection rule (d) contact** = the enemy shares a battle with an own formation (`unittriggeredbattle`, set for every unit of one battle by `MoveUnitsToBFLocation`, 94063) or lies within the own formation's **or its own** bugle range (vanilla's campaign contact, `CheckEnemyContactCampaign` 115634-115637, looks out to the closest enemy's bugle range × 1.1, else the unit's own; the earlier citation of 127231/127235 was wrong: 127235 is a no-op). Contact wins over the fog rules for the `Source` label; `NoFog` wins over everything.
- **Every detection decision is a pure `DetectionRules` function (verify-g1 F3):** `Mode` (flags off → ConfigOff, fog off → NoFog, player side → OwnSideWithinFog, else FogGrid), `IsOmniscient`, `GridUnavailable` (a FogGrid review without the grid detects by contact only), `RelationOf` (own or allied = viewer, at-war non-fleet non-garrison = enemy), `GridVisible` (fog off or no usable grid → not visible; a probe exception → not visible and `threw`), `TryObjectiveGarrison` (only when seen); the runtime's call of each is pinned by exact line in `DetectionWiringPinned`, and `UnitIsHidden` has one call site, reached only through `GridVisible`.
- **Detection is bookkeeping and runs for every alliance (integrator decision 2026-10-15):** `ReviewPlan` runs the snapshot and the belief step before both the stand-down and the bookkeeping returns, and the coordinator's W&L CIC branch calls `ReviewPlan` for the player's alliance (no plan, no publish, `ReviewExecute` never runs there), so `BeliefsFor` is populated for a stand-down alliance and during the pause (`StandDownAllianceStillDetects`). This closes the open point of the first build.
- **Order-raising threats need a recent sighting (integrator decision 2026-10-15, verify-g1 F1):** a raid and a landed sea invasion qualify only when last seen within `EnemyBeliefReadings.RecentSightingDays` = the landing window, 3 days (vanilla keeps a defender only while a real enemy stays near its destination, `CheckForEndOfDefensiveOperations` 13795-13846, and a raid aborts on the enemy's current position, 9099-9101); the watch (afloat off our coast) and every strength sum stay confidence-weighted over all live beliefs. `LapsedThreatTracker` (pure, one per alliance in `DefenseIntentRuntime`, CampaignScope) reports a sea-invasion or raid signature raised last review that no longer qualifies once more as `SightingLapsed` → Recovered (raids too), so the coastal runner holds its units for the cooldown and releases them when the signature leaves the output. It replaces the coordinator's `_previousSifSignatures`/`CollectSifSignatures` (deleted) and `CollapsedSeaInvasionKeys`; the recency clock is `EnemyDetectionRuntime.ReviewDayFor` (the rollback list is dated by the review stamp, so it is always recent). A lapsed copy keeps its `AssetName` for the signature the runner releases on but covers no asset (`LapsedThreatTracker.CoversAsset`, verify-g1 R2-4): the runtime proximity pass (`ExtractAssetProximityThreats`) still raises a threat for a believed enemy close to that asset. The ledger's own `coveredAssets` (`DefenseIntentLedger.Build`, which skips the *guard* pass for an asset with a threat response) keeps its pre-G1 behaviour for Recovered sources: the Recovered response holds the slot. Test `LapsedThreatLeavesAssetToProximity`. Tests `RaidLeavingSightRecoversThenReleases` (days 0-3 ActiveInvasion with a custom order, day 4 Recovered without one, day 5 gone), `LandedInvasionNeedsRecentSighting`, `ExpiredSeaInvasionBeliefCollapsesThreat`.
- **Viewers** are built in the detection pass from `campaignunitlist` (own or allied, active, visibility ≠ 0, not routed, 101325), so garrisons, fleets and allied units count as they paint `fows[1]`; the snapshot's `FormationRecord.SightRadius` alone would miss allied viewers. The grid is `GameController`'s second `FogOfWar` (`CreateFOWObjects` 116507); a missing grid or a probe exception fails closed (contact only, one bounded warning), never omniscient.
- **Enemy garrisons** are skipped in the army pass (`SeparateUnits` routes `garrisonreference` groups to `owngarrisons`, 10510-10513); enemy-held objective targets come from the snapshot's `ObjectiveRecord.HolderAlliance` (at war with the side), and a fort maps to its closest town, else its parent/closest IIP (the snapshot's own-garrison mapping), matched by the target's position.
- **W&L incident mask (G7: dedupe with `BattlePerception` when `main` merges into `feat/campaign-planner`; `main` now carries `BattlePerception.cs:119-130`; the code is left as is, verify-g1 M4)** ported verbatim from `fix/campaign-terrain-bugs` (`BattlePerception.PlayerIntelIncidents`/`IncidentNotLaunched`/`IncidentLaunched`/`MasksPlayerIntelIncidents` → `DetectionRules.*`; the same `FieldMask<DLC_WL.Incident, float>` from `Patches/LogisticsSwaps.cs`), wrapped as `DetectionRules.RunMasked` (restore in `finally`). **Dedupe at the merge of that branch (G7)**: keep one copy.
- **Sea invasion and raid inference.** A landing needs state `EnemyBelief` lacked: G1 adds `EnemyBelief.LastEmbarkedDay` (−1 never). Watch = `Embarked` and `NearOwnCoast`; landed = `OnOwnSoil`, not embarked, and `LastSeenDay − LastEmbarkedDay ≤ 3`; raid = `CavalryMajority` and `OnOwnSoil`, not embarked. `DefenseThreatSource` loses `InvasionForceInstanceId/SpotName/SourcePortName/RaidGroupInstanceId/RaidCurrentState` (all vanilla plan state) and gains `EnemyUnitKey`; `VanillaCollapsed` (never set in production: the `TODO(Task 14)` tombstone) becomes `SightingLapsed`, set by `LapsedThreatTracker` (above) for sea invasions and raids alike (vanilla's raid `currentstate >= 5` was plan state). Signatures: `sif:<UnitKey>`, `raid:<UnitKey>:<asset>`.
- **Morale.** Beliefs carry none (a sighting does not show it): capital threat samples use morale 1 (vanilla's `min(str, str × morale)` becomes the strength), asset-proximity `ComputeEffective` takes 1, the fort governor's enemy side drops its morale weight. Strength is `groupstrengthdirect` × confidence where the old reads used `groupstrengthactive`.
- **`SafeStrengthRatio`** keeps vanilla's own terms (own direct strength − `recruitscontractexpiries` + `troopsinrecruitment`, 12366-12394) and drops the enemy's `troopsinrecruitment` and the chapter-0 neutrals (12395-12402: not seen).
- **Rollback list** (either flag off): `EnemyDetectionRuntime.OmniscientBeliefs` builds full-confidence beliefs from `enemyunits` with the same traits, once per review day (keyed and dated by the coordinator's calendar stamp `EnemyDetectionRuntime.StampReviewDay`, set before any alliance is reviewed, also with the planner off; verify-g1 M5 and R2-3), not per `BeliefsFor` call; it carries no landing history (`LastEmbarkedDay` only when currently embarked), so in rollback a sea invasion shows as a watch until it is ashore, then as an asset-proximity threat. With the fog-honest flag off and the planner on, the model itself is fed `NoFog` detections (omniscient by design: that flag is the rollback).
- **Shared-file edits beyond the plan's list:** `StrategicCoordinator` (`_previousSifSignatures`, its reset line, the `BuildInput` argument and `CollectSifSignatures` deleted; one `ReviewPlan` call in the W&L CIC branch) and `DefenseIntentLedger.cs`/`DefenseIntentInput.cs`/`DefenseThreatSignature.cs` (the source fields above), `CampaignMilitaryGates.cs` (three deleted helpers), `CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (the `SightRadiusOf` extraction).
- **Residue of the G6 fog grep after G1** (`evidence/g1/fog-gate.txt`): the listed permanent residue (`EnemyDetectionRuntime.cs` rollback read, `ImportanceValuesPatch.cs:107-120`, `WlCampRealismPatch.cs:466,503`, `TelegraphConstructionRuntime.cs:152` annotated `// own side`, `FastForwardAiCatchUpPatch.cs:147`, `WlCampaignDispatchRuntime.cs:424,426,446`) plus later slices' lines (`StrategicCoordinator.cs:761` `ReadLocalForceBalance` → G1b; `DefensiveOpsPatch.cs:87`, `TransferOfUnitsPatch.cs:104,108,142`, `CampaignMilitaryGates.cs:88` → G5b; `CoordinatedOffensiveOperationsPatch.cs:528-547` → G6) and **one line the residue list misses: `Patches/SkirmisherScreenDistancePatch.cs:102`** (BC9 #80, after `93f6a82`): a battle patch reading the destination of the AI's *own* skirmisher link (`unit.GetLastTransmittedPathPos`), not an enemy read; now listed in G6's expected residue (verify-g1 M3). `campaignunitlist`: own-alliance scans only (`FormationDirectiveRuntime.BuildAreaIndex` now filters `u.alliance != allianceId`, the snapshot, the telegraph own-side scan) and `EnemyDetectionRuntime`.
- **The belief clock is a calendar day count (verify-g1 R2-3, integrator decision 2026-10-15).** G0's `ReviewCadenceCatchUp.DaySerial` was `year × 372 + month × 31 + day`, so a month end inflated an age by up to 3 days (a raid seen 27 February read 5 days old on 1 March and lapsed early; decay, expiry and the plausibility radius shifted the same way). It now counts days from 1 January of year 1 with Gregorian month lengths and leap years: UniStorm's campaign calendar (`calendarType` 1) rolls months over that way (`CalculateMonths` 157818-157853, February 157834-157845) and builds `UniStormDate = new DateTime(year, month, day)` from the counters (157889; 155866 at load); vanilla has no day counter of its own, and `GameVars.currenttimefromstart` is not one (30171: `(year − 1860) + (month − 1)/12 + (day − 1)/365.25 + hour/8766`, months 1/12 year wide, so ×365.25 makes 31 January and 1 February the same day; the verifier's suggestion was not taken for that reason, and the rollback cache that used it now keys on the stamp). Pure arithmetic, no `DateTime` (no throw on a transient date); positive for game dates, so −1 stays every consumer's "never". `SkippedWeeklyGate` reads the day of the month through the inverse (`DayOfMonth`) instead of `serial % 31`. Tests `CadenceDaySerialCountsCalendarDays` (27 Feb → 1 Mar 1862 = 2, 1864 = 3, 31 Dec → 1 Jan = 1, a 30-day month, every day of 1861-1866 against `DateTime`, no phantom alternating-day catch-up at 28 Feb → 1 Mar, weekly gates across February and the new year) and `BeliefAgeCountsCalendarDays`. The policy need step (`UpdatePolicyPlan`'s day gap) reads the same serial and gains the same correction. **Every `DaySerial` consumer inherits the fix** (G2 protection, G1b missions, G3 battle inbox; the integrator resolves the merge with those branches). **Boundary:** the legacy inline `year * 372 + month * 31 + day` serials (`StrategicCoordinator.cs:433` capital threat and director posture, `:623` phase truth, `:1222` operational probe, `:1390` campaign pace over the battle history; `CIC.cs:84,493`; `BattleHistoryQuery.cs:21`; `TacticalObserverPatch.cs:408` uses its own 400/32 encoding) are persisted in the sidecar (`OperationStartedDaySerial` etc.) and keep their scale; never compare the two. `DetectionWiringPinned` fails on any `372` in `Strategic/Planner/` code and on a `currenttimefromstart` in `EnemyDetectionRuntime`.
- **Day scales named and pinned (verify-g1 r3 R3-1, R3-3):** in `StrategicCoordinator` the calendar day is `calendarDay` (cadence gates, the policy need step, `_lastReviewCalendarDay`, `_needCalendarDay`, the detection stamp) and the legacy serial is `legacySerial` (every `year * 372 + month * 31 + day` local and the `UpdateCapitalThreat`/`PublishDirectorPosture` parameters); `ReviewDayScalesNeverMix` pins each call site to its scale (capital threat, director posture, replan memory and front-change day legacy; policy and cadence calendar; one calendar producer). `DaySerial` takes vanilla's 1-12 `monthCounter` as is (the month carry of the r2 build was unreachable and is removed).
- **Plausibility fixtures (verify-g1 R2-1, R2-2):** `ReviewDayFor`'s body and its stamp in `Detect` are pinned; `SameDayIdentityUsesOneDayOfMarch` (a re-key and a rename on the last sighting's own review day get one day of march) and `GarrisonIdentityHasNoMarchRadius` (a new group five units from a fort never retires its garrison; the same garrison under a new commander at the same town is re-keyed).
- **Known limitation (verify-g1 R2-5, follows from the road-march plausibility radius):** a group renamed while it moves by rail or river farther than seven days of road march leaves its old belief in place until that belief expires (30 days or confidence < 0.05): a decaying double count. Its weight halves every 7 days meanwhile, so every strength sum counts it at its decayed confidence.
- **Open point of the first build, closed:** detection for a W&L CIC player's stand-down alliance (integrator decision above).

Red-first: all 36 `EnemyArmyModelTests` were run against compiling stubs first (`evidence/g1/red-first.txt`); 35 were red, `NoThreatFromUnseenInvasionForce` passed against the stub (an empty model yields no threat either way; it is red against a model that keeps every detection regardless of sighting — covered by the rule tests). Mutation plants (scratch copy of `baee28a` with a harness that reports every test, `evidence/g1/mutation-results.txt` and `mutation-rerun.txt`): 23 plants, each turns the named test red: `Covers` always true and `TryDecide` ignoring coverage → `EuropeanPaintedCellWithoutViewerStaysUnseen`; half-life 14 → `UnseenConfidenceHalvesEverySevenDays`; slope cap 1 and the safety-zone retreat dropped → `DriftIsCampaignSpeedUpperBound`; re-key removed → `CommanderChangeReKeysBeliefNotDuplicates`; composite key removed → `DuplicateUnitKeyNeverMergesTwoArmies`; rename retire removed → `RenamedGroupRetiresOldBelief`; expiry at 31 → `ExpiresAtThirtyDaysOrBelowMinConfidence`; garrison drifts → `GarrisonBeliefDoesNotDrift`; store falls back to omniscient when the model is empty → `CapitalThreatNeverOmniscientWithFlagsOn`; mask restore outside `finally` and mask on rule (c) → `PlayerIntelIncidentsNeverRevealToAiSide`; landing window 5 → `LandingInferredFromSeenForceOnOwnCoast`; embarked without coast → `SeaInvasionThreatOnlyFromSeenEmbarkedForce`; guns unweighted → `LocalPressureExchangeUsesBeliefGuns`; runtime wiring (source pins): the belief step or its `[EnemyBeliefs]` row after the bookkeeping return, `UnitIsHidden` without the `usefow` gate, the probe outside the mask → `DetectionWiringPinned`; `FrontSectorRuntime` reading `enemyunits`, the area index over both alliances, `UpdateCapitalThreat` reading `_enemyUnitsField` → `MigratedReadersReadBeliefsOnly`. Two first-run findings were fixed in the tests: the re-key fixture sat inside the old belief's radius (the rename rule masked a removed re-key), and the pin did not cover the `[EnemyBeliefs]` row's position.

Verify-g1 r1 plants (scratch copy of `5a8d984`, same harness, `evidence/g1/mutation-r2.txt` and `mutation-r2b.txt`): 29 plants, all red by name. The verifier's survivors, re-expressed against the pure rules and the pinned runtime lines: V1 (no usable grid answers visible; the runtime passing `true`), V2 (an exception answers visible; the runtime not setting `_gridBroken`), V3 (no grid means NoFog; `GridUnavailable` false; `IsOmniscient` true for FogGrid), V4 (allied groups dropped; the runtime passing `false`), V9 (`!FlagOn` in `BeliefsFor`), V10 (the runtime passing `true`; `TryObjectiveGarrison` ignoring `seen`), V11, V13, V16; plus F1 (raid or landing without recency, no lapse report, a lapsed raid staying active, the tracker not closed, lapses remembered or appended before the remembering), F2 (re-key or retire without the plausibility radius, retire on the P6 radius, no 7-day cap, road march ignoring the road cost), the stand-down return before detection, the coordinator's CIC branch without its review, and the rollback list rebuilt per call. One plant was equivalent and its code was removed: skipping `SightingLapsed` sources when remembering could never fire, since lapsed copies are appended after the remembering.

Verify-g1 r2 landing plants (scratch copy of `9cabeaa`, `evidence/g1/mutation-r3.txt`): 13 plants, all red: the 372 serial restored, the weekly gate on `serial % 31`, `DayOfMonth` off by one, no leap years (cadence tests); `ReviewDayFor` returning −1, `Detect` not stamping its day, the coordinator not stamping the review day, the rollback keyed by `currenttimefromstart` or dated by the planner's day, the runtime proximity pass bypassing `CoversAsset` (`DetectionWiringPinned`); the plausibility floor 0 (`SameDayIdentityUsesOneDayOfMarch`); a garrison given a march radius (`GarrisonIdentityHasNoMarchRadius`); a lapsed copy covering its asset (`LapsedThreatLeavesAssetToProximity`).

Known consequence of the identity rule as specified (recorded for G1b/G3; narrowed by the plausibility radius, verify-g1 F2): two different enemy groups that share a display name and unit type (vanilla reuses names such as "1st Corps") can still be re-keyed onto one belief while one of them is out of sight when the sighting lies within a normal march of the remembered one: no double count, but the belief's position jumps; and after a collision pair (`UnitKey#InstanceId`) loses one member from sight, the plain-key detection re-keys the first unmatched composite belief, which may be the other group's.

G1 smoke items (checked with G7's smoke; `LOG`/`SESSION` as there):
1. `SESSION/campaign*.jsonl`: `[EnemyBeliefs] alliance=1 mode=FogGrid seen=<n> remembered=<n> expired=<n> contact=<n>` on the first review and then only on change; early in the war `seen` below the number of Union campaign groups; `remembered > 0` after a Union army leaves every CSA viewer's sight.
2. W&L career: `[EnemyBeliefs] alliance=<player alliance> mode=OwnSideWithinFog`; with the player commandless the rows continue for both alliances (bookkeeping) while no planner executor runs.
3. Fog off (`usefow` false in options): `mode=NoFog`, no `campaign-planner:detect-grid-failed` and no NullReferenceException.
4. `LOG` absent: `campaign-planner:detect-failed`, `campaign-planner:detect-grid-failed`, `campaign-planner:incident-restore`, `campaign-planner:detect-no-grid`; `campaign-planner:unitkey-collision` at most once and explained.
5. `SESSION/performance*.jsonl`: `campaign.planner.detect` mean ≤ 1 ms per alliance at 20x.
6. `[DefenseIntent]` threat signatures are `sif:<unit>:<commander>` / `raid:<unit>:<commander>:<asset>`; no `sif:<instance>:<spot>:<port>`.
7. Rollback: `Enable Campaign Fog-Honest Beliefs = false` → `mode=ConfigOff`; the front and defense ledgers behave as before G1.
8. A raid that leaves every viewer's sight: its `[DefenseIntent]` `raid:<unit>:<commander>:<asset>` response is `ActiveInvasion` for at most 3 review days after the last sighting, then `Recovered` once, then gone; the coastal runner releases its units (`custom-order` lines stop for that signature). The same for a landed `sif:` signature.
9. W&L CIC career: `[EnemyBeliefs] alliance=<player alliance> mode=OwnSideWithinFog` rows continue while no `[CampaignPlan]`/`[CampaignMover]` rows appear for that alliance (detection is bookkeeping).

---

## G1b — Active reconnaissance (planning half: recon planner, mission ledger, belief-driven probing, directive cleanup)

Why: `EnemyArmyModel` is passive (P6 decay only). With fog-honest beliefs and no omniscient input, nothing reduces uncertainty, so the planner would lose track of enemy armies. Vanilla never scouts on the campaign map, so Whiskey must place viewers deliberately. The pure files build against G0 alone (`SectorModel`, `LogisticsGate.ClampToReach`, `ViewerCoverage`, `EnemyBeliefQuery` are G0 contracts) and start in parallel with G1-G3; the runtime edits need G1 merged (`EnemyDetectionRuntime.BeliefsFor`), the runtime step needs G3 merged (the plan it reads), and G4 merges after G1b (it calls `ReconPlanner.PickFormation`).

**What the mod does today (survey of `main`, 2026-10-15):** the campaign probe is omniscient and type-blind — `OperationalProbeRuntime` targets the CIC phase objective (`:42-43`), its local strengths come from `StrategicCoordinator.ReadLocalForceBalance` (`:728-771`, summing `enemyunits.groupstrengthactive`), and the probe unit is the smallest eligible formation of any type (`OperationalProbeLedger` 108-113, 311-339); `ContactEvidenceLedger` (43-115) uses the same strengths plus battle history. Cavalry only yields the `RaidSupport` directive (`FormationDirectiveLedger` 212-217), whose only reader is `PerkSelectionPatch:134`; `RaidAllowed` (`FormationDirectiveLedger:41/215`) has no reader; the `Screen` directive (`:222`, `:251` default) and `FrontSectorLedger`'s `ArmyRole` (`:16`, `ChooseRole` 217-232, written to `FrontSector.Role` at `:125`) have no movement reader; the planner's `TheaterPosture.Screen` was only a label. This slice gives each a mover or deletes it.

**Vanilla facts this slice rests on (read them; verified 2026-10-15):**
- **Vanilla AI never scouts.** "Scouting" is campaign status 15 in `GameVars.unitstatustextcamp` (65018-65022), shown when a moving group has `cavalryorders == 1` (`UpdateUnitStatusCampaign` 119914-119935). `cavalryorders` (110590; 1 scouting, 2 raiding, 3 guarding) is set only by `Regiment.SetCavalryOrders` (122074-122082), reached from the player's order path `BattleUnits.ChangeCampaignOrders(reg, 120 + n)` (90720-90770; through the order queue 125409-125412 under order delays) and from raid forces (`SetCavalryOrders(2)` 9177; 0 at 20336, 20419). No `AICampaign` job ever sets 1 or 3.
- **What scouting does:** each cavalry regiment (`unittyp == 1`) of a group with `cavalryorders == 1` adds `strength × intelligencegatheringscout × cunning factor` to `groupintelligencegathering` (120424-120426) → readiness gain (`influenceofintelligenceonreadiness`, 115017/115306), the inspector-only intelligence map (101330) and the player's spotted-mark jitter (142695); scouting perk experience (115368-115372); a later arrival when the group joins a battle as reinforcement (`reinforcementdelaycavalryorders`, 67742-67744, 138868-138870); reset to 0 at readiness step ≤ 1 (113960). **It does not widen the fog radius**: `UpdateViewRange` (101296-101307) uses only `maximumlineofsight[unittyp]`, a perk-8 factor (battle units only once `fix/campaign-perk-ids` lands), weather, lie-down and incident 63.
- **So reconnaissance = viewer placement.** Every campaign group with visibility state ≠ 0 paints its side's grid within its radius (`AssignUnitToFOW` 116520-116534; 101296-101302); the side sees what its viewers cover (G1 detection rule).
- **Raid forces are not scouts.** `CheckCreationOfRaidForces` (job 30, 17568-17600; alliances 0-1, `maxairaidforces`, `probabilityofraidforces[alliance]`) raises a new cavalry corps (unittyp 15) near a town (`RaidForce` ctor 8890-8932); `UpdateRaidForces` (9030-9056) fills it to `minimumgroupstrengthforraids`, `SearchObjective` (9130-9186) picks an unguarded enemy-reachable building within `raidgrouptargetdistance` (depots weighted ×4) and moves there with `DefensiveMovingOrder` + `SetCavalryOrders(2)`; `CheckAbort` (9058-9128) aborts on morale, supply `GetGroupSupplyState < 0.25` (9092; halved metric, so 0.5 raw) or an enemy closing in (read from the omniscient `closestenemyunitfarreg`) and returns to a friendly town/depot. Raid groups are excluded from offensives (14095) and are `FormationFlags.RaidForce` (busy) for the planner; their sightings still count through G1's detection.
- Cavalry share of a group: `groupstrengthperunittypcampaigndirect[1]` over the array sum (the array vanilla sums at 14213). Unit types (`unitprefs.txt`): 0 infantry, 1 cavalry, 4 scouts (battle), 13 brigade, 14 division, 15 corps, 16 army, 17 fleet.
- **Fog is one-way and the AI is omniscient.** `iswithinfog` is forced false for player-alliance units on campaign (127211-127215); the fog-filtered range lists escape for player-alliance targets (122650, 122699); `closestenemyunitfar*` has no fog term (122657-122662). `AICampaign` (8775-~18000) reads none of `fows[1]`, `iswithinfog`, `unitrevealedbyenemy`, `lastspotted`, `spottedmarks`, `UseIntelligenceMap`, `GetIntelligenceOnPos`, `groupintelligencegathering`. `fows[1]` is still painted every frame on campaign (29916-29920, 100675-100681), which G1 reads; the AI side's picture is therefore Whiskey's: `fows[1].UnitIsHidden` plus the viewer-radius check computed from vanilla's own radius formula.
- **Sight and command ranges:** `maximumlineofsight` for division, corps and army groups is 50 (`unitprefs.txt` 645/689/733), reduced by weather (101302); campaign `commanderrange` is 25 / 40 / 75 (661/705/749, set at 113033). `FormationRecord.SightRadius` equals the radius that paints the grid, so ViewerCoverage never claims sight the grid lacks. BUG-AI-003 (vanilla applied the Expert Scouts factor on `HasPerk(8)` with no campaign guard, 101298; campaign perk 8 is Ambulance Corps) is fixed by `fix/campaign-perk-ids` (flag `Fix Perk Id Mismatches`, default on: the perk table follows the unit type — ≤ 13 battle, 17 fleet, 14-16 and 18 campaign), so campaign sight takes no perk term at all; `SightRadiusFormula` takes the flag and keeps the old factor only when it is off.
- **Destinations are off limits.** Vanilla reads enemy destinations (`GetLastTransmittedPathPos(ignoreorderdelay: true)`, 127552) at 13121, 13600, 13673, 9099 and 94018 (and 10535 in `UpdateAreaPoints`); recon, beliefs and every migrated ledger read only `GetPosition()` of seen groups. A pure test pins it (`ReconNeverReadsDestinations` against the snapshot contract: `FormationRecord`/`EnemyDetection` carry no destination of an enemy).
- **No vanilla probe exists.** `CheckOffensiveMovements` masses to `aidominanceforoffensiveoperations` 1.1 (campaignprefs 1480-1481) × the initiative factor (14257-14281); the only small move is a single unit when the target zone is empty (14373-14377). Defensive operations mass to 1.5× and need ≥ 0.85 (13641, 13682). So the G1b probe is new behaviour, not a replacement of a vanilla one; it is sized well below vanilla's offensive mass (`AdvanceGuardMaxStrengthShare`).
- **March facts the executor relies on:** the pathfinder prefers roads 2:1 for every unit (130430-130434; the campaignprefs 752-754 comment is wrong); `MoveUnitTo` bypasses order delay (14514) and stacks with ±2 offsets (94009-94029); `aiarrivalfactor` syncs arrivals of units in vanilla operations (8824-8866, 10213-10250; applied 133964); groups not force-marching stop at night (133958-133961). Recon moves are ordinary `MoveUnitTo` moves (no forced march: it cuts supply, 114258, and `doublequick` is not confirmed to reach groups, 91541/91559); a screen that stops for the night is still a viewer.
- **W&L intel is player-only** (incidents 60/61/64 reveal all, 101183-101185; incident 63 widens the player command's view, 101307-101309; companion actions 71/73; project 108 "recruit agents"). Nothing for the AI side to reuse; under `ai_vs_ai` the player's AI side sees through `fows[0]` like the player (`OwnSideWithinFog`), so its screens also widen the player's map picture — intended.
- Contact: `CheckEnemyContactCampaign` (115620+) and `buglerange`; movement `MoveUnitTo(…, true)` (14479-14540, queued, around enemies).
- Design backlog adopted: `docs/strategic-recon-commitment.md` § "Advance Guard And Picket Sandbox" (the file is 98 lines; the section is near its end) and the archived `docs/superpowers/specs/archive/2026-05-14-scourge-campaign-advance-guard-sandbox-design.md` (missions AdvanceGuard, Picket; rules: never detach below readiness minimum, never the last viable combat child, never the W&L player's command, rejoin on close contact, retreat when the strength gate fails, persist missions, clear on missing parent/child/objective). **Not adopted:** splitting a column (vanilla has no AI detach API — `CampaignMainPanel.DetachUnitToIndependentCorps` 178375 is UI-only — so missions use whole existing campaign groups), and the supply-base attack/patrol missions (raid forces already do supply-base raids).

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/ReconPlanner.cs` (pure: approach axes, coverage gaps, screen/picket/advance-guard/probe tasks, formation choice), `Strategic/Planner/ReconMissionLedger.cs` (pure: mission state machine, persisted); modify `Strategic/FormationDirective.cs` + `Strategic/FormationDirectiveLedger.cs` (directive cleanup below; G6 later removes `ApplyOperationalProbe` from the same file, a different region), `Strategic/FrontSectorLedger.cs` (delete `ArmyRole`, `FrontSector.Role`, `ChooseRole`, and any summary/signature use of them), `Patches/PerkSelectionPatch.cs` (`:134` case only; G6 migrates the rest of the file), modify `StrategicCoordinator.ReadLocalForceBalance` (`:728-771`, enemy sum from `EnemyDetectionRuntime.BeliefsFor`, so the surviving probe layer and phase truth are fog-honest until G6 deletes them), `Strategic/OperationalProbeRuntime.cs` (`CurrentEnemyStrength` / `ContactEvidenceInput` from beliefs, `:95-118`), `Strategic/ContactEvidenceLedger.cs` (doc comment: strengths are beliefs; adds `BeliefConfidence01` to the input so stale beliefs cannot escalate), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (recon step after the plan, before allocation); test `ReconPlannerTests.cs`. Plugin: bind `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance`.

**Interfaces — Consumes:** G0 contracts (incl. `FormationRole.Recon`, `ReconTask`, `EnemyBelief`, `ViewerCoverage`, `EnemyBeliefQuery`, `SectorModel`, `TheaterPlan`, `LogisticsGate.ClampToReach`); G1's `EnemyDetectionRuntime.BeliefsFor` (runtime edits only).
**Produces:**
```csharp
public enum ReconState { Assigned = 0, Moving = 1, Holding = 2, Recalled = 3, Cleared = 4 }
public static class ReconPlanner {
  public const int MaxScreensPerTheater = 2; public const int MaxMissionsPerAlliance = 6;
  public const float StandoffFraction = 0.8f;   // hold point ≥ 0.8 × own sight radius from the nearest belief
  public const float MinCavalryShare = 0.5f;     // a screen is a cavalry-majority group when one exists
  public const float AgeWeight = 0.5f, RadiusWeight = 0.3f, StrengthWeight = 0.2f;
  public const float AdvanceGuardMaxStrengthShare = 0.25f; // of the main-effort strength
  // Tasks: screens toward the oldest / most uncertain believed armies and along unwatched approaches to the own capital
  // and the main-effort axis; one picket per major own town on a threatened axis; one advance guard ahead of the main effort.
  public static void PlanTasks(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, TheaterPlan plan, System.Collections.Generic.List<ReconTask> into);
  public static int PickFormation(CampaignSnapshot s, in ReconTask task, System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<int> taken, bool mainEffortHasScreen); // instanceId or -1
  public static bool UnwatchedAlong(float ax, float az, float bx, float bz, float sampleSpacing,
                                    float[] viewerX, float[] viewerZ, float[] viewerRadius, int viewerCount); // ViewerCoverage samples
}
public struct ReconMission { public int MissionId; public ReconKind Kind; public int InstanceId; /* in-session */ public string UnitKey; /* persisted; rebound by IndexOfKey after a load */ public float HoldX, HoldZ; public int SectorId;
  public ReconState State; public int IssuedDay; public int LastContactDay; public string Reason; }
public sealed class ReconMissionLedger { public const int MaxMissionDays = 20;
  public void Sync(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<ReconTask> tasks, Allocation a, int daySerial);   // assign / keep / clear
  public void OnReview(int instanceId, bool exists, bool inBattle, bool contact, float supply01, bool ownGroupInRetreat, int daySerial); // transitions
  public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<ReconMission> Missions { get; }
  public bool IsRecon(int instanceId);
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<ReconMission> missions); }
```
Contract types it uses are in G0: `FormationRole.Recon`, `ReconKind`, `ReconTask`, `Assignment.ReconMissionId`, `FormationRecord.CavalryShare/SightRadius/Chain`, `LogisticsGate.ClampToReach` ("LogisticsGate covers recon reach"). G4 consumes `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` and assigns `Recon` before Support/MainEffort (rules in G4).

**Rules (pure, pinned by tests):**
- **Axes.** Approaches = segments from the own capital and from the main-effort front point toward each sector centroid holding a belief or an enemy-held objective; plus the main-effort axis itself. An approach is *unwatched* when a sample every `0.5 ×` sight radius is outside every own/allied viewer's radius (`ViewerCoverage`).
- **Screen tasks.** Score believed armies by `AgeWeight × age/ExpireDays + RadiusWeight × radius/maxRadius + StrengthWeight × strength/maxStrength`; the top ones and the unwatched capital/main-axis approaches become screen tasks, ≤ `MaxScreensPerTheater` per `Theater`, ≤ `MaxMissionsPerAlliance` in all. Hold point = along the axis toward the target, at least `StandoffFraction ×` the screen's sight radius from the nearest belief, then `LogisticsGate.ClampToReach`; no task when no in-reach point remains.
- **Formation choice.** Prefer cavalry-majority campaign groups (`MinCavalryShare`), then the smallest fighting group nearest the task's `FromX/FromZ`; never busy formations (G4's busy rule), never a `PlayerCommand` chain class, never a `PlayerMapGroup` formation as the only screen of an approach, never the last cavalry-majority group allocated to the main effort (**the main effort keeps its own screen**), never a group below vanilla's movement minima (readiness step ≥ 2, `aiminimumstrengthformovement`, `aiminimummoraleformovement`, 14091-14106).
- **Picket.** One per own major town / depot on a Hold or Screen sector axis that has no own viewer, from formations already in that sector; never the sector's final reserve (Hold strength after the picket ≥ `HoldRatio ×` believed enemy).
- **Advance guard.** One per main effort when the main-effort force exceeds `RequiredRatio ×` believed enemy and no believed enemy is within one sight radius of the front: a group of ≤ `AdvanceGuardMaxStrengthShare` of the main-effort strength holds one sight radius ahead on the axis; rejoins (mission `Recalled`, hold point = main body) when a belief comes within the standoff.
- **Screen-posture sectors.** Every sector the plan marks `TheaterPosture.Screen` gets at least one Screen task on its most threatened approach (that is what the posture means now).
- **Belief-driven probe (replaces `OperationalProbe*`, which G6 deletes).** When the main-effort objective's surroundings hold no belief, or only beliefs with confidence < 0.5, one Probe task sends a small group (cavalry-majority preferred; ≤ `AdvanceGuardMaxStrengthShare` of the main effort) to the standoff point short of the objective. On contact the probe's sighting refreshes the beliefs and `ContactEvidenceLedger` (fed by beliefs and battle history, with the new confidence input) classifies it: Escalate → the mission clears and the planner's balance term uses the fresh belief; Withdraw / overmatched → `Recalled`. No probe while the main effort is already in contact with the objective.
- **Directive cleanup (no dead code).** `FormationDirective.Screen` now means "on a planner Screen/Picket/AdvanceGuard/Probe mission": `FormationDirectiveLedger` takes `Func<string, bool> onReconMission` (the runtime passes `ReconMissionLedger.IsRecon` by unit key) and assigns `Screen` only then; the old heuristic cases become `Delay` (`division-vs-army-no-support`, `:222`; `Delay` has a reader at `FormationDirectiveRuntime:161`) and `Hold` (the `default-screen` fallback, `:251`). `RaidSupport` and `RaidAllowed` are deleted (vanilla raid forces own supply raiding); `PerkSelectionPatch:134` maps `Screen` (a formation on a recon mission) to the existing `ArmyPerkRole.Scouting` (`PerkSelectionScorer.ArmyScore`: Bureau of Military Information 3, Expert Scouts 16, Balloon Corps 2, Skilled Cartographers 4), not to `Raid` (Partisan Brigades 12, Bushwhackers 13: raiding perks). `ArmyPerkRole.Raid` keeps its other reader (`PerkSelectionPatch:154`). `ArmyRole`/`ChooseRole` are deleted (sector roles are the planner's `TheaterPosture`).
- **Transitions.** Contact (enemy within the screen's `buglerange`, or the screen in battle) → `Recalled` toward the nearest own Hold/MainEffort group; raw `Supply01` < 0.5 (vanilla's raid abort `GetGroupSupplyState < 0.25` at 9092 is on the halved metric, so 0.5 raw) → `Recalled`; formation missing → `Cleared`; `MaxMissionDays` → `Cleared` and re-planned.

**Interface as built** (G4 and G5d build against this, not the block above):
```csharp
public static class ReconPlanner {
  // constants as planned, plus internal BeliefExpireDays = 30 (= G1 EnemyArmyModel.ExpireDays) and PicketHoldRatio = 0.6 (= G3 TheaterPlanner.HoldRatio)
  public static void PlanTasks(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, TheaterPlan plan, ReconMissionLedger missions, List<ReconTask> into);
  public static int PickFormation(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, TheaterPlan plan, ReconMissionLedger missions,
                                  in ReconTask task, ICollection<int> taken, bool mainEffortHasScreen); // instanceId or -1
  public static bool UnwatchedAlong(float ax, float az, float bx, float bz, float sampleSpacing, float[] viewerX, float[] viewerZ, float[] viewerRadius, int viewerCount);
}
public sealed class ReconMissionLedger { public const int MaxMissionDays = 20;
  public void Sync(CampaignSnapshot s, IReadOnlyList<ReconTask> tasks, Allocation a); // binds Recon assignments, writes Assignment.ReconMissionId
  public void OnReview(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a);                           // transitions from the snapshot (fog-honest), rebinds by UnitKey
  internal void OnProbeContact(int instanceId, ContactEvidenceOutput evidence, int daySerial);
  public IReadOnlyList<ReconMission> Missions { get; } public bool IsRecon(int instanceId); public void Restore(IEnumerable<ReconMission> missions); }
internal static class ReconOrders { internal static int CavalryOrderFor(ReconKind, ReconState, float cavalryShare, WlChainClass); internal static bool MayAct(WlChainClass); }
```

**Where the plan was wrong, silent or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. `PickFormation(s, task, taken, mainEffortHasScreen)` cannot tell which formations are the main effort's or a picket's sector, so it takes the `SectorModel` and `TheaterPlan`; `PlanTasks` and `PickFormation` also take the `ReconMissionLedger` (null allowed) for 2 and 3 below.
2. **Flapping (plan silent).** With "unwatched" judged over every own viewer, a screen arriving at its post makes its own approach watched, the task vanishes, `Sync` clears the mission and the next day the gap is back. Formations on an active mission are therefore not viewers for the unwatched/covered tests (`ScreenOwnPostDoesNotWatchItsApproach`).
3. **Churn (plan silent).** `PickFormation` keeps the incumbent: a formation whose active mission of the same kind holds within half its sight radius of the task's hold point gets the task back (`ScreenStaysWithIncumbent`); a formation being recalled is never re-tasked (`RecalledFormationNotRepicked`). G4 must therefore pass the ledger.
4. `Sync(tasks, allocation, daySerial)` cannot fill `ReconMission.UnitKey`: it takes the snapshot (key, day). G4 copies the task's `SectorId` and hold point into the Recon assignment's `SectorId`/`TargetX/Z`; `Sync` pairs them back by exact equality (G4 contract) and writes the mission id into `Assignment.ReconMissionId` (its producer; G5d reads it).
5. `OnReview(instanceId, exists, inBattle, contact, supply01, ownGroupInRetreat, daySerial)` had no input for arrival or the recall point; it became `OnReview(snapshot, allocation)`, which derives them: state = Holding within max(bugle range, ¼ sight) of the hold point, else Moving with `HasPath|MoveQueued`, else Assigned (the executor re-issues). Contact = `InBattle` or a belief **seen today** within bugle range (an advance guard: within the 0.8 × sight standoff, the plan's "rejoins when a belief comes within the standoff"); never `closestenemyunitfar*` or a destination. Recall point = the nearest own formation the allocation holds with (Hold or MainEffort), else the own capital, else the nearest own formation, never a formation that fails `WlChainRules.MayMove` (the player's own command or a map-group player, C4; verify r1 F5; `RecallPointIsNearestHeldFormation`, `RecallPointNeverAPlayerFormation`); a recalled group back there is Cleared (`rejoined`). `OnRetreat` → Cleared (`retreat`: vanilla owns the move; the plan's `ownGroupInRetreat` had no stated outcome). Expiry counts from `IssuedDay` (a re-targeted mission keeps it). Cleared missions stay one review (so G5d can send 120) and are dropped at the next `OnReview`.
6. **Probe contact.** The plan's "Contact → Recalled" would end a probe the moment it does its job; a Probe's contact only stamps `LastContactDay`, and `OnProbeContact` (the `ContactEvidenceLedger` verdict) decides: `AllowsEscalation` → Cleared (`probe-escalate`), `OvermatchedContact`/`EnemyReacted` → Recalled (`probe-withdraw`). Internal, because `ContactEvidenceOutput` is internal.
7. `IsRecon(int instanceId)` is kept; the plan's "by unit key" is the runtime's `Func<string, bool>` (`IndexOfKey` → instance id → `IsRecon`), so the ledger has one lookup.
8. **`ReconOrders` is built here** (the task brief moved it from G5d with `MayAct` and the G5d tests `ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens`, `RecallClearsScoutingOrder`, `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand`, `PlayerCommandRefusedLive`); G5d consumes it. `CavalryOrderFor` is −1 for every chain class but `None` (also `CicSide`), −1 for non-screens and for a screen below the cavalry share, 120 for a recalled or cleared screen whatever its share (a repeat is free, 90738).
9. **Hold points (plan silent on approaches).** An unwatched approach's screen holds at the approach's forward edge (the target sector's centroid, or the main objective for `main-axis`) pulled back toward the axis start until it is 0.8 × sight from every live belief **and every enemy-held objective target** (a town may hide a garrison), then `ClampToReach`; the standoff is re-checked after the clamp. Belief screens hold the standoff short of the belief on the axis from the nearest own formation; screen-posture screens toward the nearest belief (else enemy-held objective) of the sector's own formations. "Unwatched" = some sample along the segment, one every 0.5 × sight, lies outside every own viewer (the snapshot has no allied formations, so allied viewers do not count on the pure side). Approach targets are the centroids of sectors holding a live belief or an enemy-held objective.
10. **Task-time ranges.** Before a formation is picked, the standoff uses the smallest sight radius and the reach clamp the smallest command range over the formations that could take a task, so the point is in view and in reach for any of them; no such formation → no tasks.
11. **Main effort (plan silent).** Front = strength-weighted centre of the own formations in the main sector, else the own formation nearest the main objective; main body = own formations within the relocation range of the front (vanilla's gathering radius) that pass `WlChainRules.MayMove` (never the player's own command or a map-group player, C4; verify r1 F5, `MainBodyStrengthExcludesPlayerFormations`); its strength is the advance-guard/probe size cap (≤ 25 %) and the advance-guard condition (`> MainRequiredRatio × StrengthWithin(beliefs, objective, RelocationRange)`). The main effort's own screen is the `main-axis` task (its `SectorId` = the main sector); every other task may not take the main body's last cavalry-majority group unless `mainEffortHasScreen`.
12. **Probe trigger.** No belief of confidence ≥ 0.5 within the relocation range of the main objective, the objective outside every viewer, and no main-effort contact (an own formation in battle in the main sector, or within its bugle range of the objective).
13. **Busy set** = G4's list plus `CapitalDefense`, `InOffensiveOps`, `InDefensiveOps` (vanilla moves those; a recon move would fight it). **Revised at G4 verify r3 F1 (integrator, 2026-10-15):** `PickFormation` drops every formation that fails `WlChainRules.MayMove`, so the map-group player is never picked for any task, a town picket included (a picket is a pre-pledge, and his own vision already reaches the side through vanilla, so no credit is needed for it); the town gets an AI picket or none, and `ForceAllocator.AssignRecon` can never withhold him (`MapGroupPlayerIsNeverPickedAsAPicket`, through the real `PlanTasks`; this agrees with § G4 Rules, "Recon ... never a busy/player formation"). Every task has one holder. Movement minima mirror 14091-14106 strictly (`>`), on `groupstrengthdirect` where vanilla reads `groupstrengthactive`. A picket comes only from its sector (`PicketComesFromItsOwnSector`) and leaves `(PicketHoldRatio + Tempo.MinimumHoldRatioModifier) × believed enemy in the sector` (G4's hold-need ratio; no `MinGarrisonStrength` floor, per the plan text), counting only committed defenders (`WlChainRules.MayMove`; the recon sums are deliberately more conservative than the planner's hold sums, which also count the map-group player by position): the sector's only AI formation is its final reserve beside the player's formations (C4; verify r1 F1; `PicketReserveIgnoresPlayerCommand`, `PicketReserveIgnoresMapGroupPlayer`, which now read: no pick). Busy-set and 25 % cap pins: `BusySetIncludesVanillaOperationLists`, `AdvanceGuardAndProbeSizeCap`.
14. **Priorities and caps.** Screen-posture 5 > main-axis 4.5 > probe 4.2 > capital-approach 4 (+ nearness) > advance guard 3.8 > front-approach 3 (+ nearness) > belief 2 + score > picket 1 + importance; screens closer than half a sight radius share one post; ≤ 2 screens per theater (the task sector's theater, else `TheaterClassifier.FromPosition` of the hold point), ≤ 6 tasks of every kind per alliance. A Screen-posture sector beyond those caps gets no screen (cannot happen with ≤ 2 such sectors per theater).
15. **Directive consequence (plan silent).** The default directive becomes Hold, and both the old `OperationalProbeLedger.EligibleProbeFormation` and `CoordinatedOperationPackageLedger`'s directive gate reject Hold, so the old probe and package layers lose most candidates from this merge until G6 deletes them; this is by design (G1b/G5d probes replace them), not a regression to chase at smoke. A division facing a believed army without support now Delays, which `FormationDirectiveRuntime.ShouldAllowAreaMovement` blocks (#15's area moves; #15 goes in G6). Conversely Screen still allows #15's area moves, so G5d must stand recon formations down from #15 (P10) until G6 (carried in G5d's own section). **Perks (integrator decision, verify r1 F2):** the old fallback `default-screen` had no case in `PerkSelectionPatch.ResolveArmyRole`, so it fell through to the objective's role (River, Siege/CapitalDefense, Maneuver, Raid, General); Hold maps to capital-defence perks. The fallback Hold (`default-hold`, and an attached child of a `default-hold` parent, which inherits that reason) keeps the old path; only an explicit Hold (`army-hold`, `attached-parent-missing`) takes `CapitalDefense`. The mapping moved from the patch into the pure `PerkSelectionScorer.TryDirectiveRole(directive, reason, out role)` (one mapping, harness-tested: `DefaultHoldKeepsObjectivePerkRole`; the patch's call is source-pinned in `ScreenMapsToScoutingPerkRole`); G6's `PerkSelectionPatch` migration keeps it.
16. Mirrored constants: `BeliefExpireDays` (G1) and `PicketHoldRatio` (G3) are G1b-internal copies; G7 points them at `EnemyArmyModel.ExpireDays` and `TheaterPlanner.HoldRatio`.

**Census (new members; orphan-sweep `--include-test-only` 72 → 59).** G1b now reads these G0 test-only members, which leave the list: `ReconKind.Picket/AdvanceGuard`, `ReconTask` and `HoldZ`, `EnemyBelief.LastSeenDay/UncertaintyRadius`, `TheaterPlan.MainSectorId/MainRequiredRatio`, `Assignment.ReconMissionId` (now written by `Sync`), `CampaignSnapshot.IndexOfKey` (`OnReview` rebinding), `EnemyBeliefQuery`/`StrengthWithin`, `LogisticsGate`/`ClampToReach`, `SectorModel.SectorOfArea/SectorAt/TheaterOf/Centroid`, `ViewerCoverage`/`Covers` (they stay transitively test-only until the runtime step calls `PlanTasks`). New TEST-ONLY rows, each with its reader: `ReconPlanner.PlanTasks` (G1b runtime step), `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` (G4 `ForceAllocator.Allocate`), `ReconMissionLedger.OnReview` and `OnProbeContact` (G1b runtime step), `ReconOrders`, `CavalryOrderFor`, `MayAct` (G5d `ReconExecutorRuntime`). Hidden from orphan-sweep by name collisions with other classes (`Sync`, `Restore`, `UnitKey`, `SectorId`, `State`, `Reason`, `Kind`) or by use within the recon module itself (`Missions`, `MissionId`, `IssuedDay`, `LastContactDay`: no hit in `src` outside the three recon files), but equally runtime-read only: `ReconMissionLedger.Sync` (G1b runtime step), `Restore` (G6 sidecar load), `Missions` (G5d executor, G6 sidecar; in-module `ReconPlanner.Incumbent`), `IsRecon` (G1b runtime `onReconMission`; in-module viewer exclusion), `FormationDirectiveLedger.Build`'s `onReconMission` (G1b runtime step), `ReconMission.MissionId/Kind/HoldX/HoldZ/State/Reason` (G5d moves, orders, `[ReconMission]` row), `UnitKey/SectorId/IssuedDay/LastContactDay` (G6 `ReconMissionDto`; `IssuedDay` is also read in-module by the expiry rule; `LastContactDay` has no other production reader), `ReconState` values (G5d `CavalryOrderFor`, `[ReconMission] state=`). New at verify r1: `PerkSelectionScorer.TryDirectiveRole` (read by `PerkSelectionPatch.ResolveArmyRole`), `FormationDirectiveLedger.DefaultHoldReason`/`IsDefaultHold` (read by the ledger and the scorer).

**Mutation plants** (scratch copy, cwd = the copy, only `ReconPlannerTests` run, each test caught separately; `evidence/g1b/mutation-results.txt`): 38 plants, all killed. The PROVE-MODE three: drop the main-effort screen rule → `MainEffortKeepsItsOwnScreen`; standoff 0 → `ScreenHoldPointKeepsStandoffFromNearestBelief` (+4); `ViewerCoverage.Covers` always true → `ScreenCoversUnwatchedCapitalApproach` (+6). The four tests green on the stubs: alliance gate `return true` → `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`; no `ClampToReach` → `NoScreenWhenNothingInReach`, `ScreenHoldPointClampedToSupplyReach`; `CavalryOrderFor` without the chain guard → `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand`; a `GetLastTransmittedPathPos` code token in the ledger → `ReconNeverReadsDestinations`. Rules: theater cap 3 / total cap 7 → `ScreensCappedPerTheaterAndAlliance`; own post counted as a viewer → `ScreenOwnPostDoesNotWatchItsApproach`; no incumbent → `ScreenStaysWithIncumbent`; recalling re-picked → `RecalledFormationNotRepicked`; strength minimum inclusive / readiness ignored → `BelowMovementMinimaNeverChosen`; player command pickable → `NeverPlayerCommandChainClass`; no cavalry tier → `CavalryMajorityPreferred`; picket reserve check dropped → `PicketNeverTakesFinalReserve`; advance guard without the strength test → `AdvanceGuardOnlyWithSpareStrengthAndNoCloseContact`; probe ignores battle → `NoProbeWhileMainEffortInContact`; no screen-posture tasks → `ScreenPostureSectorGetsAScreen`; recall at 0.25 (halved metric) → `LowSupplyRecallsScreen`; stale belief as contact → `ContactRecallsScreen`; advance-guard standoff contact dropped → `AdvanceGuardRecalledOnCloseBelief`; `MaxMissionDays` 21 → `MissionExpiresAfterTwentyDays`; withdraw clears / probe recalled on contact → `ProbeEscalateClearsAndWithdrawRecalls`; no UnitKey rebind → `MissionsKeyedByUnitKey`; unassigned missions kept → `SyncAssignsMissionIdsAndClearsUnassigned`; orders to pickets / no recall order / `MayAct(PlayerCommand)` → `ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens` / `RecallClearsScoutingOrder` / `PlayerCommandRefusedLive`; directive ignores `onReconMission` / `default-screen` restored / division-vs-army back to Screen → `ScreenDirectiveOnlyForReconMissions` / `DefaultFallbackIsHold` / `DivisionVsArmyIsDelayNotScreen`; runtime wiring `PerkSelectionPatch` Screen → Raid → `ScreenMapsToScoutingPerkRole` (source pin). Round 1 had three survivors, each fixed in the test, not the code: `CavalryMajorityPreferred` (the cavalry group was also the smallest; now larger), `MissionExpiresAfterTwentyDays` (computed its days from the constant; now literal 19/20 plus a pin of 20) and a mis-targeted coverage plant (`Context.Covered`, killed by seven other tests; the plan's plant is `ViewerCoverage.Covers`, round 2).

G1b smoke items (pure half; checked with G7's smoke): `LOG` `[FormationDirective] alliance=<a> summary=…` rows (entries `<unitKey>:<id>:<directive>:<reason>:<x>:<z>`) contain `:Hold:default-hold:` and `:Delay:division-vs-army-no-support:` where they used to show `:Screen:default-screen:` / `:Screen:division-vs-army-no-support:`, and never `:RaidSupport:` or `cavalry-raid-support`; no `:Screen:recon-mission:` entry and no `[FormationDirective] source=PerkSelection … role=Scouting` line until the runtime step passes `onReconMission` (then only for formations on a recon mission); `default-hold` formations log `source=PerkSelection` roles from their objective (`role=General|Maneuver|Siege|River|Raid|CapitalDefense` as before G1b), not `role=CapitalDefense` for all of them; explicit Hold formations (`army-hold`) keep `role=CapitalDefense`.
