### G4 as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g4` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g4`) from `feat/campaign-planner` `51442ac`. This section is the build record (the repository has no `docs/loop/` directory). Commits `278a8fa` (the shared reserves), `760892e` (allocator, home theater prior, runtime step), `695763a` (reserve fix found by a test), `5655429` (the five integration-verify minors F1-F5, below) and the documentation commit. Harness 2724 PASS (2668 + 50 in `ForceAllocatorTests` + 6 in `DailyReviewSchedulerTests`), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (151 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 43 -> 37 (six rows gained a src reader through G4's writes, none added), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new patch, flag or first-fire id; scope `campaign.planner.allocate` (2.0); telemetry event `Allocation` (registered at G0).

Files: new pure `Strategic/Planner/{ForceAllocator, HomeTheaterPrior, ReserveRules}.cs` (test csproj); edited `TheaterPlanner.cs` (`Reserve` calls `ReserveRules`; `Busy` internal; `LandThreatAt`), `LogisticsGate.cs` (`NearestSource`), `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (allocation step, `[Allocation]` row, the unreliable-day run), `DailyReviewScheduler.cs`, `StrategicCoordinator.cs`, `PlayerCommandClassifier.cs`, `RiverLines.cs`, `TerrainLineRuntime.cs`, `EnemyArmyModel.cs` (the five minors); tests `ForceAllocatorTests.cs` (new), `DailyReviewSchedulerTests.cs`, `TheaterPlannerTests.cs` (two pins updated), `TestFixtures.cs` (`BodyOf`, `AllocationSignature`), csproj; `docs/patch-catalog.md` (one row).

**Where the plan was wrong, silent or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. **`Allocate` takes the mission ledger.** The G4 block lacked `ReconMissionLedger missions` (null allowed); G1b as built says "G4 must pass the ledger" (incumbent preference). Signature: `Allocate(plan, s, sectors, playerReliability01, playerNonCompliantDays, homeTheater, reconTasks, missions)`.
2. **G1b's runtime step is not in the base.** `ReviewPlan` has no `PlanTasks`/`Sync`/`OnReview`, no `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance`, no ledger instance. The allocator is complete and tested with recon tasks and a ledger (including `Sync` pairing); the runtime passes `NoReconTasks` and a null ledger behind one `// G1B SEAM` (pinned to occur once, deleted with G1b's runtime step: `PlanTasks` before the allocation, `Sync` after it, before `Publish`). Until then no formation gets `Recon` in game. (Closed by the G1b runtime step, branch `feat/campaign-planner-g1b-rt`: the seam is gone and its pin is 0.)
3. **`playerNonCompliantDays` has no producer** (stage F's ledger exposes `Reliability` and `Samples` only), and after verify r1 the allocator no longer takes it (item 9). `ForceAllocator.PlayerNonCompliance` is telemetry: it counts consecutive review days with reliability below `UnreliableBelow` (0.5) while the alliance carries a `PlayerMapGroup` formation (one count per day; a day that goes backwards, a load, restarts it); the runtime keeps one per alliance (CampaignScope). In memory (not read by any patch, so it does not join `PrepareDay`): **every `CampaignScope` reset restarts it, a return from a campaign battle included**; G6 adds it to the sidecar (`PlannerStateDto.unreliableDays`, below).
4. **"CIC player -> empty allocation" cannot key on `CampaignSnapshot.Player`**, which is global (the enemy's snapshot carries `CommanderInChief` too). It keys on stage F's `CicSide` chain class of the alliance's own formations; the enemy side of a CIC career keeps its allocation (`CicPlayerYieldsEmptyAllocation` asserts both).
5. **MainEffort is exactly the planned mass; Reserve is the part above 1.3 x the required force.** The plan lists both. Reserve is carved from the mass only when the plan's `MainRequiredRatio` x `TheaterPlan.MainEnemyStrength` (new G3 output, verify r1 F2: the believed land enemy G3 judged the odds against at the main objective itself, confidence- and river-weighted, not the enemy near the clamped gather point in `SectorPlan.TargetX/Z`) leaves the rest at or above `ReserveSurplusMargin` (1.3) x that; farthest from the target first, stopping at the first formation the main effort cannot spare (a skipping greedy took the 8000 corps standing at the objective: found by `HoldReleasedIntoTheMassMasses`); none when no enemy is believed there. Without surplus MainEffort equals the mass; Recon and DepotBuilder picks, and the Reserve, are the only subtractions.
6. **Hold is "formations already there", never "then the nearest".** G5a's mover does not move Hold, and G3 reserves per sector from its own formations; the allocator uses the same picks (`ReserveRules.Capital`/`Hold`, extracted from `TheaterPlanner.Reserve`, behaviour-identical: 2668 PASS before and after) so the mass the odds were judged on and the roles handed out cannot diverge; the hold reserve a target's own threat releases (`HoldThreatAtTarget`) is in the mass and is MainEffort (`HoldReleasedIntoTheMassMasses`). `SectorPlan.HoldNeed` is read as published; the ordering point is the nearest live land belief to the sector centroid anywhere (G3's `NearestLandBelief`, unbounded: an ordering point, not a threat sum).
7. **`MinGarrisonStrength` is a withholding floor, not a pull from the mass.** `max(HoldNeed, 5000)` sets how much Hold strength is withheld in a sector (shortest prefix by distance to its army, immobile formations at their weight counted first and never withheld, the player map group at its discounted weight); an unmet floor is a Shortfall, never a reason to take a formation out of the mass.
8. **Recon picks.** `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` runs per task in the order given, with `mainEffortHasScreen` set once a Screen task of the main sector is placed. G3's "outside the mass while one qualifies" is applied within a ranking tier (cavalry-majority, other combat groups, map-group player): a formation outside the mass replaces the pick only when its tier is not worse, and the incumbent of an active mission keeps its post, so an infantry corps does not displace a cavalry screen to spare the mass (`ReconPrefersCavalryFormations`, `ReconPrefersFormationsOutsideTheMassWithinATier`).
9. **Player classes (revised at verify r1 and again 2026-10-15; see the decision record below).** `PlayerCommand` and `CicSide` formations are immobile Hold (reason `player-command`) and count nothing; `CarryingGroup` is an ordinary movable formation; a `PlayerMapGroup` formation is order-only and **counts toward the hold of the sector where its last waypoint lies, at strength × morale** (`WlChainRules.CountsTowardCoverage`, `ReserveRules.HoldSectorOf`/`HoldStrength`): `ReserveRules.Hold` adds it to what holds a sector anyway, `TheaterPlanner`'s sector facts (posture Hold/Screen) and capital-threat own sum count it (range at the waypoint), and the allocator's Counted, withheld prefix (it counts first and is never withheld, like an immobile formation) and shortfall sums use the same measure. So a player standing in a threatened sector releases the AI corps that would hold it into the mass (`PlayerStandingInASectorReleasesItsAIHolderIntoTheMass`, through the real planner); a player marching away leaves the sector to an AI holder the same day (`PlayerMarchingAwayLeavesTheSectorToAnAIHolder`: corps 4 keeps the hold, a second corps of the sector is added, and the sector reports its shortfall at the AI holder if still short); a sector he alone stands in is Hold while he covers the need and Screen with a shortfall at his formation (a position marker) when he is short (`SectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerIsHeldUntilHeIsShort`). He is never mass, capital guard, Recover (`StarvedMapGroupPlayerIsNeverRecover`), depot builder or reserve, never a surplus, never marked Withheld, and never given a must-hold role (`PlayerIsNeverInTheMassOrAMustHold`, `MapGroupPlayerNeverSoleMustHold`). He may receive his sector's role as an order: Support toward the main target in a Support or main sector (dispatch-only), otherwise Hold in place (`MapGroupPlayerMayReceiveASupportOrderButIsNeverCounted` keeps its name for the Support order; the player in a Support sector is not counted in the allocator's Hold sums because he holds nothing there). The reliability weighting of the first build is gone: `Allocate` has no `playerReliability01` or `playerNonCompliantDays` parameter, and the reliability and the run of unreliable days only appear in the `[Allocation]` row.
10. **Shortfall and surplus semantics (the plan left them open).** A Shortfall is the formation that carries an unmet need, worst deficit ratio first, AI formations only: the main effort short of `MainRequiredRatio` x believed enemy (the main-effort formation nearest the target), the capital guards short of the floor (the nearest guard), a sector's Hold below `max(HoldNeed, 5000)` (its AI Hold formation nearest the enemy; with none, and a hold need, the map-group player's formation there as the position marker). A Surplus is a Reserve formation or a Hold formation released beyond the garrison in a sector with no hold need, largest first; never MainEffort, CapitalGuard or a player formation.
11. **`HomeTheaterPrior`** reduces `HistoricalArmyAreaRegistry`'s doctrines to `(alliance, army names) -> Theater` through `ArmyAreaClassifier` and `TheaterClassifier` (Virginia capital corridor, Shenandoah, Washington and Maryland-Pennsylvania are East; the Ohio valley and north-west Virginia are West); pinned against the registry until G6 deletes it. It is a 5 % distance discount in the Hold ordering, a tie-break only.
12. **Recover is per formation** (raw supply below 0.4 or morale below the movement minimum), also outside the mass; the walk target is the nearest valid own supply source whatever the distance (`LogisticsGate.NearestSource`). **A Recover formation counts 0 toward its sector's hold** (verify r2 N2, integrator decision 2026-10-15): `ReserveRules.Recovers` is the one predicate behind both the Recover assignment and `ReserveRules.Hold`'s "holds anyway" sum, and the allocator's Hold sums only ever add Hold-role formations, so a sector is never "held" by corps walking to supply; its healthy corps stay Hold and withheld, and the sector reports a shortfall if they are too weak (`RecoveringFormationsDoNotHoldTheirSector`, through the real planner, the verifier's seed-6 shape; `ReserveRulesHoldIgnoresRecoveringFormations`, a busy formation of the same strength, starved or not, still counts; `StarvedMapGroupPlayerIsNeverRecover`: the player is never Recover). Eight N2 plants, all killed (`evidence/g4/mutation-results-n2.txt`; plant 5, the dropped `MayMove` guard, first survived and `StarvedMapGroupPlayerIsNeverRecover` was added). G3's sector-fact `Own` sum (posture Hold/Screen) still counts committed formations including a recovering one: unchanged by this decision.
13. **`[Allocation]` row** gains `unreliableDays` (telemetry), `mass`, `short` and `surplus`: `[Allocation] alliance=<a> main=<n> support=<n> hold=<n> reserve=<n> capital=<n> depot=<n> recover=<n> recon=<n> busy=<n> player=<class> reliability=<r> unreliableDays=<n> mass=<strength> short=<n> surplus=<n>`; `hold` excludes the immobile formations, which `busy` counts.
14. **Order inside the Plan job.** The allocation runs after `TheaterPlanner.Plan` and before `CampaignPlanStore.Publish(alliance, plan, allocation, sectors)`: the allocation is built fresh each review and never edited after `Publish`, so it joins the atomic daily publish (the working copy that `PrepareDay` copies) with no new copy code (`AllocationPublishesAtomicallyWithThePlan`). `SlicedMatchesUnsliced` now runs the real `ReconPlanner.PlanTasks`, `ForceAllocator.Allocate` and `ReconMissionLedger.Sync` (ledger carried across days) in its Plan job; the comparison covers the allocator's dependence on the job order, not hidden statics (pinned separately by `AllocatorIsPureAndFogHonestBySource`).

**Integration-verify minors F1-F5 (coordinator request; commit `5655429`).** F1: `ReviewPlan`/`ReviewExecute` take the pause `BeginReviewDay` captured (`d.JobMachinePaused`) through a new `PlayerCommandClassifier.ReviewMode(..., bool jobMachinePaused)`; the executor also keeps the live re-check. The scheduler model has a pause input that flips after the third frame of every day: the sliced days equal the unsliced ones, and a control reading the live value per job differs (`PlannerFollowsTheDaysPauseAcrossAMidDayFlip`). F2: the runtime header names the Plan and Execute jobs. F3: `TerrainMemoDrain.NoteScenario`/`DropForScenario`; `TerrainLineRuntime.Reset` (the CampaignScope reset, i.e. a load) drops queue and memo when the scenario changed; the same scenario (a save of the campaign, the return from a battle) keeps them; `TerrainProbeMirrorsVanillaEngagementLine`'s reset pin allows exactly that one guarded drop. F4 (the in-frame order of `SaveAll`'s call sites 36702, 66948 and 216812 against `AICampaign.Update` is not established; Unity gives no order between a UI or timer callback and a script's `Update`, so the overlap is possible and is made impossible by construction rather than proven to occur): `DailyReviewSaveDrainPatch` (Prefix on `SceneManagement.SaveAll`) can finish the review earlier in a frame than the `AICampaign.Update` Postfix that pumps, which would run the 0.9 ms drain on the save frame; `ReviewFrameGate` (a no-op when they never share a frame) marks every frame that ran a review slice (`RunReviewSlice`, which every drain also goes through) and `PumpDailyReview` drains only on an unmarked frame; the coordinator's pump is the drain's only caller (pinned). F5: the belief comment says `EnemyArmyModel.Restore` has no caller until G6.

Red-first (`evidence/g4/red-first.txt`): the first 47 tests were run against an API-only stub (`Allocate` returning an empty allocation, `HomeTheaterPrior.For` Unknown, the counter a no-op) with a catching `RunAll`: 40 red; the 7 green on the stub are negative-shaped or already true (`CapitalGuardsAreNeverInThePlannedMass`, `AllocationDeterministic`, `PlayerFormationNeverShortfallTarget`, `AllianceTwoAndEmptyInputsYieldEmptyAllocation`, `NearestSourceIgnoresRangeAndInvalidSources`, `ReserveRulesAreSharedWithThePlanner`, `AllocatorIsPureAndFogHonestBySource`) and each is killed by a plant below. The implementation was drafted before the test file (the process slip: the stub run is the red-first evidence, as at G1 and G3). `HoldReleasedIntoTheMassMasses` was red against the first implementation (a real bug, fixed in `695763a`). The tests for `AllocationStepPinned` and `AllocationPublishesAtomicallyWithThePlan`, and for F1-F5, were written after their code; their strength is the plants.

Mutation plants (scratch copy as working directory, catching `RunAll`, one at a time, restore from a byte copy with a checksum check; `evidence/g4/mutation-results.txt` and `mutation-results-minors.txt`): **55 plants, all killed by the named test.** The allocator: capital guards never assigned; MainEffort takes every free formation; hold need recomputed from beliefs; no minimum garrison; reserve margin 0.5; reserve nearest first; reserve skips instead of stopping; Recover on the halved supply (survived once because the test used only formations the mass counts; a far-formation case now kills it); player strength undiscounted; threshold off by one; immobile strength not counted in the prefix; home tie-break dropped; CIC stand-down never / for the enemy side too; Recon never spares the mass / ignores the tier; main-effort screen assumed present / never noted; recon hold point or sector altered; recon mission id left at the struct default; a player formation a shortfall carrier; MainEffort listed as surplus; shortfalls best first; depot builder prefers the mass / is the largest; the counter counts a day twice; home theater of the north-west army; nearest source accepts any owner; alliance bound dropped; a clock read in the allocator; the allocator ignoring the capital reserve (kills `ReserveRulesAreSharedWithThePlanner` and `CapitalGuardFilledFirstUpToFloor`); the capital reserve unordered; a hold-reserved formation the mass released kept as Hold. Runtime wiring (source pins): an empty allocation published; reliability constant; the unreliable-day count dropped; home theaters dropped; the run counted for any formation (survived once, now pinned); the run never reset. Minors: the captured pause dropped from either job, the plan step reading the live class, the executor's live re-check or its capture dropped, the overload ignoring the pause, the gate always open, the pump without the gate, a slice not marking its frame, the drop never / always, the reset not dropping, the scenario never noted, the header and the comment restored.

Census. New members are read in src: `ForceAllocator` (`Allocate`, `Summary`, `PlayerNonCompliance`, the constants and reasons), `HomeTheaterPrior.For`/`ForAlliance`, `ReserveRules.*`, `LogisticsGate.NearestSource`, `TheaterPlanner.LandThreatAt`, `ReviewFrameGate`, `TerrainMemoDrain.NoteScenario`/`DropForScenario`, `PlayerCommandClassifier.ReviewMode(..., bool)`. Six G0/G1b rows now have a src reference (the write side): `FormationRole.CapitalGuard`, `FormationRole.DepotBuilder`, `Assignment.Withheld`, `Allocation.Shortfalls`, `Allocation.Surpluses`, `ReconPlanner.PickFormation`. Their readers (`Withheld`: G5a `CampaignWithholdView`; `Shortfalls`/`Surpluses`: G5b `ReinforcementRouting`; `CapitalGuard`: G5a mover, G5b `CapitalFloorPolicy`; `DepotBuilder`: G5c) are G5's; `orphan-sweep` is name-based, so they leave the test-only list although nothing reads them yet. Remaining test-only rows are unchanged (37, `evidence/g4/testonly-g4b.txt`).

**Verify r1 revision (2026-10-15; integrator decisions on F1, F2, M1-M5; verdict `scratchpad/loop/stageG/verdicts/verify-g4.md`).**
- **F1 (structural; superseded by the 2026-10-15 decision record below).** The map-group player counts 0 in every hold, reserve and defender sum, whatever his reliability (spec C4 "never a committed defender"): `ReserveRules.Hold`, `TheaterPlanner`'s sector facts (so a sector only he stands in is Screen) and capital-threat own sum, and the allocator. The reliability weighting, `Allocate`'s `playerReliability01`/`playerNonCompliantDays` parameters and `NonComplianceDays` are deleted; the reliability and the unreliable-day run stay in the `[Allocation]` row as telemetry. A sector he alone stands in reports a shortfall at his formation (position marker, only when it has a hold need). Item 9 and plan:752 are rewritten above. Tests through the real planner: `PlayerPresenceNeverReleasesTheSectorsHolders` (corps 4 stays Hold and withheld with him there), `SectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerReportsAShortfall`, `PlannerDoesNotCountThePlayersFormationAsHolding`; the hand-built-plan tests of the old discount were replaced by `MapGroupPlayerNeverCountsInAHoldSum`.
- **F2.** G3 publishes `TheaterPlan.MainEnemyStrength` (the confidence- and river-weighted believed land enemy within the relocation range of the main objective, the odds G3 judged) and the allocator's reserve split reads it instead of the enemy near the clamped gather point; `TheaterPlanner.LandThreatAt` (the straight-distance wrapper) is gone. `ReserveMeasuredAtTheMainObjectiveNotTheFrontier` (an out-of-reach objective, 20000 at the objective and 3000 near the frontier: no reserve, no surplus).
- **M1.** The record and the allocator header now say the map-group player may receive Support as an order (never counted); `MapGroupPlayerMayReceiveASupportOrderButIsNeverCounted`. **M2.** The unreliable-day run restarts at every `CampaignScope` reset (a return from a battle included); G6's sidecar list gains `unreliableDays`. **M3.** `ReserveKeepsTheMarginAboveRequired` (14000 left: kept at 1.3, carved at 1.0). **M4.** `ReserveRulesHoldCountsCommittedDefendersOnly` pins the held sum (busy and capital-reserved count in full, the player's group and own command count 0); `HoldSectorKeepsHoldRatio` asserts a hold reserve's target is its own position. **M5.** A formation that counts 0 is never marked Withheld. **M6.** The evidence files are outside the repository by design: scratchpad/evidence/g4/ (`red-first.txt`, `red-first-raw.txt`, `mutation-results.txt`, `mutation-results-minors.txt`, `mutation-results-r2.txt`, `final-harness.txt`, `r2-harness.txt`, the gate outputs).
- **Ledger note (smoke item 1):** `main` + `reserve` + the depot and recon picks taken from the mass equal the mass count.
**Decision record: the map-group player counts by position (integrator, 2026-10-15; supersedes F1 above).** The user delegated the call ("You tell me?", 2026-10-15) and the integrator decided: the W&L map-group player's group counts at full strength (strength × morale, as vanilla) toward the hold of the sector where it actually is or where its last waypoint lies, mirroring vanilla's capital-defense count (`AICampaign.AssignUnitToDefendCapital` 11733-11740: every own unit whose `GetLastWaypointPos` is nearer the capital than the nearest enemy subtracts `groupstrengthactive × groupmorale`, the player's group included). It is never part of the mass (the AI cannot move it), never pre-pledged and never given a must-hold role (`WlChainRules.MayMove` and `MayMove` stay false for it); its reliability stays telemetry only; if the player marches away, the next review sees the gap. Recover formations still count 0 (verify r2 N2). Built as `WlChainRules.CountsTowardCoverage` (committed defenders plus `PlayerMapGroup`; the player's own command and a CIC side stay out), `ReserveRules.HoldPoint`/`HoldSectorOf`/`HoldStrength` (the hold point is the last waypoint, falling back to the position; the strength is × morale for the player only, the AI's sums are unchanged), and the three consumers: `ReserveRules.Hold`, `TheaterPlanner` (sector facts `Own`, capital threat `own`) and `ForceAllocator` (`_sector` of the player is his hold sector; `Counts(i)` replaces the 0/1 weight; the withheld prefix counts him first and never withholds him). Not changed by this: `ReconPlanner`'s picket reserve and main-body sums still count committed defenders only (the recon planner is conservative about stripping a sector), and the main-side defense code (`DefenseIntentRuntime`, `DefensiveOpsPatch`, which keep asking `MayMove` for who may be pledged) gets the same rule in a separate fix. Tests: `MapGroupPlayerCountsInAHoldSumAtStrengthTimesMorale`, `PlayerStandingInASectorReleasesItsAIHolderIntoTheMass`, `PlayerMarchingAwayLeavesTheSectorToAnAIHolder`, `PlayerIsNeverInTheMassOrAMustHold`, `SectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerIsHeldUntilHeIsShort`, `PlannerCountsThePlayersGroupWhereItsWaypointLies`, `ReserveRulesHoldCountsTheMapGroupPlayerNotHisCommand` (they replace the F1 tests `MapGroupPlayerNeverCountsInAHoldSum`, `PlayerPresenceNeverReleasesTheSectorsHolders`, `SectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerReportsAShortfall`, `PlannerDoesNotCountThePlayersFormationAsHolding`, `ReserveRulesHoldCountsCommittedDefendersOnly`). Sixteen plants for the rule (the chain predicate widened and narrowed, morale ignored, the hold point at the position, the sector facts, the capital range and the capital sum, `ReserveRules.Hold` narrowed and at full strength, the player withheld and ignored in the prefix, the allocator's count without morale or for the own command, the player in his physical sector, no position marker), all killed (`evidence/g4/mutation-results-pos.txt`); the first run of the set read as survivors only because a test file did not compile, and was rerun. **Name unification at the main merge (d08c8e4):** main landed the same rule on the defense side as `WlChainRules.CountsTowardCoverage` (None, CarryingGroup, PlayerMapGroup) and split the old `CountsAsCommittedDefender` into `MayMove` (the same set as None, CarryingGroup) and `CountsTowardCoverage`. The planner's `CountsTowardHold` was exactly the set `CountsTowardCoverage`, so it was deleted and every planner reader asks `CountsTowardCoverage`; every planner use of `CountsAsCommittedDefender` is now `MayMove`. One predicate each, no alias.
**Verify r3 revision (2026-10-15; integrator decisions on F1-F6 of `verify-g4r3`, covered commit `1eb3f83`).** F1: `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` requires `WlChainRules.MayMove` (see G1b item 13), `ReconTier` and the picket sum lose their player cases; `MapGroupPlayerIsNeverPickedAsAPicket` (real `PlanTasks`: town at x 400, player alone at x 450, enemy at x 250/300/620/650, 2000 or 20000), and `MapGroupPlayerNeverSoleScreen`/`PicketReserveIgnoresMapGroupPlayer` now expect no pick. F2: `BuildSectorFacts` keys every fact (`Own` and `Count`, with `Supply`/`Morale`/`Strongest`) on `HoldSectorOf`, so a player bound for an empty threatened sector makes it Hold with no screen task (`PlayerBoundForAnEmptyThreatenedSectorMakesItHold`: at x 310, waypoint x 500). F6: the same single lookup credits a player whose position is off the sector map by his waypoint sector (`HoldSectorOfCreditsTheWaypointWhateverThePosition`, `SectorFactsCreditTheHoldSectorBeforeAnyPhysicalCheck`). F3: G5b routes to the hold point (above). F4: `CicStandDownPrecedesEveryHoldSum` pins the stand-down return before the sector model, the plan and the allocation, and `CountsTowardCoverage(CicSide) == false`. F5: the stale comments in `TheaterPlanner` and `ForceAllocator` are fixed.
**Verify r4 revision (2026-10-15; G4 verify r4 V1, V2, V4, landed at `8f89f86`, code and tests only).** V1: the mixed-sector picket tests `MixedSectorPicketNeverTakesTheMapGroupPlayer` (`ReconPlannerTests.cs:830`) and `MixedSectorRealPlannerNeverPicketsThePlayer` (`ForceAllocatorTests.cs:958`, real planner) show the `WlChainRules.MayMove` guard blocking the map-group player in a sector he shares with orderable formations. V2 (integrator decision): a sector's Recover posture is judged only on the formations the planner can order: `SectorFacts.Orderable` (`TheaterPlanner.cs:130`) counts the `WlChainRules.MayMove` formations of each sector, `Supply` and `Morale` are summed over those only, and Recover needs `Orderable > 0`; the map-group player's and the player's own command's strength still count toward `Own` and `Count` by position (hold sector), so the player's supply or morale can never send a sector to Recover, while his strength still holds it. Test `StarvingPlayerAloneInAThreatenedSectorIsNotRecover` (`ForceAllocatorTests.cs:991`). V4: the comments in `ReserveRules` and `TheaterPlanner` that spoke of a player "off the sector map" now name the real fallback: his hold point falls back to his position when his waypoint is not a usable point (NaN).
**Verify r5 landing items (2026-10-15; W1, W2 of `verify-g4r5`, tests only).** W1: `StarvingAICorpsBesideAHealthyMapGroupPlayerStillMakesTheSectorRecover` (an AI corps at supply 0.3, or morale 0.2, beside a healthy map-group player still makes the sector Recover; summing every formation's supply or morale while dividing by `Orderable` reads above the floor and misses it: the verifier's Q5 plant and a morale-only variant, both killed). W2: `ScreenSectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerGetsAnAIScreen` restores the assertion that a Screen sector held only by a starving or healthy map-group player keeps its `screen-posture` task and that an AI formation (corps 1), never the player, takes it; the fixture needs an own IIP at x 560 within command range 40 of the post (x 576). **Correction to the r4 note:** the lone-formation screen task that did not appear in the r4 fixture was not dropped by the standoff or anchor rules; `AddScreenPostureTasks` sends the post through `LogisticsGate.ClampToReach`, and with the nearest own IIP at x 500, beyond command range 40, no point on the axis is in reach, so G1b makes no task ("no task when no such point remains"). Plants for W2: `PickFormation` admitting the player for a screen is killed (the healthy-player case is what shows the `MayMove` guard, since a starving formation fails `CanMove` anyway). Evidence: `evidence/g4/mutation-results-r5.txt`.

Total 70.

## G5a — Mover and steering executors (after G4 merges and after `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` merges)

**Depends on `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs`** (in progress on `main`): it fixes the zone supply-capacity freeze — `AIArea.iips/cbuildings/towns` are only filled while `airuns` ≤ 1 (14927, 14946, 14965), so depots and IIPs built later never count in `supplycapacity` (10772-10802) — and the importance-pointer redirect relies on vanilla's `ownpoints <= supplycapacity` gate (10983). G5a builds on that fix and does not re-fix it.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/MoveTracker.cs` (pure: issued/confirmed/failed/retry/give-up), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (pure view over the published allocation and the move tracker, read by the availability Postfixes), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignMoverRuntime.cs` (runtime), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSteeringRuntime.cs` (runtime: objective write, `theaterposition` writer), `Patches/CampaignAvailabilityFilterPatch.cs` (new; two classes); rewrite `Patches/ImportanceValuesPatch.cs` (#2); modify `Patches/PickCampaignObjectivePatch.cs` (transitional only: the executor-flag stand-down at the top of `Prefix`; its `SnapshotWrittenByMod`/`RestoreWrittenByMod` statics stay and the objective executor writes the mod-written value through them, so the coordinator's sidecar calls at 1911/1957 keep persisting it until G6 moves the field); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (executor step); create `Strategic/Planner/CampaignTempoGate.cs` (pure: vanilla's tempo roll, moved here, and the first-move gate; decision 5) and `Strategic/Planner/PlannerChapterRules.cs` (pure: the pre-war gate; decision 8); modify `Strategic/CoordinatedOperationPackageLedger.cs` (delete `CoordinatedOffensiveVanillaGates.TempoProbability`), `Patches/CoordinatedOffensiveOperationsPatch.cs` (`:690`, call `CampaignTempoGate.TempoProbability` until G6 deletes #38), `Strategic/CampaignPaceLedger.cs` (rule 4 only), `Strategic/Planner/PlannerState.cs` (`TempoReleasedDecision`, `LastFullReviewDay`, and their `Restore` parameters), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`FormationRecord.TimeOfNotMoving` only, the struct at `:51`), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (the own-formation read only, `s.Own.Add(new FormationRecord` at `:277`; G5a starts after G3c, which edits `ReadObjectives` in the same file), `Strategic/Planner/TheaterPlanner.cs` (the `Busy` mask only, `:93-94`: adds `FormationFlags.InDefensiveOps`); tests `CampaignExecutorTests.cs`, `CampaignTempoGateTests.cs` (the five `TempoProbability` cases of `CampaignMilitaryFixTests.cs:386-397` move there).

**Vanilla to read:** `MoveUnitTo` 14479-14540 and the queue 9933, 9965-10052, 79295; `CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` 25608; `IsTerrainPositionRestricted` 25755; `unitsinoffensiveoperations` handling 14383-14385, `UpdateMicroMovementInOffensive` 13968-14061, `AdjustOffensiveOperationsSpeed` (11350), `CheckStanceSetting` 11893-11930; `CheckForEndOfDefensiveOperations` 13782-13848 (why never the defensive list); `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` 14080-14158 (Postfix target, public static); `IsUnitAvailableForDefensiveOperation` 13460 (private static, Postfix by name with the full parameter list); `CalculateMostValueableAIZones` 10964-11009 and the readers 14206-14228; `PickCampaignObjective` 17769-17789 and 14922/14961/12337; `UpdateCampaignTheaters` 17034-17110 (`theaterposition`, box 17090-17110); W&L: 8251-8275, 8366, 14381-14456 (order-instead-of-move); stage F as merged (`9aa71cf`; names unchanged since `c10aca3`): `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue(WlStrategicOrderRequest)` / `WlOrderTypeForIntent`, `WlCampaignDispatchRuntime.OnMoveUnitTo`/`Flush` (#69, the `MoveUnitTo` Postfix and its `UpdateUnitAI` flush), `WlCampaignDispatchPolicy.ClassifyType`, `WlMoveSource`.

**Interfaces — Consumes:** G0 store/snapshot/`SectorModel`, G4 `Allocation` (incl. `Assignment.Withheld`), G3 `TheaterPlan`; stage F (as merged at `9aa71cf`): `PlayerComplianceRuntime.Reliability` (→ `PlayerComplianceLedger.Reliability`), `WlChainRules.MayMove/OrderOnly`, and for a `PlayerMapGroup` formation `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue(new WlStrategicOrderRequest { AllianceId, Unit, TargetPosition, TargetName, Intent, SourceSystem = "campaign-planner" })`, which maps the intent to vanilla's order type (`WlOrderTypeForIntent`: `Offensive` 16, `Redeploy` 5, `DefendCapital` 8, `ConstructSupplyDepot` 10) and emits through `WlCampaignDispatchRuntime.EmitForPlayerCommand(Regiment, int, Vector3, string, float, float)` with F's dedupe. **Planner moves and F's typing:** the planner calls `MoveUnitTo` inside the daily review, outside `UpdateUnitAI`, where F's move source is `WlMoveSource.Other`; F's #69 Postfix records a `CarryingGroup` move and its next `UpdateUnitAI` flush types it by list membership (`ClassifyType`: order type 16 or 6 when listed in the offensive list, else order type 5), exactly as it types vanilla's own moves of that group. G5a therefore does **not** call `EnterMoveSource` (that hook names the vanilla callers list membership cannot classify, `RollUp` and `FinalPositions`) and adds no `WlMoveSource` value; it lists MainEffort/Support units in the offensive list right after `MoveUnitTo` returns `true`, before the flush.
**Produces:**
```csharp
public enum MoveOutcome { None = 0, Issued = 1, Confirmed = 2, Failed = 3, Retried = 4, GaveUp = 5, SkippedPlayer = 6, SkippedBusy = 7, Queued = 8, Deferred = 9 }
public sealed class MoveTracker { public const int MaxMovesPerDay = 6; public const float ConfirmFraction = 0.25f;
  public bool ShouldIssue(int instanceId, float tx, float tz, int daySerial);
  public void RecordIssued(int instanceId, float fromX, float fromZ, float tx, float tz, int daySerial, bool queued);
  public MoveOutcome Judge(int instanceId, float nowX, float nowZ, float waypointX, float waypointZ, int daySerial);
  public bool InTransit(int instanceId); /* issued or queued and not yet judged */
  public System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<MoveRecord> Records { get; } public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<MoveRecord> records); }
public struct MoveRecord { public int InstanceId; /* in-session */ public string UnitKey; /* persisted */ public float TargetX, TargetZ; public int IssuedDay; public int Retries; }
public static class CampaignWithholdView { // read by the availability Postfixes; G5c adds the depot-task hold, G8 the garrison role
  public static bool WithheldFromOffense(int alliance, int instanceId);                      // Assignment.Withheld || InTransit || a MainEffort member of the plan's mass || a Support formation (not the map-group player) while TempoReleasedDecision != DecisionSerial (decision 5: only the mover launches the mass, and no Support goes ahead of it)
  public static bool WithheldFromDefense(int alliance, int instanceId, float enemyX, float enemyZ); // Assignment.Withheld || InTransit, never the mass or deferred-Support clause (both stay available to vanilla's defense); a Hold-at-need formation whose sector (SectorModel.SectorAt) contains the enemy position stays available
}
```

**Executors (inside the review, executor flag on):**
- **Objective (spec C6):** write `aifaction[f].followedcampaignobjective = plan.MainObjectiveId` when it differs and the objective is available; with no main effort, reset a mod-written value to −1 so vanilla re-picks (17780-17786). Records the mod-written value through `PickCampaignObjectivePatch`'s existing written-by-mod statics (still persisted by the coordinator at 1911/1957); G6 moves that state into the executor and deletes the patch.
- **Mover:** for each MainEffort/Support/CapitalGuard/Recover/DepotBuilder assignment whose formation is not where its role needs it: if its `Chain` fails `WlChainRules.MayMove`, the W&L rule below applies instead; otherwise validate the target (`CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` semantics; skip a restricted target), then `AICampaign.MoveUnitTo(unit, target, movearoundenemyunits: true)`; on `true` add MainEffort/Support units to `unitsinoffensiveoperations` (never `unitsindefensiveoperations`); `false` with a running order → `Queued`, retried next review. Judged next review (P8). W&L: a `PlayerCommand` chain class is never moved (`SkippedPlayer`); a `PlayerMapGroup` formation (`WlChainRules.OrderOnly`) gets `WlStrategicOrderBridge.TryIssue` with intent `Redeploy` for his Support order (he is never MainEffort, never CapitalGuard and never DepotBuilder, G4 item 9; deduped by F); a `CarryingGroup` is moved normally and F's `MoveUnitTo` Postfix dispatches. Telemetry `[CampaignMover] alliance=<a> issued=<n> confirmed=<n> failed=<n> retried=<n> gaveUp=<n> queued=<n> skippedPlayer=<n>` once per review when any count > 0.
- **W&L player side transport modes (G0 landing B):** vanilla gives `userails = userivers = true` only to units of non-player alliances (`alliance != playeralliance`, 112862-112866); the player's alliance, including its AI-owned groups under `ai_vs_ai`, keeps stored values (field default false, 111466-111470; scenario/save data 74178-74180, 71375-71378). Before `MoveUnitTo` for a movable formation of the W&L player's alliance (`Chain` `None` or `CarryingGroup`) whose snapshot `UseRails`/`UseRivers` is false, the mover sets vanilla's AI default (`userails = userivers = true`); never on `PlayerCommand` or `PlayerMapGroup`. Tests `WlSideMovableGetsVanillaAiTransportDefault`, `PlayerCommandTransportFlagsUntouched`. Smoke: G0's first-review line for the player's alliance in a career shows `useRails=`/`useRivers=` (the real stored values); after G5a the same formations move along rivers and rails.
- **Theater box (spec C8):** for a MainEffort/Support formation already inside its sector, set `theaterposition` to the point toward its target that keeps the formation inside the box (`|unit − center| < theatersize` on both axes); for Hold formations, center on the formation. At most twice per formation per week.
- **Stale plans:** every planner patch and `CampaignWithholdView` ignore a published plan/allocation whose `DaySerial` is more than 2 review days older than today's (a paused alliance or a failing review never steers on stale state); test `StalePlanIgnoredByPatches`.
- **Importance pointers (#2 rewrite, spec C7):** Postfix on `AIArea.CalculateMostValueableAIZones(int aifaction)`: if `this` area lies in a MainEffort or Support sector of that faction's alliance (0/1, bound-checked), point **both** `mostvalueableaiareaclose[f]` and `mostvaluealbeaiareaclosedistanceonly[f]` at the area containing the sector target, only when that area passes vanilla's own gates from `this` (the distance and `ownpoints <= supplycapacity` checks of 10984; reuse `CampaignMilitaryGates.ShouldRedirectToPlanTarget`, re-keyed on the plan). Reads `CampaignPlanStore` only, and steers only once the decision is released (as-built item 12). First-fire id kept: `campaign.patch.importance-values`.
- **Recover = no overextension (finding: vanilla never checks supply before or during an offensive).** `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations` (14080-14158) has no supply term and job 6's supply gate is dead (`flag` is always true, 14009-14019); job 6 re-targets listed units on morale/readiness only (14023-14030). So a Recover formation is (a) withheld by the availability Postfixes from new operations and (b) removed by the executor from `unitsinoffensiveoperations`, as vanilla's `RelieveUnitsInOperations` (14068-14078) removes retreating/low-morale units (a formation in a vanilla defensive operation is `Busy` from G5a on, so it is kept Hold before it is judged for Recover, `ForceAllocator.cs:306` before `:336`; vanilla ends or relieves its own defence, 13782-13848 and 14068-14078, and the formation is judged for Recover at the next review after), then moved by the mover toward its nearest supply source (`LogisticsGate`). Do not revive the dead vanilla gate (its 0.75 threshold is unreachable on the halved metric).
- **W&L per-unit decision:** the "may Whiskey move this group, or must it be an order" decision is the snapshot's `Chain` (F's `ChainClassOf`, G0) read through `WlChainRules.MayMove`/`OrderOnly`; `CarryingGroup → MayMove` is F's lifting of the old refusal for the group above a player below map level. G5a adds no classification of its own. Spec C4 stands.
- **Availability (new `CampaignAvailabilityFilterPatch`):** Postfix on `IsUnitAvailableForOffensiveOperations(int, Regiment, Vector3, bool, bool)` (14080, public static): `__result = false` when `CampaignWithholdView.WithheldFromOffense(alliance of the faction, unit)` (Reserve, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon, Recover, in-transit, Hold at sector need, the MainEffort members of the plan's mass, and the Support formations of a deferred decision except the map-group player: chapter tempo gate below). Postfix on `IsUnitAvailableForDefensiveOperation(int _aifaction, Regiment unit, Vector3 enemyposition)` (13460, private static, targeted by name with that parameter list): `__result = false` when `CampaignWithholdView.WithheldFromDefense(alliance, unit, enemyposition)`, which never applies the mass clause (decision 5 withholds the mass from offense only) and keeps a Hold-at-need formation available when `enemyposition` lies in its own sector (vanilla passes `enemyunits[j]`'s position at 13610, its unsorted-list bug, BUG-AI-002; the Postfix judges whatever position vanilla passed). Never sets `true`. First-fire ids `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive`, `campaign.patch.planner-availability.defensive`. Rollback: `Enable Campaign Planner Executors`.
- **Chapter tempo gate (decision 5, "Keep chapter pacing").** The first offensive move of each new main effort (the first `MoveUnitTo` of a MainEffort formation, i.e. of the planned mass) waits for vanilla's chapter tempo roll; later moves of the same decision do not roll again. The roll covers launching the main effort only; a Support formation is not rolled for, but its move into enemy-held ground waits until its decision's main-effort launch is released (no piecemeal attack ahead of the main effort), while its moves in own or neutral ground are never held. The W&L map-group player, who is never MainEffort (G4: never in the mass, never withheld), gets his Support order (`TryIssue`, intent `Redeploy`) by exactly the same rule as an AI Support formation's move. The planner's roll replaces vanilla's for the planned mass and is not added to it (*Vanilla's own offensive*, below).
  - *Equivalence, per lead package and per term only.* Vanilla calls `CheckOffensiveMovements` once per own unit per faction pass (11319-11327, `ownunits[currentoffensiveunitrun]`); each call builds that lead's package and, when the package targets enemy ground, rolls once for it (14335-14350); a launch then closes the cycle (`recruitingzonesupdated = false` at 14458, tested at 14200), so at most one package launches per cycle. The planner rolls once per review for one package, the main effort's mass. What matches is the chapter term `aiagressiveness[chapter] × timediff × 10 × faction × campaign × winter` of one package over the same elapsed time (per day: 001 Union ≈ 0.021 in chapter 1, 0.041 in 2, 0.055 in 3; CSA half). Not claimed: the faction-wide chance that some package launches in a cycle (vanilla's is about 1 − Π(1 − tval_i) over its k eligible leads; the planner launches one main effort per decision), and the idle term `clamp01(idle³)`, which vanilla does not scale by `timediff` (14340), so its weight per day depends on vanilla's pass cadence. The idle term is applied once per roll, as vanilla applies it once per lead roll; the planner rolls once per review.
  - *Pure (`CampaignTempoGate`):* `TempoProbability(chapterAggression, timediff, factionAggression, campaignAggression, winterModifier, idleYears, wlScenarioActive, wlAggressiveness, playerGroupInPackage)` moved verbatim from `CoordinatedOffensiveVanillaGates` (`CoordinatedOperationPackageLedger.cs:394-414` on this branch; formula 14339-14348); `ChapterAggression(float[] aiagressiveness, int chapter)` = `aiagressiveness[chapter]`, 0 for a chapter outside the array (−1 before vanilla's frame-50 update, 211208/29857); `ForcedInvasion(string scenario, bool objective6Accomplished, int alliance, float floatingDate)` = vanilla's 14303-14306 flag (001, Union, objective 6 open, date > 1861.513); `Releases(float roll, float tval, bool forced)` = `forced || roll <= tval`, always called as `Releases(roll, tval, ForcedInvasion(...) || debugPermanentMovement)` with `GameVars.debug_permanentaimovement` (vanilla returns when `num8 > num9 && !debug_permanentaimovement && !flag`, 14350; #38 reads the same flag at `CoordinatedOffensiveOperationsPatch.cs:679`); `TimeDiffYears(int today, int lastFullReviewDay)` (the tempo clock below). `CoordinatedOffensiveVanillaGates.TempoPasses` (`CoordinatedOperationPackageLedger.cs:427`) duplicates `Releases` until G6 deletes its file with #38; G5a does not touch it.
  - *Tempo clock (`timediff`).* `TimeDiffYears(today, PlannerState.LastFullReviewDay)` = `min(today − LastFullReviewDay, ReviewGapDays) / 365.25` with `ReviewGapDays = 1` (one daily review's worth, the cap), and `ReviewGapDays / 365.25` when `LastFullReviewDay < 0` (none recorded). `LastFullReviewDay` is the day serial of this alliance's previous Full review (`PlannerReviewMode.Full`: not paused, not standing down) on which `PlannerChapterRules.ExecutorsAllowed` was true; it is set at the end of every such review's executor step, whether or not a roll happened, and persisted (`PlannerStateDto.lastFullReviewDay`, G6). The cases: (a) the first roll of a new decision uses the gap since the previous Full review, never since the previous release or roll, so a decision chosen 60 days after the last release rolls with one day's worth, not 60; (b) the first review after 001's pre-war idle has no executors-allowed review before it (pre-war reviews do not set the day) → one review's worth; (c) the first review after a W&L job-machine pause or a stand-down → capped at one review's worth (vanilla carries the paused gap, because its `lastupdate`, 11327, is not advanced while its job machine is paused, and would then launch at once; the planner does not); (d) after a reload the restored day gives the capped gap, and a v1/v2 sidecar (no stored day) gives one review's worth; (e) vanilla's own first pass uses `timediff = 0` (`lastupdate <= 0`, 11315-11317), the planner one review's worth, so the chapter term counts from the first review.
  - *Inputs (all own-side or public prefs, fog-honest):* `GamePrefs.aiagressiveness` (5 values, loaded 56244-56248 from the scenario's prefs: 001 0.00/0.75/1.50/2.00, 002 0.00/1.15/1.75/2.25), `Policy.CurrentChapter`, `GamePrefs.aiagressivenessoffensiveoperationfaction[alliance]` (Union 1.0, CSA 0.5; alliance ≤ 1 only, else 1, 14317-14319), `GameVars.usedcampaignagressiveness` (64808), the winter modifier when `monthCounter <= winterquartersmonthuntil || >= winterquartersmonthfrom` (14310-14313; vanilla's literal factor is `winterdefensiveoperationsmodifier`, 14312, which is vanilla bug BUG-LOG-004: `winteroffensiveoperationsmodifier`, 0.05, is loaded at 56381 and never read, and the defensive one belongs to `CheckForDefensiveOperations`, 13567. The planner applies the factor the roll has in this install: the offensive modifier while `Enable Winter Offensive Modifier Fix` is on (the default) and the defensive one with it off, `WinterOffensiveModifierPatch.CurrentRollModifier`), `idleYears` = date − mean `timeofnotmoving` over the planned mass (`TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds`; vanilla over the package, 14321-14327; the snapshot gains `FormationRecord.TimeOfNotMoving`, read from `Regiment.timeofnotmoving` 110614, additive), the W&L multiplier `dlcwl_careerincreasedaggressiveness` (twice when a `CarryingGroup` formation holding the player is in the mass, 14341-14347; a `PlayerMapGroup` formation is never in it). `timediff` from the tempo clock below.
  - *Where:* the mover, before the first `MoveUnitTo` of a MainEffort formation toward a main target in a state of another alliance (a `CarryingGroup` holding the player is moved, so it is gated the same way). Vanilla rolls before its move/order split (the one roll at 14336-14350 gates both `MoveUnitTo`, 14383, and the W&L order `AIBattle.CheckCurrentOrderUpdate(…, 16 or 5, …)`, 14449-14456); the planner has no MainEffort order to gate, because a `PlayerMapGroup` formation is never MainEffort, and vanilla's own roll may still take the player's group into one of its packages (14234, 14263) and order it (14449-14456): that is vanilla's behaviour and stays. Vanilla rolls only for enemy ground: `GameVars.nation[BattlefieldSetup.GetStateOfField(target, true)].alliance != own` and an enemy weight `num2 > 0` at the target (25714; 14335-14336), with the believed land strength within relocation range of the target standing in for vanilla's omniscient `num2`. **Support:** a Support move or the map-group player's Support order whose target lies in enemy-held ground (`GameVars.nation[BattlefieldSetup.GetStateOfField(target, true)].alliance`, 25714 as at 14335-14336, at war per `GameVars.alliance[own].IsAtWarWith(that)`, 63127) waits, `MoveOutcome.Deferred`, until `TempoReleasedDecision == DecisionSerial`; it is never rolled for itself. A move inside own or neutral (not at war) territory, a CapitalGuard/Recover/DepotBuilder/Recon move or order, and every move of a decision already released are never gated. The roll is seeded (`PlannerState.CampaignSeed`, `DecisionSerial`, day serial, alliance: FNV-1a, reproducible like P5's draw). Released → `PlannerState.TempoReleasedDecision = DecisionSerial` (persisted, G6 DTO). The mover also sets `TempoReleasedDecision = DecisionSerial` before the first MainEffort dispatch of a decision whose main effort needs no roll (vanilla's own roll condition at 14336 fails: the target is not in a state of another alliance, or no believed land strength lies within relocation range of the target), exactly as on a passed, forced or debug roll; a later review on which the advancing mass reveals an enemy does not roll the rest of the mass. Released means `TempoReleasedDecision == DecisionSerial` everywhere in this plan. A decision whose mass has no dispatchable MainEffort formation (every member busy, e.g. `InDefensiveOps`) dispatches nothing, so it is never released: its Support stays deferred and withheld from vanilla's offensive until G3 replans. G3 must not keep such a decision: with an empty dispatchable mass the main effort is not viable, so it falls to Hold at that review. Not released → the plan stays published, the mass stays withheld from vanilla's offensive (below), no MainEffort formation moves and no Support move or order into enemy-held ground goes out (`MoveOutcome.Deferred`, value 9 of the enum above), retried next review.
  - *Vanilla's own offensive (only the mover launches the mass).* `CampaignWithholdView.WithheldFromOffense` also returns true for every MainEffort member of the published plan's mass (`TheaterPlan.MassInstanceIds`, whose members G4 assigns MainEffort except the Reserve, Recon and DepotBuilder picks carved from it, which are withheld anyway; Support formations are outside it and stay vanilla's), so G5a's availability Postfix (14080) takes it out of vanilla's offensive candidate path: the package list holds only units that pass the test (14234) and the call returns when its lead is not in the list (14243-14245), so a withheld lead launches nothing, and a withheld unit is never recruited into another lead's package (14263). A deferred mass therefore waits whole and vanilla rolls nothing for it. Once released, the mover dispatches the mass and lists each dispatched member in `unitsinoffensiveoperations`, which vanilla's own test already excludes (14107-14111); the rule therefore changes only unlisted members (deferred, waiting under `MaxMovesPerDay`, or dropped from the list when vanilla ends the offensive), and each of those is launched on an offensive only by the mover. Vanilla's two other callers of the test, `RollUpEnemyObjectivesInZone` (13953) and `CheckOccupationOfLostCities` (17448), skip mass members too; neither rolls tempo in vanilla, and the mover's role move covers the mass. Until G6, #38 binds the same patched method (`OffensiveAvailabilityWrapper.IsAvailable`, `OffensiveAvailabilityWrapper.cs:66-73`), so its packages skip the mass as well, and the mover refuses #38's locked packages (steps, below). **Deferred Support.** While the decision is deferred (`TempoReleasedDecision != DecisionSerial`), `WithheldFromOffense` also returns true for each of its Support formations except the map-group player, through the same Postfix at 14080, so vanilla's own roll cannot launch a Support formation into enemy ground ahead of the main effort (the piecemeal path); on release (`TempoReleasedDecision == DecisionSerial`) the clause drops and Support is vanilla's again under vanilla's own roll. The map-group player is never withheld: vanilla may still roll his group (14234, 14263, 14449-14456), as stated above. Formations the allocation leaves available (Hold above its sector's need, and Support once released) stay vanilla's, under vanilla's own roll. The mass and deferred-Support clauses are offense-only: `WithheldFromDefense` never applies them, so vanilla's `CheckForDefensiveOperations` may take a deferred mass member or a deferred Support formation (its availability tests at 13610 and 13658 pass it; 13716-13717 move it into `unitsindefensiveoperations`), and the next review's snapshot flags it `FormationFlags.InDefensiveOps` (`CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs:337`). G5a adds `FormationFlags.InDefensiveOps` to `TheaterPlanner.Busy` (`TheaterPlanner.cs:93-94`), so a formation in a vanilla defensive operation is not Movable: G3 drops it from the mass and G4 keeps it Hold in place with reason `busy` (G4's busy rule) until vanilla ends the defence (13782-13848); the mover dispatches nothing to it. Not `InOffensiveOps`: the mover lists its own dispatched MainEffort/Support units there, so that flag in `Busy` would freeze the mass after its first dispatch. `CapitalDefense` is unaffected (#4's pledge goes to `groupstodefendcapital`, G5b, not the defensive list). Pre-war the view withholds nothing (pre-war gate).
  - *Telemetry:* `[CampaignTempo] alliance=<a> main=<id> decision=<n> chapter=<c> gap=<days> p=<tval> roll=<r> released=<bool> forced=<bool>` on the first review of a decision and on release (bounded: at most two rows per decision).
  - *Tests (`CampaignTempoGateTests`):* the five moved `TempoProbability` cases; `ChapterAggressionIsVanillasArray` (001 and 002 values, chapter −1 and 4 → 0); `ForcedInvasionIsVanillas14303` (001 only, Union only, objective 6 open, date bound); `DebugPermanentMovementReleases` (any roll releases with the debug flag set, 14350); `RollMatchesVanillaPerPackageRule` (for packages of one to four formations with given `timeofnotmoving`, chapter, faction, winter and W&L inputs, the planner's probability equals vanilla's `num9` computed step by step as 14316-14347 over the same list, and `Releases` equals vanilla's `!(num8 > num9 && !debug && !flag)` over a grid of rolls that includes `roll == tval`); `IdleTermAddedOncePerRoll` (with `timediff = 0` the probability is `clamp01(idle³)` times the W&L factors; doubling `timediff` changes only the chapter term; one review's roll adds the idle term once); `ReleaseRateMatchesFormula` (`idleYears = 0`, `CampaignSeed = 1861` fixed in the test, 200000 seeded rolls per case over consecutive day serials and decision serials: the per-roll release frequency lies within `4·sqrt(p(1−p)/n)` of `p`, for 001 Union chapters 1-3 and 001 CSA chapter 1); `FirstRollOfNewDecisionUsesOneReviewGap` (a decision chosen 60 days after the previous release rolls with one day's worth); `NoRollMainEffortReleasesDecision` (a main target in an enemy state with believed strength 0: `TempoReleasedDecision` is set on the first review before the first MainEffort dispatch, its Support is not withheld and its Support move into enemy ground goes out, and a later review with believed strength above 0 does not roll the rest of the mass); `EmptyDispatchableMassIsNotKept` (through the real `TheaterPlanner.Plan`: a main effort whose every mass member is busy, e.g. flagged `InDefensiveOps`, is not kept; the sector falls to Hold at that review and no Support stays withheld for it); `GapCappedAfterPauseOrPreWar` (the first review after 001's pre-war idle, after a 30-day W&L pause and after a stand-down: one review's worth); `NoStoredDayUsesOneReviewGap` (reload from a v1/v2 sidecar); `LastFullReviewDayOnlyOnExecutorsAllowedFullReview` (pre-war, paused and Bookkeeping reviews do not advance it); `GateOnlyFirstMoveOfADecision`; `SupportWaitsForMainEffortLaunchIntoEnemyGround` (a Support move whose target lies in enemy-held ground is `Deferred` until the decision's release and goes out on the release review; a decision whose main effort needs no roll is released before its first MainEffort dispatch, so its Support goes out on that review); `SupportRepositionsFreelyInOwnGround` (Support moves in own and neutral ground go out while the decision is deferred); `PlayerSupportOrderFollowsAiSupportRule` (from the real allocator, a `PlayerMapGroup` Support assignment's `TryIssue` `Redeploy` order waits into enemy-held ground and goes out in own ground on exactly the reviews an AI Support formation of the same plan does); `DefendingMassMemberIsBusyAtNextReview` (through the real `TheaterPlanner.Plan` and `ForceAllocator.Allocate`: a mass member flagged `InDefensiveOps` is out of `MassInstanceIds`, is Hold with reason `busy`, and gets no mover dispatch; a member flagged `InOffensiveOps` stays in the mass); `OwnTerritoryMoveNeverGated`; `NonMainRolesNeverGated`; `DeferredMoveLeavesPlanPublished`; `DeferredMassWithheldFromVanillaOffense` (every MainEffort mass member is withheld from offense while the decision is deferred and while undispatched after the release; a Hold formation above its sector's need is not, nor is a Support formation once the decision is released; with the pre-war gate nothing is); `DeferredMassAvailableForVanillaDefense` (a deferred mass member and a deferred Support formation: `WithheldFromDefense` false wherever the enemy position lies, the defensive Postfix leaves vanilla's result at 13610/13658, while `WithheldFromOffense` is true); `DeferredSupportWithheldFromVanillaOffenseUntilRelease` (an AI Support formation of a deferred decision is withheld from offense, not from defense; on the release review `WithheldFromOffense` is false for it; the map-group player's Support formation is never withheld; a decision whose main effort needs no roll is released at its first MainEffort dispatch and withholds no Support from then on); `SeededRollReproducible`; `WlCareerMultipliesTwiceWithPlayerInMass`; `ReleasedDecisionSurvivesRestore`; `PlannerReadsNoCalendarEra` (source pin over `Strategic/Planner/`: no `EraStage` and nothing matching `-E "Year\s*[<>]=?\s*18[0-9]{2}"`; decision 7).
  - *Plants (≥ 18):* chapter index off by one; faction factor ignored; winter ignored; forced flag ignored; debug flag ignored; gate on every move of the decision; idle term dropped; idle term scaled by `timediff`; W&L multiplier once; `timediff` measured from the previous release; gap not capped; Support ungated into enemy ground; Support held in own ground; the player's Support order ungated into enemy ground; mass not withheld from vanilla's offensive; mass withheld from defense; defending member still in the mass; deferred Support left to vanilla's roll; no-roll launch leaves `TempoReleasedDecision` unset.
  - *Census (to add at G7):* `FormationRecord.TimeOfNotMoving` | G5a idle term; `PlannerState.TempoReleasedDecision`, `LastFullReviewDay` | G5a gate and tempo clock, G6 sidecar.

This file is also edited by `fix/chapters` (rule 2's calendar runway): disjoint lines, merged by whichever lands second. ### G5A as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g5a` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g5a`) from `feat/campaign-planner` `36f07ff`. This section is the build record's summary (the full record is `scratchpad/loop/stageG/build-g5a.md`). Commits `e950439` (tempo gate, pre-war rules, busy mask), `6a7d5d4` (the pure mover pass, tracker, withhold view and steering rules), `cdfe0aa` (runtime halves, patches, deletions), `228b6be` and `b598b7f` (tests the mutation round asked for), then this documentation commit. Harness 3095 PASS (3013 base - 3 retired perf tests + 85 new; the five moved TempoProbability asserts are one of them), `./build.sh` and the test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (155 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 33 -> 34 (`MoveTracker.Records`, read by G6's sidecar v3 writer; `Restore` shares its name with other members, so the name-based sweep does not list it), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new flag, no new Prefix-skip, no Transpiler; two new patch classes (first-fire `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive|defensive`), #1 and #2 changed in place. Mutation round: 110 plants, all killed (99 on the first run, the 11 survivors and the one compile error fixed by tests and re-run), `evidence/g5a/mutation-results*.txt`.

**Where the plan was silent, wrong or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. **The pure mover pass is a file the owned list did not name.** The mandated tests (`MapGroupPlayerGetsOrderNotMove`, `SupportWaitsForMainEffortLaunchIntoEnemyGround`, `PlayerSupportOrderFollowsAiSupportRule`, `GateOnlyFirstMoveOfADecision`, ...) cannot run against a runtime file, so the decisions live in `Planner/CampaignMover.cs` (pure, over an `IMoverHost`: restricted target, state owner, at war, package lock, `MoveUnitTo`, the player's order, the offensive list, the transport default) and `CampaignMoverRuntime` implements the host over vanilla. `Planner/SteeringRules.cs` (pure: objective write, theater box and its weekly limiter, importance sector test) is the same split for `CampaignSteeringRuntime`; `Planner/PlannerFactionMap.cs` (runtime) caches faction index -> alliance for the per-unit patches.
2. **`MoveTracker`** gains `IssueOutcome` (Issued / Retried / Queued: `Retried` is reported at the re-issue, `Failed` and `GaveUp` at the judgement), `RecordRefused` (vanilla refused the target or it is restricted ground: a miss without the wait), `CollectInTransit`, `Forget`, `Clear` and an optional `unitKey` on `RecordIssued`. A restored record is settled (a spent retry stays given up). The cap counts `MoveUnitTo` calls only; a queued move spends nothing and the map-group player's order is a dispatch (F dedupes it), not a move.
3. **GaveUp reassigns the role, in G3 and G4 (verify follow-ups 1 and 2).** `MoveTracker.CollectGaveUp(list, daySerial)` lists the moves it gave up on and that have not lapsed (`GaveUpMove`: instance id, target and objective, ordered by id). `ReviewPlan` collects the set once, before the plan, and passes it two ways. **G3** (owner's decision, a change to `TheaterPlanner.cs` beyond the Busy mask): the mover records the objective on each move when it issues it (`MoveRecord.ObjectiveId`: a MainEffort or Support move carries its sector plan's `TargetObjectiveId`, any other role's move -1), so nothing is read back from an earlier plan (the first design resolved it from the previous published plan; that left G3 blind after a load and when a target moved, and was deleted with `GaveUpObjectives`), `TheaterPlanner.Plan` takes the `GaveUpMove` list as an optional last parameter, and `MassFor(…, objectiveId, …)` leaves a formation out of the mass of the objective it gave up on, as it leaves out a busy one. The mass, `MassStrength` and every candidate's odds are recomputed on the force that can still march; if no force is left the existing no-force rule drops the main effort to Hold at that review, and if the odds fall the decision switches or is dropped by the normal triggers. **G4**: `ForceAllocator.Allocate` takes the moves as an optional last parameter; a formation that gave up at the target its role would send it to loses that role: the depot-builder and recon picks skip it and take the next candidate (`SmallestIn`, `AssignRecon`), and any other marching role (`MainEffort`, `Support`, `CapitalGuard`, `Recover`) becomes Hold in place with reason `gave-up` and `Withheld` false (`Assign`). **Lapse:** a give-up lasts `MoveTracker.GaveUpExpiryDays` = 14 review days from the retry's issue day, then `ShouldIssue`, `CollectGaveUp` and so G3, G4 and the mover agree that the formation may try the same target again as a first issue. Why 14: a give-up costs two reviews (one miss, one retry); vanilla's refusal of a target (`MoveUnitTo`, 14486) follows a state's war status, which changes in days or weeks, not reviews; a shorter bound would respend two of the daily six moves per formation on every cycle, a longer one would shut a formation out of an objective for a season. A different target starts afresh at once. **Telemetry:** a kept main effort with no formation the mover can send logs `[CampaignTempo] alliance=<a> main=<id> decision=<n> chapter=<c> undispatchable=true` once per decision on entering the state (`PlannerState.UndispatchableRowDecision`, cleared when a sendable main effort returns); after this fix the state should not occur, and the row is how G7's smoke would see it. Tests: `GaveUpMassIsDroppedOrRebuiltNeverStalled` (the verifier's repro through the real `Plan`, `Allocate` and `Review`, with the stall as the control), `GaveUpRecordsLapseAndTheRoleComesBack`, `MovesCarryTheObjectiveTheyWereIssuedFor`, `UndispatchableMainEffortLogsOnceAndClears`, `GaveUpMainEffortLosesTheRoleAtThatTarget`, `GaveUpDepotBuilderIsReplacedByTheNextFormation`, `GaveUpReconIsReplacedByTheNextFormation`, `GaveUpCapitalGuardBecomesAnUnwithheldHold`, `GaveUpSetIsCollectedFromTheTrackerOnly`, `ReviewPlanPassesTheTrackersGaveUpSet`.
4. **The executor context.** The release, the decision and the pre-war bool the availability Postfixes need are not in `CampaignPlanStore`; they are published with the plan as an `ExecutorContext` (`PublishExecutorContext` in `ReviewPlan`, again at the end of `ReviewExecute` once the mover has released the decision) and join the day's atomic publish, so a patch between the slices of a review never reads today's release next to yesterday's plan. The map-group player's formation id rides in it (the deferred-Support clause never withholds him). `MoveTrackers` (per alliance, CampaignScope) is read live: an issued move is withheld from vanilla at once.
5. **Staleness and "today".** `PlanFreshness.NoteToday` is stamped by every `ReviewPlan` call (both alliances, every mode, so a paused alliance's plan ages out; `EnemyDetectionRuntime.ReviewDayFor` advances in a pause too but is per-alliance detection state). A published plan older than two days is ignored by the view and the importance steering; and `ReviewExecute` acts only when the plan and the snapshot are today's (`PlanFreshness.IsToday`), because a Plan job that failed before publishing leaves yesterday's plan in the working copy.
6. **`CampaignMilitaryGates.ShouldRedirectToPlanTarget` is re-keyed** (the same-theater clause is gone: Support sectors point at a main target in another theater): `(targetIsSource, sourceInSteeredSector, distance, max, ownPoints, supplyCapacity, valueClause)`; the two R7 tests in `CampaignMilitaryFixTests` follow (`ImportanceRedirectStaysInSteeredSectors`).
7. **The winter factor of the roll** (settled in the verify follow-up). Vanilla's roll reads `GamePrefs.winterdefensiveoperationsmodifier` in the winter months (`num5`, 14310-14312: month <= `winterquartersmonthuntil` or >= `winterquartersmonthfrom`); that is BUG-LOG-004, because the offensive modifier (`winteroffensiveoperationsmodifier`, 56381) is never read and the defensive one is `CheckForDefensiveOperations`' own (13567). `WinterOffensiveModifierPatch` makes vanilla's roll read the offensive one while its flag is on. The planner replaces that roll, so it applies exactly the factor the roll has in this install: `WinterOffensiveModifierPatch.CurrentRollModifier()` (moved there from #38's private `WinterModifier()`; #38 and the mover read it) is the offensive modifier with the fix on, the defensive one with it off, 1 outside winter. The plan text above had named the defensive one, which is only the unfixed case; it is corrected. Pinned by `WinterFactorIsTheFactorVanillasRollHasInThisInstall`.
8. **Superseded and deleted:** `CoordinatedOffensiveVanillaGates.TempoProbability` (moved), `KeyedResolveCache` with its three F9 perf tests and fixtures (the objective-id area lookup they served is gone: #2 resolves the sector target through the colour map once per published plan), #2's CIC reads. `CoordinatedOffensiveVanillaGates.TempoPasses` stays duplicated with `CampaignTempoGate.Releases` until G6 deletes the file with #38 (plan).
9. **Telemetry.** `[CampaignTempo]` and `[CampaignSteer]` are registered in `TelemetryTagPolicy` (G0's list lacked the tempo row); `[CampaignMover]` carries three more counts (`skippedBusy`, `deferred`, `ordered`) after the plan's `skippedPlayer`; `[CampaignSteer] alliance=<a> objective=<kept|released|id> theaters=<n>` is emitted when a write was made.
10. **Choices the plan left open:** dispatch order under the cap CapitalGuard, MainEffort, Recover, DepotBuilder, Support (the main effort releases the tempo before Support is judged); `ArrivalRadius` 10 (a formation whose final waypoint is within it of the target is there or underway); the tempo target is the objective record of the main objective nearest the main sector's target (a clamped frontier point in own ground must not stand in for an enemy objective); a move is not issued while a `DefensiveMovingOrder` is running for the formation (`MoveUnitTo` would refuse, 14493) and a pending order is not judged (the queue serves one order at a time, 9966-10052); a restricted target (`IsTerrainPositionRestricted`, the test `SetWaypoint` applies through `CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable`, 130768) is skipped and counted as a miss; `userails`/`userivers` are raised before the move; the roll is FNV-1a over the little-endian fields finished with the murmur3 mixer (24-bit float), so consecutive days and decisions are uncorrelated (`ReleaseRateMatchesFormula`, four cases of 200000 within 4 sigma).
11. **`LastFullReviewDay`** is assigned at the end of `CampaignMover.Review`, which `ReviewExecute` reaches only after the pause, stand-down, pre-war and today's-plan returns (`LastFullReviewDayOnlyOnExecutorsAllowedFullReview` pins the three writes).
12. **#2 waits for the release (integrator decision, verify follow-up).** `CampaignWithholdView.TrySteering` returns false until `ExecutorContext.Released` (`TempoReleasedDecision == DecisionSerial`). Before the tempo roll releases the launch, #2 leaves vanilla's own importance pick, so no formation vanilla leaves free in a MainEffort or Support sector is pointed at the plan target ahead of the main effort (decision 5: no piecemeal attack). The spec's C7 redirect therefore starts with the launch, not with the plan. `ImportanceRedirectWaitsForTheRelease`.
13. **`PlannerFactionMap.AllianceOf`** is pinned line by line (`PlannerFactionMapReadsTheAllianceNotTheIndex`): the faction index is never the alliance id (P1).

**Census (to add at G7).** `FormationRecord.TimeOfNotMoving` | G5a idle term (`CampaignMover.EvaluateLaunch`); `PlannerState.TempoReleasedDecision`, `LastFullReviewDay` | G5a gate and tempo clock, G6 sidecar (`PlannerStateDto`); `PlannerState.TempoRowDecision` | transient, not persisted; `MoveTracker.Records`/`Restore` | G6 sidecar v3 (`PlannerStateDto.moves` by unit key); `ExecutorContext` | G5a patches via `CampaignWithholdView`; `PlanFreshness.IsFresh/IsToday` | the view, `ReviewExecute`; `SteeringRules.*`, `CampaignMover`, `CampaignTempoGate.*` | the runtime halves; `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ExecutorsEnabled` | #1, #2, the availability Postfixes.

**G5a smoke items (with G7's smoke):** (a) `[CampaignTempo] ... undispatchable=true` should never appear (a kept main effort with no formation to send); `[CampaignTempo] alliance=<a> main=<id> decision=<n> chapter=<c> gap=1 p=<tval> roll=<r> released=<bool>` on each new main effort into enemy ground (`p=none` and `released=true` when no roll was needed), then at most one more row on its release, and a `[CampaignMover] ... confirmed > 0` in the following reviews, never an `issued` row before the release; (b) `[first-fire]` for `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive`, `...defensive` and `campaign.patch.importance-values`; (c) pre-war 001: `[once:campaign-planner:pre-war-idle]` and no `[CampaignMover]`, `[CampaignTempo]` or `[CampaignSteer]` row before the war, `[Appointment]` rows allowed; (d) `[CampaignSteer]` rows only on a change; (e) W&L corps career: the map-group player's Support order is a dispatch (`order type 5`) and waits into enemy ground exactly while the AI Support formations' moves do; a brigade career: the carrying corps moves and a dispatch panel appears; (f) rollback: `Enable Campaign Planner Executors = false` gives no mover, steering or availability effect and #1 picks again. (g) give-ups recur no more than once per 15 days for one formation at one objective: read the `[CampaignMover] ... gaveUp=<n>` rows (and the `retried` rows before them) per formation and objective over a long run; a formation giving up at the same objective sooner than `MoveTracker.GaveUpExpiryDays` + 1 days after its last give-up is a finding (G5a verify round 2, N2)
