**Vanilla to read:** `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` 11668-11869 (need 11712-11741; prune 11751-11781; add 11791-11817; move 11819-11867); `ZoneRecruiting` 12405 / heatmap deficit 12467-12520; `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17232-17332 (17327-17328); `BattleUnits.MoveUnit` 93091-93150 (guard 93099; `SetTransfer` 93147); `GrabExistingGroup` 12739-12806 (callers: `ZoneRecruiting` 12558, `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17319; `RemovePlayerUnits` 12755/12790/12797; may create a division in a corps, 12796-12801); `RaiseNewCampaignGroup` 12940-13076; recruit travel `SetTransfer` 139051-139075 (`speedforunittransfers` 45/yr, campaignprefs 269-270); `CheckCombinationOfUnits` 17158 (`skipdlccheck: true` precedent).

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public static class CapitalFloorPolicy {
  // 11712-11741 with beliefs in place of enemyunits: minStrength + Σ min(str, str×morale)/max(10,d)^0.3×0.5 over beliefs within range − own str×morale closer than the nearest enemy
  public static float VanillaNeed(float minStrengthForMovement, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<EnemyBelief> beliefs, float believedMorale,
                                  float capitalX, float capitalZ, float relocationRange, System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<FormationRecord> own);
  public static int PickPledge(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, float vanillaNeed); /* instanceId or -1 */ }
public static class ReplacementFlowPolicy { // planShortfall = required strength − own strength at the main target; believedEnemy = EnemyBeliefQuery.StrengthWithin there. No vanilla heatmap enemy term.
  public static bool ShouldSteer(TheaterPlan plan, float believedEnemyAtTarget, float planShortfall, out float targetX, out float targetZ); }
public static class ReinforcementRouting { public const float MaxRouteFactor = 1.5f; // × relocation range from the recruiting cell
  public static bool TryRouteRecruit(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, float cellX, float cellZ, out float routeX, out float routeZ); // nearest shortfall formation
  public static bool TryTransferPair(CampaignSnapshot s, Allocation a, out float deficitX, out float deficitZ, out float surplusX, out float surplusZ); }
```

**#4 rewrite (Prefix, Priority.High, non-skip; unchanged rule):** pre-pledge the allocation's nearest CapitalGuard formation into `groupstodefendcapital` only while vanilla's own need (`num2`, recomputed by `CapitalFloorPolicy.VanillaNeed` from beliefs, the 11712-11741 formula) is > 0 and the formation passes vanilla's 11791 filters; never a player-owned or custom-ordered (`CoastalDefenseCustomOrderRunner.IsCustomOrdered`) unit. The excess floor stays CapitalGuard (G5a mover + availability). First-fire id kept `campaign.patch.defensive-ops`. Telemetry `[CapitalFloor] alliance=<a> vanillaNeed=<x> floor=<y> pledged=<unit|none>` on change.

**#3 rewrite (Prefix/Postfix/Finalizer, snapshot-restore, unchanged mechanism):** swap `positiondeficit` for the worst shortfall formation's position and `positionsurplus` for the largest surplus formation's position when `ReinforcementRouting.TryTransferPair` succeeds (else the main-effort target via `ReplacementFlowPolicy.ShouldSteer`, with the plan shortfall and the believed enemy at the target; vanilla's `positiondeficit.enemystrength`, the old `:142` read, is no longer read); vanilla then moves one brigade from near the surplus to a division near the deficit (`GrabExistingGroup` 17319, `MoveUnit` 17328) — in careers through the C1 fix. Finalizer restores. Ids kept `campaign.patch.transfer-of-units.prefix|postfix|finalizer`.

**Recruit routing (new `RecruitRoutingPatch`):** vanilla attaches each new regiment to a division near the recruiting heatmap cell (`GrabExistingGroup` from `ZoneRecruiting` 12558) and nobody routes it to an army that needs it. Vanilla's target is `private static Regiment GrabExistingGroup(AIFaction _aifaction, Vector3 position, float limitdivisions = 1f, float searchradiusfactor = 1f, bool enhanceradius = true)` (12739; `AIFaction` is a private nested type, 10299, and `position` is passed by value): the patch targets it by name through `AccessTools.Method(typeof(AICampaign), "GrabExistingGroup")` and declares only `static void Prefix(ref Vector3 position)` (Harmony's by-ref argument edit; no `AIFaction` parameter). Argument edit, no skip. It acts only while `RecruitmentPatch.InZoneRecruiting` is true (the existing `ZoneRecruiting` scope, so the transfer call at 17319 is untouched) and takes the faction from `RecruitmentPatch.ActiveAifaction`; replaces `position` with the nearest shortfall formation within `MaxRouteFactor ×` relocation range of the cell (`ReinforcementRouting.TryRouteRecruit`); vanilla's own division search, `RemovePlayerUnits` and abstract travel (`SetTransfer`) then run unchanged. First-fire `campaign.patch.recruit-routing` (situational: only when vanilla recruits). Telemetry `ReplacementFlow`: `[ReplacementFlow] alliance=<a> kind=<recruit|transfer> to=<formation> from=<cell|formation>`.

**Career transfer fix (C1, decided yes; BUG-WL-013):** `WlCareerTransferScopePatch` Prefix/Finalizer on `CheckTransferOfUnits` sets/clears a `[ThreadStatic]` scope when `DLC_WL.dlc_scenarioactive`; `WlCareerTransferMoveUnitPatch` Prefix on the instance method `BattleUnits.MoveUnit(GameObject unittomove, GameObject unittomovebelow, bool skipdlccheck = false)` (93091; the flag is passed by value, so the Prefix declares `ref bool skipdlccheck` as Harmony's argument edit) sets `skipdlccheck = true` while the scope is set **and neither unit is `dlcw_isundercommander`** (never re-parents a player unit). No skip; vanilla's body runs. First-fire ids `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.scope`, `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.move-unit` (situational: careers only). `OnceLog.Info("campaign-planner:wl-transfer-fix-first", "[WlTransferFix] career transfer allowed unit=<name> to=<name>")`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] **Player formations are never a routing target (G4 verify r2 N1; spec C4).** `Allocation.Shortfalls` may carry a `PlayerMapGroup` formation's instance id as a *position marker* (a threatened sector only the player stands in, once he is short of its need, G4 `SectorHeldOnlyByThePlayerIsHeldUntilHeIsShort`). `ReinforcementRouting.TryRouteRecruit` / `TryTransferPair` and the #3 swap use that formation's **hold point only** (`ReserveRules.HoldPoint`: the map-group player's last waypoint, the point his group counts at, not his physical position; the shortfall's sector is `ReserveRules.HoldSectorOf`) as the cell or point the replacement flows toward and never its instance id: no `MoveUnit`, `SetTransfer` or attach is aimed at a player formation, and a candidate whose `Chain` fails `WlChainRules.MayMove` is dropped from every pairing's source side. Vanilla's `GrabExistingGroup` already drops player units from its own candidates (`RemovePlayerUnits` 12755/12790/12797), so a position-only use is safe. Test: `ReinforcementRoutingNeverTargetsAPlayerFormation` (a shortfall list whose only carrier is a `PlayerMapGroup` formation routes to its hold point, and a pair built from it never names its instance id as a destination or source).
- [ ] Tests first: `PledgeOnlyWhileVanillaNeedPositive`, `PledgeNeverPlayerOrCustomOrdered`, `VanillaNeedMatchesFormulaFixture` (fixture reproducing 11712-11741), `ExcessFloorStaysCapitalGuard`, `SteerOnlyWhenPlanShortfallWorse`, `NoSteerWithoutMainEffort`, `SteerReadsNoVanillaEnemyTerm` (source pin: `TransferOfUnitsPatch.cs` no longer mentions `enemystrength` or `recruitmentheatmapenemy`), `CareerFixNeverForPlayerUnits` (pure decision `WlTransferFixDecision.Allow(bool scope, bool career, bool srcOwned, bool dstOwned)`), `RecruitRoutedToNearestShortFormation`, `RecruitNotRoutedBeyondRange`, `RecruitRoutingNeverToPlayerFormation`, `RecruitRoutingOnlyInsideZoneRecruiting`, `PledgeKeepsChainGuard`, `TransferDeficitIsShortFormationSurplusIsReserveExcess`, `TransferSurplusNeverFromMainEffort`, `TransferUsesBeliefsNotHeatmapEnemy`.
- [ ] Implement; gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): capital floor, replacement flow, recruit routing, career transfer fix [G5b]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (`[CapitalFloor]` rows; in a W&L career `[once:campaign-planner:wl-transfer-fix-first]` and vanilla's transfer debug line followed by the unit actually changing parent).

### G5B as built (2026-10-15)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g5b` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g5b`) from `feat/campaign-planner` `d3f267f`, with `ae349e8` (integ2 landing X1 X2) merged in at the end. Commits `402e3ab` (code, tests, flag), `22d9566` (an orphan the rewrite left), `140c7ab` (test tightening after the mutation round), the merge, and the director's dead capital modifier (below). Harness 3025 PASS (3003 at `d3f267f`, minus 6 tests of superseded code, plus 28 in `CapitalAndReplacementTests`), build and test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (156 entries; 155 + `Enable W&L Career Transfer Fix`), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 33 (the same 33 rows as `d3f267f`), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No new Prefix-skip, Transpiler or `Plugin.cs` flag beyond the one named in the plan.

Files: new `Strategic/Planner/{CapitalFloorPolicy, ReinforcementRouting, ReplacementFlowPolicy}.cs` (pure, in the test csproj), `Strategic/Planner/{LiveFormationReader, ReplacementFlowRow}.cs` (runtime), `Patches/{RecruitRoutingPatch, WlCareerTransferFixPatch}.cs`; rewritten `Patches/{DefensiveOpsPatch, TransferOfUnitsPatch}.cs`; edited `Patches/RecruitmentPatch.cs` (the two accessors, nothing else), `Strategic/CampaignMilitaryGates.cs`, `Strategic/CampaignMapLedger.cs`, `Strategic/DirectorPosture.cs`, `Strategic/StrategicResilienceDirector.cs`, `Plugin.cs`, `tools/smoke-situational.txt`; test `CapitalAndReplacementTests.cs`.

**Where the plan was wrong, silent or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. **No snapshot reaches a patch.** `CampaignPlanStore` publishes plan, allocation and sectors; the pooled `CampaignSnapshot` is rebuilt in place by the review and is not published, so `PickPledge`/`TryRouteRecruit`/`TryTransferPair` cannot take a `CampaignSnapshot s`. They take `IReadOnlyList<FormationRecord> own` (and, where needed, the `TheaterPlan`), built live from the faction's `ownunits` by the new runtime `LiveFormationReader` (instance id = `Regiment.GetInstanceID`, the same id the snapshot recorded; `Chain` from `ChainClassOf(in chain, ...)`; Strength is `groupstrengthactive` for the capital need, vanilla's measure at 11733-11740, and `groupstrengthdirect` for the flow). `PickPledge(Allocation, float vanillaNeed, IReadOnlyList<PledgeCandidate>)` takes a pool of `PledgeCandidate { InstanceId, DistanceToCapital, Chain, PassesVanillaFilters, CustomOrdered }`: vanilla's own 11791-11817 filters stay the patch's runtime reads (`EligibleForCapitalDefense`, unchanged) and arrive as a bool. The pure checks are the chain guard, the allocation's CapitalGuard role and the custom-order refusal.
2. **`TryTransferPair` yields amounts.** Vanilla sizes a transfer from the two `TransferData` magnitudes (`num3 = (surplus.own - surplus.enemy) x maxtransferfromsurplus`, `num4 = deficit.enemy - deficit.own`, both must be positive, 17275-17290), so a position pair alone gives the patch nothing honest to inject. It returns a `TransferPair` (deficit and surplus instance ids, points and strengths). The deficit strength is `ReinforcementRouting.ShortfallStrength`: the carrier's need by role (the main effort's `MainRequiredRatio x MainEnemyStrength` less the MainEffort formations, the capital floor less the CapitalGuards, a sector's `max(HoldNeed, MinGarrisonStrength)` less what counts there, the map-group player at strength x morale), which restates `ForceAllocator.ListShortfalls`; `ShortfallAmountsAgreeWithTheAllocatorsCarriers` runs the real allocator and fails if a carrier it lists has no positive need here. The surplus strength is the surplus formation's. The patch injects `TransferData(deficit point, 0, need)` and `TransferData(surplus point, strength, 0)`; both records are replaced, so the Finalizer restores both (`SurplusField`/`OriginalSurplus`; today's code restored one). `y` of the injected points is 0, as vanilla's heatmap cells carry it.
3. **The main-target steer.** The plan's `ShouldSteer(plan, believedEnemyAtTarget, planShortfall, ...)` stays; `TryMainTarget` (the main sector's published gather point) and `PlanShortfall(plan, believed, own, tx, tz, radius)` (the plan's required ratio over the believed enemy less the coverage-counting own strength within the relocation range, each formation by its hold point) feed it. "Worse" has no vanilla deficit to be worse than now (its enemy term is gone): it means an army is believed at the main target and the shortfall is at least `ReplacementFlowPolicy.MinimumShortfall` (2500, the old plan-target steer's "token presence"). Only the deficit is replaced here; vanilla's surplus stays.
4. **Both planner flags gate all three.** #3, #4 and the recruit routing return unless `Enable Campaign Planner` and `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` are on (they are executors in P10's sense: they write through vanilla's own lists and arguments), and unless the alliance is 0 or 1 and not the W&L CIC player's. With no published plan/allocation they do nothing and vanilla runs unchanged.
5. **The front ledger's transfer budget gate stays in #3.** The plan is silent. `FrontSectorLedger.EvaluateTransfer` still vetoes a transfer that would strip a sector below its minimum hold (blocked rows are reported once per signature); it reads the sector keys of the new points. G6 decides the ledger's fate; the allocation is the authority for who is short and who has surplus.
6. **The CIC input is gone from #4.** Personality gate, era, director capital modifier, the capital-town value multiplier and the search-radius multiplier went with `CampaignMilitaryGates.PersonalityCapitalDefenseNeed`; the one 11712-11741 formula is `VanillaCapitalDefenseNeed`, fed by `CapitalFloorPolicy.VanillaNeed` (believed enemy weighted by confidence, `believedMorale` 1 since a sighting shows no morale, own groups by last waypoint). `CampaignMapLedger.TryGetTown`, orphaned by the capital-town multiplier's removal, is deleted, and so is `DirectorPosture.CapitalDefenseBudgetModifier` with its computation in `StrategicResilienceDirector.ProposePosture` and its one test (`DirectorRaisesCapitalDefenseBudgetUnderTooFastCollapse`): #4 was its only reader, and P7's floor takes no director term (the guard-budget modifier, read by `DefenseIntentRuntime`, stays). Known trade-off, as the plan decided it: the need is computed on beliefs, so when the believed picture and vanilla's omniscient `num2` disagree vanilla may strip the pledge on its next call (one unit per call, only while the allocation holds a CapitalGuard, i.e. a believed capital threat).
7. **Recruit routing.** `TryRouteRecruit(allocation, own, relocationRange, cellX, cellZ, out routeX, out routeZ, out toInstanceId)`: `toInstanceId` is -1 when the carrier is a player formation (position marker). `MaxRouteFactor` is 1.5 and is pinned.
8. **Player formations (G4 verify r2 N1).** A map-group player's formation carrying a Shortfall is a position marker: routes and deficits use `ReserveRules.HoldPoint` (his last waypoint), `DeficitInstanceId`/`toInstanceId` are -1 for him, and any candidate failing `WlChainRules.MayMove` (the player's group, his own command, a CIC side) is dropped from the source side. `ReinforcementRoutingNeverTargetsAPlayerFormation` (plus `RecruitRoutingNeverToPlayerFormation`) pins it, including stale surplus entries naming a player group and a player command.
9. **Third first-fire id.** `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.scope.finalizer` (the scope's Finalizer) joins `.scope` and `.move-unit`, all under the one situational glob `campaign.patch.wl-career-transfer.*`; `campaign.patch.recruit-routing` is situational (only while vanilla recruits and a short formation is in reach). The scope Prefix returns before marking outside a career (the L3 precedent).
10. **Tests removed or changed outside the new file.** `CampaignMilitaryFixTests`: five tests for the deleted helpers (`TransferRequirementIsLocalNotTheaterWide`, `TransferSteersOnlyWhenPlanShortfallIsWorse`, `TransferStrengthReadsVanillaHeatmapGrids`, `CapitalPrePledgeOnlyWhenVanillaAlsoNeedsDefenders`, `CapitalPersonalityNeedDoesNotDoubleCountPledgedDefenders`), superseded by `SteerOnlyWhenPlanShortfallWorse`, `TransferUsesBeliefsNotHeatmapEnemy`, `PledgeOnlyWhileVanillaNeedPositive` and the two `VanillaNeed` tests. `PlayerPositionTests`: the capital-need test now drives `CapitalFloorPolicy.PickPledge`, and the runtime-wiring pin moved from the deleted per-unit `MayMove` line to the candidate's `Chain = ...ChainClassOf(in chain, ...)` line plus the pick's own `MayMove` line. `StrategicMapTests`: two lookups through `Towns` (`TownNamed`). `StrategicDirectorTests`: the capital-budget test goes with the field. `WlCampaignTests` is untouched: `DefensiveOpsPatch.cs` still classifies per unit in a loop over `ownUnits` with the context captured before it.
11. **Not built here.** `docs/patch-catalog.md` rows #3/#4 and the new patches' ordinals (G7 assigns them); the stale-plan retirement (P1: a plan older than 2 review days leaves the patches) is G5a's `CampaignWithholdView` rule, so until G5a these patches read the last published plan like any other reader.

**Verify-g5b revision (2026-10-15; `verify-g5b` on `09a7abd`; integrator decisions F1, F3, F4).** F1: `VanillaNeedCountsEveryChainClassAtItsWaypoint` drives `CapitalFloorPolicy.VanillaNeed` with a `PlayerMapGroup`, a `PlayerCommand` and a `CarryingGroup` record whose waypoint is inside the closest-enemy ring and asserts strength x morale is subtracted (11733-11740), and that a map-group player at the capital leaves `PickPledge` with nothing to pledge; the verifier's plant V01 (a `MayMove` skip in the own loop) is killed. F3 (vanilla's donor is the first top group within the relocation range of the source point, not our formation): `TryTransferPair` takes `donorRadius` (`GamePrefs.aimaximumdistancetosearchforunitrelocations`, the range `GrabOwnTopUnitsNearPosition` 12824-12846 uses when `CheckTransferOfUnits` 17284 passes none; XZ distance to `transform.position`, `<=`) and uses a surplus only where no protected formation stands within it: MainEffort, withheld and not itself a surplus (a withheld Reserve is a legitimate donor), in transit (`LiveFormationReader` now sets `FormationFlags.InTransfer` from `transfertime > 0`, as the snapshot does), or failing `MayMove`. It tries the next surplus and, with none qualifying, writes no swap (`TransferSourceKeepsVanillasDonorPickOffTheFront`). Nine plants, all killed. F4: whole-line `HasLine` pins for the custom-order read, `IsMovedByPlayer` in the 11791 filter, the `activeStrength` contract (reader line and the three call sites), `IsPlayerCICOf` in #3 and #4 (`LiveReadsBehindThePatchesArePinned`); the verifier's V09, V10, V12, V16 are killed. F2 (accepted trade-off, item 6): smoke item 1 below also checks that a `[CapitalFloor] pledged=<unit>` row is followed by that unit moving; a pledged unit that vanilla strips before its move loop (vanilla's num2 <= 0 branch, 11760-11781) shows as no `group to defend capital` movement and is expected rarely. `feat/campaign-planner` `36f07ff` (G3c) is merged in. Verify r2 N1 (integrator): a surplus that is itself in transit is never a source (a marching corps does not donate); N2: the donor loop is 17291-17300 and the main-target steer can still replace the deficit when no pair qualifies. The source self-exclusion (verifier's R09) is deleted as provably unreachable (no dead code): the guard now tests the source itself, at distance 0, by the same predicate as its neighbours (`IsProtectedFromDonation`: unmovable, in transit, MainEffort, CapitalGuard, or withheld and not a listed surplus), which is also what makes a marching, front or unmovable surplus never a source, so the source loop's separate `MayMove`, transit and role checks went with it; a withheld Reserve protects neither itself nor another Reserve because `Allocation.Surpluses` lists it. Ten plants on that predicate and the source preconditions, all killed (a first run left four survivors and led to test cases for a stale front, a capital guard marked withheld or not, another withheld role, an unallocated and an empty source).

Red-first (`evidence/g5b/red-first.txt`): the 28 tests were run against API-only stubs of the three policies and `WlTransferFixDecision.Allow` (the real patch sources present) with a catching `RunAll`: 20 red; the 8 green on the stub are negative-shaped or source pins that hold on the patches already written (`NoSteerWithoutMainEffort`, `SteerReadsNoVanillaEnemyTerm`, `RecruitRoutingOnlyInsideZoneRecruiting`, `PatchesWireTheLivePlanAndBothFlags`, `TransferFinalizerRestoresBothRecords`, `CareerFixIsArgumentOnlyInsideTheTransferScope`, `PolicyFilesArePureAndFogHonestBySource`, `SupersededHelpersAreGone`), each killed by a plant below. The implementation was drafted before the test file (the process slip: the stub run is the red-first evidence, as at G1, G3 and G4).

Mutation plants (scratch copy as working directory, catching `RunAll`, only the three owning test classes run, one plant at a time, restore from a byte copy with a sha256 check; `evidence/g5b/mutation-results.txt`): **56 plants, 55 killed by the named test, 1 equivalent mutant.** Capital need and pledge: believed morale ignored, confidence ignored, position instead of last waypoint, NaN waypoint unguarded, pledge at need 0 or NaN, custom-ordered unit pledged, chain guard dropped, vanilla filters dropped, any role pledged, the farthest guard pledged. Routing and pairs: route factor 3, route range ignored, last instead of nearest, a player id returned as destination, position instead of hold point (route and deficit), the player's id as the pair's destination, the source `MayMove` check dropped, front-line sources allowed, the deficit its own source, a non-positive need accepted, each shortfall term (believed enemy, guards held, garrison floor, sector filter, coverage predicate, strength x morale, a Reserve carrier). ## G5c — Construction steering: depots, forts, railroads (after G5a merges and after `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs` merges)

**Depends on `fix/campaign-logistics-bugs`**, which is fixing vanilla job 19's two bad depot branches on `main` now: (a) an unguarded **enemy** depot admitted as a supply source (`own | allied | !EnemyUnitsInRange`, 14740) and (b) a unit skipped entirely when any own-started depot (built or not) is within command range (14683-14715) — "starving next to an empty depot". G5c builds on that fix and does not re-fix them. Supply depots, forts and telegraph stations have `subsidytype -1` (`buildingtypes.dat` 353, 1094, 1137), so `Place` charges the treasury (`constructioncosts`, 96211-96257), never a subsidy lane (see `docs/findings.md` § subsidy lanes).

**Dead construction-ledger code on `main` this slice wires (no dead code left):** `ConstructionRuntime` never creates a `ConstructionCandidateKind.SupplyDepot` candidate (it creates PrivateBuilding `:133`, Railroad `:172`, Fort `:204`, and telegraph candidates), so `TopSupplyDepot` and the depot clause of `IsEmergencyAllowed` (`ConstructionIntentLedger.cs:139-140`) are dead; `TopRailroad`, `TopFort` and the CSA rail cap (`ConstructionSuppressionReason.CsaRailDoctrineCap`, `:112-115`) are read only by telemetry (`StrategicCoordinator.cs:1532-1535`) because `ConstructionSteeringScorer.SuppressionFor` (`:45`) serves #20's private-building scaling and skips other kinds. Wiring:
- `ConstructionRuntime.BuildInput` creates one `SupplyDepot` candidate per `TheaterPlan.DepotTasks` entry (read from `CampaignPlanStore`); `DepotTaskLedger.Sync` starts a task only when the ledger did not suppress its candidate; for a depot that is `VanillaInvalid` alone (no own IIP to call): the ledger has no fiscal suppression of a depot and the existing emergency allowance lets depots through `EmergencyHold`, as vanilla's own job 19 places its wish with no funds check (14772). `TopSupplyDepot` is the next task to start. (Corrected at G5c verify F5; see G5C as built, item 5.)
- `RailLineScorer` multiplies by the ledger's Railroad candidate score for that line and excludes a line the ledger suppressed (CSA rail cap); `TopRailroad` breaks score ties.
- The fort preference ranks the ledger's Fort candidates; `TopFort` is the preferred site when it is eligible.
- `SuppressionFor` stays private-building-only (its #20 purpose), with a comment naming the G5c consumers of the other kinds.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/DepotTaskLedger.cs` (pure: Marching → Wished → Built | Abandoned), `Strategic/Planner/DepotSteeringRuntime.cs` (runtime), `Strategic/Planner/RailLineScorer.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/RailLineSteering.cs` (pure: the roll loop below), `Patches/RailroadLineSteeringPatch.cs` (new); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (G5a's file: a builder `DepotTaskLedger.HoldsBuilder` holds stays withheld from both pools); modify `Patches/FortConstructionGovernorPatch.cs` (axis site preference; enemy read already on beliefs from G1), `Strategic/Construction/FortConstructionGovernor.cs` (pure site-preference rule), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionRuntime.cs` (depot candidates), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionIntentLedger.cs` (only if a signature change is needed), `Strategic/Construction/ConstructionSteeringScorer.cs` (comment); test `ConstructionSteeringTests.cs`. Plugin: bind `Enable Supply Depot Steering`, `Enable Fort Steering`, `Enable Railroad Steering`.

**Vanilla to read:** `CBuilding.AddConstructionWish` 97479-97536; `Place` 96163-96257 (cost); `WorkDownConstructionWishes` 97538-97591; `IncreaseCondition` 96607-96738 (builder in bugle range 96626-96655); building ids 95083-95121; `CheckSupplyDepotConstruction` 14659-14779; `CheckFortConstruction` 16347-16405, `FortConstructionOrder` 9636-9675, 9814-9865, `fortconstructionsites` 81931-81943; `UpdateRailroadConstruction` 16052-16074; `Railroad.StartConstruction` 77818-77845; `UpdateConstruction` 77859-77893.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public enum DepotTaskState { Marching = 0, Wished = 1, Built = 2, Abandoned = 3 }
public struct DepotTaskRecord { public float X, Z; public int SectorId; public int BuilderInstanceId; /* in-session */ public string BuilderUnitKey; /* persisted */ public DepotTaskState State; public int StartedDay; }
public sealed class DepotTaskLedger { public const int TimeoutDays = 20; public const int MaxActivePerAlliance = 1;
  public void Sync(TheaterPlan plan, Allocation a, int daySerial);
  public bool ShouldWish(int taskIndex, float builderX, float builderZ, float bugleRange, int daySerial);
  public void RecordWished(int taskIndex, int daySerial); public void RecordBuilt(int taskIndex); public void Abandon(int taskIndex, string reason);
  public bool HoldsBuilder(int instanceId);
  public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<DepotTaskRecord> Tasks { get; }
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<DepotTaskRecord> tasks); }
public static class RailLineScorer { public static float Score(in RailLineRecord r, CampaignSnapshot s, TheaterPlan plan, SectorModel sectors); } // by the line's endpoints (G0: `AX..BZ`, `AIipId/BIipId`), not its average point: an endpoint that is an own supply source (`s.Supply` by `Id`) and an endpoint near the main axis or a threatened sector score; main-effort supply reach gain
public static class RailLineSteering { // pure replica of vanilla's loop with the steering choice
  // eligible[j] = progress[j] <= 0 && startable[j] (StartConstruction(a, checkonly: true)), taken once before any start.
  // rolls[j] = the j-th Random.Range(0f, 1f) draw (one per line, in line order, as 16069 draws before testing progress).
  // Returns the started line indices (one per success on an eligible line; none for a success on an ineligible line).
  public static int[] Choose(bool[] eligible, float[] rolls, float probabilityPerCall, float[] score); }
```
Depot executor: the DepotBuilder (G4) marches (G5a mover); on arrival within its `buglerange` of the task site, `CBuilding.AddConstructionWish(CBuilding.id_supplydepot, site, nearestOwnIip, alliance)` once (P8), skipped when the IIP already has `currentlyunderconstruction` (97479); `Place` charges the treasury with no funds check (`AdjustTreasury`, 96211-96257; depots have `subsidytype -1`), so Whiskey mirrors vanilla's job 19 (14772): there is no affordability test, and the exposure is bounded by one active task per alliance, one wish per task and the 20-day retry cooldown (integrator ruling at G5c verify F5); the builder stays withheld (`CampaignWithholdView`, via `DepotTaskLedger.HoldsBuilder`) until the depot's `constructiontimer <= 0` (`Built`) or `TimeoutDays` (`Abandoned`). Telemetry `[DepotTask] alliance=<a> site=<x>,<z> builder=<unit> state=<Marching|Wished|Built|Abandoned> reason=<…>`. Vanilla's job 19 is otherwise unchanged (its two bug branches are the bug-fix slice's).
Fort steering: #27's filter additionally hides, for the call, `fortconstructionsites` farther than `rangefortconstructionsite` from every MainEffort axis / Hold-or-Screen sector boundary **when at least one axis/threatened site is otherwise eligible** (snapshot-restore as today); vanilla's one-order-per-alliance rule and builder logic are untouched.
Railroad steering (C11 option a, approved by the user 2026-10-15): Prefix (bool) on `UpdateRailroadConstruction(int alliance, float timediff)`. It lets vanilla run (result `true`) for the player's alliance outside `ai_vs_ai`, for alliance ids of 2 and above, for the W&L CIC player's alliance, while vanilla's job machine is paused (`ExecutorsPaused`, 11368: vanilla keeps rolling railroads on the alliance tick, so its own roll runs), with no published plan, or with the flag off. Otherwise: (1) compute vanilla's probability `num` exactly as 16058-16066; (2) before any start, take `eligible[j] = constructionprogress ≤ 0 && StartConstruction(alliance, checkonly: true)` for every line (vanilla starts only the line it is rolling, so at line `j`'s roll the state of `j` equals the state at entry; 77818-77831 refuses a complete line, unowned IIPs and a non-permitted line); (3) draw `Random.Range(0f, 1f)` once per line in line order, as 16069 does before it tests progress; (4) a success on an ineligible line starts nothing, as in vanilla; a success on an eligible line starts the highest-scored eligible line not yet started in this call (line `j` itself when none scores higher) with `StartConstruction(alliance)`; (5) skip vanilla. So every success on an eligible line starts exactly one line, every started line is one vanilla could start, and the number and timing of starts equal vanilla's for the same draws (`RailLineSteering.Choose` holds the decision; the patch only reads state and calls `StartConstruction`). Header comment carries the spec's written justification. First-fire `campaign.patch.railroad-steering`. Telemetry `[RailroadSteer] alliance=<a> rolled=<j> started=<k> score=<s>`.

**Steps:**
- [x] Tests first: `DepotWishOnlyOnArrivalWithinBugleRange`, `DepotWishOncePerTask`, `DepotBuilderHeldUntilBuilt`, `DepotAbandonedAfterTimeout`, `OneActiveDepotTaskPerAlliance`, `DepotBuilderWithheldWhileHeld`, `DepotTasksKeyedByBuilderUnitKey`, `FortPreferenceOnlyWhenAxisSiteEligible`, `FortPreferenceKeepsVanillaSitesOtherwise`, `RailScorePrefersMainAxis`, `RailScoreUsesEndpointsNotAverage` (two lines with one average point: the one linking own supply to the main axis wins), `RailSteeringPreservesSuccessCount` (`RailLineSteering.Choose` against a pure replica of vanilla's 16067-16073 loop over the same seeded draws; the fixture includes a line with unowned IIPs, a non-permitted line, a line already under construction and a complete line, all ineligible, plus a case where the best line is started before its own index is rolled and then rolls a success itself; the start counts are equal in every seed), `RailSuccessOnIneligibleLineStartsNothing`, `RailEachLineStartedAtMostOncePerCall`, `RailSteeringStandsDownForPlayerAndEurope`, `RailSteeringStandsDownWhileJobMachinePaused`, `DepotCandidateCreatedFromPlanDepotTask`, `DepotTaskSuppressedByFiscalPostureExceptEmergency`, `CsaRailCapExcludesLineFromSteering`, `TopRailroadBreaksScoreTies`, `TopFortPreferredWhenEligible`.
- [x] Implement; gates; commits `697ea39` (pure half), `10a17a6` (runtime half, patches, flags), `59ad9e2` (cases the mutation round asked for).

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (`[DepotTask]` Marching → Wished → Built for one task; `[RailroadSteer]` rows only for AI alliances; #27 `[Patch:FortGovernor]` lines unchanged in shape).

### G5C as built (2026-10-16)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g5c` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g5c`) from `feat/campaign-planner` `7d2a2cd`, built by the `build` seat (claude-sonnet-5-5, high) in parallel with G5d on disjoint files; `feat/campaign-planner` `9b2cfa0` (G5d merged) was merged into it by a merge commit before the third verify round's fixes, so F7 is built against the shared `MoveTracker` and mover as they ship. Commits `697ea39` (pure half, red-first), `10a17a6` (runtime half, the railroad Prefix, the fort filter, flags), `59ad9e2` (the cases the mutation round asked for), `2a21ee7` (this paragraph), `e9d871a`, `fa59fda`, the verify round's F1-F6 fixes (`1af9412`, `05c62b8`), the second verify round's F6a-F6c fixes (`6a0bc19`), the wish-position fix (`1dc88f3`), the merge, the third verify round's F7-F9 fixes (`62a5df1`), the G3 ruling on the held set (`7b1812d`), the behaviour test for its capital mask (`b7f1920`), and the fourth verify round's F10 and F11 (the commit after it). Harness 3277 PASS (3266 at the merge with `9b2cfa0`: 3133 at `7d2a2cd`, G5d's 63 and G5c's 70; plus 11 in `ConstructionSteeringTests`), `./build.sh` and the test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (159 entries: the three new flags), orphan-sweep 0 and `--include-test-only` 31 -> 31 (the rows are identical to `9b2cfa0`'s after stripping line numbers), `test_smoke_check.py` OK. No Transpiler; the one Prefix-skip is the user-approved `RailroadLineSteeringPatch` (P14, C11). Mutation round: 165 plants (77 before the first verify round, 20 added for F1-F6, 22 for F6a-F6c, 7 for the wish position, 22 for F7-F9, 10 for the G3 ruling, 7 for F10-F11), 7 survived the first pass, 1 did not compile, 1 (C4) survived the wish-position round and 1 (K22) the third, all closed by named tests, all 165 killed in one full run on the final tree (`evidence/g5c/mutation-r11-all.txt`, `plants.json`, scratch copy as cwd, byte-copy restore, sha256 checked).

**Where the plan was silent, wrong or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. **The call site.** G5c's owned list names none for `DepotSteeringRuntime`, and `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewExecute` is G5d's file. The executor is called from `CampaignSteeringRuntime.Execute` (G5a's file, which `ReviewExecute` already reaches behind the pre-war gate and the today's-plan check, ahead of the mover), first statement, with its own scope `campaign.planner.construction` (2.0). `ReviewExecuteWiringPinned` and a new pin (`DepotExecutorRidesTheSteeringExecutorBehindTheGates`) hold the order. `DepotSteeringRuntime.Execute` has its own try/catch with a static-key OnceLog warning, so a fault there cannot idle the steering and the mover for the review (G5c verify F4, `DepotExecutorFaultCannotIdleTheMover`); the integrator may move the call into `ReviewExecute` proper at G6/G7.
2. **Files the owned list did not name.** `Planner/ConstructionSteeringBoard.cs` (pure: the per-alliance picture the two patches read), `ConstructionModels.cs` (`ConstructionCandidate.SiteIndex`, `ConstructionSuppression.SiteIndex`, `ConstructionOutput.SiteCandidates`), `ConstructionIntentLedger.cs` (copies the index into a suppression, keeps every scored Railroad and Fort candidate), `LogisticsGate.cs` (`Supplies` made `internal`: one rule for "an own supply source"), `Plugin.cs`, the test csproj (four `Compile Include` lines, in the Construction block); at the third verify round, with the integrator's authority, `ForceAllocator.cs`, `ReserveRules.cs`, `TheaterPlanner.cs` and `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (the `ReviewPlan` calls) for the pin and the held set. The ledger kept only the top candidate per kind, so "multiplies by the ledger's Railroad candidate score for that line" and "excludes a line the ledger suppressed" were not computable without the index and the full list.
3. **Rail and fort data path.** The patches run on the alliance tick, outside the review, where only `CampaignPlanStore` is published and the snapshot is private. `DepotSteeringRuntime` builds a `ConstructionBoard` each review (rail score per line index, fort preferred flag and rank per site index) from today's snapshot, plan, sectors and the last construction pass, and swaps it in whole (`ConstructionSteeringBoard.Publish`, `CampaignScope` reset, read by `TryRead` under the same `PlanFreshness` rule as a plan). A rail line's score is `RailLineScorer` (own supply 0.35, axis closeness 0.30, threatened sector 0.15, reach gain 0.20; by the line's endpoints) times the ledger's candidate score for that line; a suppressed line (CSA rail cap) is `ExcludedRailScore` (-1: it still starts when it is all that vanilla could start, because the number of starts is vanilla's); the ledger's `TopRailroad` / `TopFort` carries `TopBonus` 0.001 so it wins an otherwise equal score. `RailLineSteering.Choose` ties: strictly better first, then the rolled line, then the lowest index.
4. **`DepotTaskLedger.Sync(plan, allocation, snapshot, construction)`** replaces the plan's `Sync(plan, a, daySerial)`: the snapshot carries the day, the unit keys (`IndexOfKey`, the rebinding after a restore) and the chain classes, and `construction` is the ledger's verdict. `StartedDay` is the day the current state began (the start, then the wish), so `TimeoutDays` (20) bounds the march and the construction separately. `ShouldWish(i, x, z, bugleRange)` takes no day and `RecordBuilt(i, day)` takes one (G5c verify F3). Added surface: `Abandon(i, reason, day)` (the only abandon: every one carries its day), `AbandonActive` (the flag turned off releases every held builder), `SiteBuilt`, `SiteHasOwnDepot`, `NearestOwnDepot`, `WatchWish`, `CollectPinnedBuilders` (`PinnedBuilder`), `CandidateName`, `WishMoment`, `Transitions` for the `[DepotTask]` rows, `DepotTaskLedgers.For(alliance)` (per campaign, as `MoveTrackers`), `DepotCandidates.Add`. A site that timed out is not restarted for `RetryAfterAbandonDays` (20; a wish costs treasury each time, `Place` pays, 96211); the record carries `EndedDay` (-1 while active), so the cooldown is a pure day difference that a save and a reload keep (G5c verify F2), and `WishX`/`WishZ`, where the wish was placed (below), and at most `MaxFinishedRecords` (8) finished records are kept. With `MaxActivePerAlliance` 1 the second task's builder is still assigned and marched by G4/G5a and idles at its site until the first task is built or abandoned.
5. **No fiscal suppression of depots exists, and none was added.** `ConstructionIntentLedger.IsSuppressed` lets a SupplyDepot candidate through every fiscal posture (`IsEmergencyAllowed` names depots; the credit-defense and rail-cap clauses are kind-specific); the only suppression a depot can have is `VanillaInvalid`. The plan's `DepotTaskSuppressedByFiscalPostureExceptEmergency` therefore has nothing to assert; `DepotTaskStartFollowsTheLedgersVerdict` asserts what is true (every posture, emergency and the bond floor included, lets a valid depot through; a candidate with no own IIP to call is suppressed and its task never starts; no verdict yet suppresses nothing). A depot candidate is `VanillaValid` when `ConstructionRuntime.NearestOwnIip` finds a visible own IIP without a building under construction (97481-97484).
6. **Depot verdicts are taken fresh.** The construction pass runs weekly or on a map/front/formation/fiscal change and does not see the plan, so its candidates lag the day a task appears. `BuildInput` (weekly) now also creates the plan's depot candidates (wiring the ledger's dead `SupplyDepot` kind and `TopSupplyDepot`), and the executor takes its own verdict each review with `ConstructionRuntime.BuildDepotInput` (fiscal and pressure facts and today's depot candidates, no private-building, railroad or fort scan, and no era stage: decision 7). `TopSupplyDepot` is the next task to start; a top task without a builder yields to the first one that has one.
7. **The wish and the window before it (G5c verify F6).** `DepotTaskLedger.WishMoment` decides, the runtime acts. *Here*: the builder is within `max(buglerange, CampaignMover.ArrivalRadius)` of the site (the mover does not re-march a formation within its arrival radius of a site that moved with the frontier): the wish goes at the builder's own position, as vanilla's depot job passes the unit's (14770). *Adopt*: there, already flagged `VanillaDepotBuilder` (vanilla's job 19 is already raising an own-started depot within its command range, 14695-14712, listed at 14778) **and** an own depot still rising stands within `buildingsitemaxradius` + `CampaignMover.ArrivalRadius` of the site **and** within the builder's own bugle range (`DepotTaskLedger.NearestOwnDepot(..., unbuiltOnly: true, builder position, bugle range)`: a depot rises only while an own unit stands within that unit's `buglerange` of it, `IncreaseCondition` 96626-96655, and the range is strict; verify r3 F8): the task becomes Wished (`vanilla-depot-adopted`) with no second wish. The flag is set by any own-started depot within the builder's command range, 50-100 for a corps or an army, which need not be this site's; a flagged builder with no depot at the site gets its own wish (second verify round, F6b). *Ahead*: `MarchTime.EstimateDays` to the site is at most `AheadDays` (1, one review day) **and** the mover's tracker shows the builder marching to this site on a move issued at an earlier review (`MoveTracker.MarchingToward`: the target within `SiteMatchRadius` of the site, state Issued or Confirmed, issued before today) **and** the task did not start today: the wish goes at the site now, while the builder still marches. The wish is paid at once (`AddConstructionWish` -> `Place`, 97506, 96211-96257) and a depot with no own unit within its bugle range is removed after `timetoremovemilitarybuildings` (`IncreaseCondition`, 96626-96648; campaignprefs 546-547), so an arrival estimate alone, a lower bound that terrain, a retreat or another order can break, does not pay for a wish (F6a). The mover runs after the executors in a review, so at executor time the move issued at the previous review is still Issued or Confirmed on the tracker; a task that starts today has no earlier move of its own and never gets Ahead. *Wait* otherwise. Why: job 19 (`CheckSupplyDepotConstruction` 14659-14779) does not read the availability Postfixes. For an idle formation (`regimentpaths <= 0`) with low supply (`GetGroupSupplyState < supplystatedepotconstruction`), no own-started depot within its command range (`flag`) and no depot connection it `MoveUnitTo`s the nearest own town or depot beyond its bugle range (14762-14767), or wishes a depot itself at the unit's position (14770-14772) when no such installation is in range (`val == null`, `!flag2`). So a builder that arrived mid-day and stood idle until the next review could be marched off before its wish; the hold in the availability Postfixes cannot stop that. The window is closed from both sides: the wish is placed before arrival (a marching unit is skipped, 14715), and once the depot stands in its command range the unit is skipped (`flag`, 14715). The fort job (21, `CheckFortConstruction` 16347-16405) is the other vanilla mover of an idle unit that reads no availability test: `FortConstructionGovernorPatch` hides every in-range site for a held builder, so it orders no fort (`HeldDepotBuilderIsNotSentToBuildAFort`), and asks for the hold through `CampaignWithholdView.HoldsDepotBuilder`, which is the withhold view's own picture test (`Read`: a published plan and allocation, executors allowed, the plan fresh) followed by the same `HeldBuilder` helper the offense and defense clauses use, so a stale plan or the pre-war gate never holds a builder from the fort job either (`DepotHoldReachesTheFortJobOnlyUnderAFreshPlan`, second verify round F6c); it excludes a unit only once its depot stands (`unitsconstructingsupplydepots`, 16376-16381). Left to vanilla by G5a's decision: the defensive operation executor (13744-13771) takes any fighting force near an enemy, and the capital defence (11862); both are vanilla's reaction to a threat. The returned `CBuilding` is the placeholder at -20000 that `WorkDownConstructionWishes` replaces (97538-97591), so "built" is read from the snapshot's depots: a built own or allied depot within `buildingsitemaxradius` + the arrival radius (`builtRadius`) of the **wish position** (`DepotTaskLedger.SiteBuilt`). The depot rises on the nearest suitable ground to where the wish was placed, up to `buildingsitemaxradius` away (97557-97585), and the wish is placed at the builder (`Here`: up to its bugle range from the site, a corps' 70 or more), at the site (`Ahead`) or at the adopted depot (`Adopt`), so the task record keeps `WishX`/`WishZ` (set by `RecordWished`, persisted with `EndedDay` in the G6 sidecar record) and Built, `wish-lost` and the Adopt choice all look around those, not around the site. A Wished task is watched at every review (`DepotTaskLedger.WatchWish`): Built when such a depot stands, and Abandoned as `wish-lost` (the builder released at once) when, on a later review than the wish, no own depot at all, built or still rising, stands within `builtRadius` of the wish position: the wish was cancelled with no site found (`WorkDownConstructionWishes`, 97538-97591) or the depot was removed unattended (96626-96648). The wish's own day is not judged, because until `WorkDownConstructionWishes` runs (once per AI update, 11201) the building is the placeholder at -20000. `DepotIsLookedForWhereTheWishWasPlaced` pins a wish placed 70 from the site whose depot rises 100 from it (Built, not `wish-lost`), the depot at the wish position vanishing (`wish-lost`), and a depot near the site alone (not this wish's, so `wish-lost`). *Adopt* records the position of the nearest own unbuilt depot within `builtRadius` of the site (`NearestOwnDepot`). With the IIP: the nearest own one with no building under construction (a wish at one returns that building and places nothing, 97481-97484); a null building (no IIP, `debug_maxcompanyfoundings`) abandons the task. `HoldsBuilder` (Marching and Wished) is read by `CampaignWithholdView` in both availability Postfixes before the allocation lookup (`DepotBuilderHeldButNotAllocatedIsWithheld`), because a depot rises only while a unit stays within its bugle range (96626-96669); once the depot is being raised, job 19 may list the builder as `VanillaDepotBuilder` and `TheaterPlanner.Busy` then keeps it Hold in place, but that flag is not relied on (below).
   **The pin (verify r3 F7).** Nothing on Whiskey's own side read the ledger: the allocator re-picks the depot builder every review (`SmallestIn`), so a plan that dropped the task, a moved site or a smaller formation entering the sector could free the builder of a paid wish, and the mover would march it off (an Ahead builder is never flagged `VanillaDepotBuilder` while it marches, 14690; a Here builder is unflagged until job 19's next pass). The rule, in one place: `DepotTaskLedger.CollectHeldBuilders` lists the bound builder of every active task (`HeldBuilder`); a Wished one is flagged and carries its wish position (for an Ahead wish that is the site its builder is still marching to), a Marching one carries the site; `ReviewPlan` hands the list to `ForceAllocator.Allocate` (new optional last argument); the allocator's first step (`AssignPinnedBuilders`, before the immobile and every reserve) gives each Wished formation DepotBuilder at that target, withheld, until the ledger stops listing it (Built, `wish-lost`, timeout, builder-lost, the flag off), whatever the plan does. A formation the planner may not move (the W&L map-group player, the player's own command, a CIC side) is skipped there, and `Sync` abandons a task whose builder is one, so a pin never touches them (`PinnedBuilderListsOnlyWishedTasksOfMovableFormations`, `PinnedBuilderIsNeverTheMapGroupPlayerAndIsNoReserve`). `ReserveRules.WishedBuilders` is the one rule for who is pinned (Wished, and one the planner may move): the allocator's pin and `ReserveRules.Capital`'s mask both read it, so a pinned builder is not counted as a capital guard it can raise, in G4 and in G3 alike (the hold reserve counts it at its strength either way). The mover is unchanged and reads no depot state (`AllocatorAndPlannerWiringPinsTheWishedBuilders` fails if `CampaignMover` names one): a builder already at its wish has its waypoint at the target and gets no move; an Ahead builder's target is the site its tracker entry names, so the march stands. `WishedBuilderKeepsItsRoleWhateverThePlanDoes` and `AheadBuilderStaysOnItsMarchToTheSite` run the real `ForceAllocator.Allocate` and the real `CampaignMover.Review`, each with an unpinned control that re-tasks the builder and moves it off. Two wiring pins of the `Allocate` call text (`ReviewPlanPassesTheTrackersGaveUpSet`, `ForceAllocatorTests`) were updated for the new argument; G4's allocator behaviour tests are unchanged. **G3 reads the same set (integrator ruling).** `ReviewPlan` collects the held set once, ahead of the plan, and hands it to `TheaterPlanner.Plan` (new optional last argument) as well as to the allocator: `MassFor` skips a held builder, Marching or Wished, so the mass the odds are judged on (and `MassStrength`, `MassInstanceIds` and the main force picked from it) is the force that can march, and the main effort G4 assigns is exactly that mass. `WishedBuilderIsNoCapitalGuardInG3sPlan` runs the real `Plan` with a capital in the theater: the formation nearest the capital is its guard and out of the mass, a Marching builder there is still raised as the guard, and a Wished builder there is not counted in the capital reserve, so the next nearest formation is the guard instead. `HeldBuilderIsNotInTheMassG3Judges` runs the real `Plan` on one theater with and without a held builder (the mass differs by exactly the builder's strength and one formation, Marching and Wished alike, an unknown id changes nothing) and then the real `Allocate` over that plan (MainEffort is the planned mass, the builder is the depot's); G3's behaviour tests are unchanged, and the one wiring pin of the `Plan` call line in `CampaignExecutorTests.cs` took the new argument.
   **Pending placement (verify r3 F9).** `WorkDownConstructionWishes` serves one shared queue, `aiplacements[0]`, `amountofconstructionsitesearches` terrain probes per AI update (11201), so a wish behind others can still be the placeholder at (-20000) across a review. `WatchWish` therefore waits while an own unbuilt depot stands at the placeholder (`PlacementPending`: owner the alliance, not built, x and z below -19000) instead of reporting `wish-lost`; the wait is bounded by `TimeoutDays`, which `Sync` applies to every active task (`APendingPlacementIsNotALostWish`). Any own placeholder counts, not only this task's: the snapshot cannot tell them apart, and the timeout bounds it.
   **Builder re-tasked (verify r4 F10).** When the capital reserve (or a Recover) takes a Marching task's builder and no other free formation of the sector can take the depot job, the allocation names no DepotBuilder at the site; `FollowPlan` then ends the task at once as `builder-retasked` and removes its record. Nothing was paid before the wish, so no cooldown applies (a finished record would start one), and the formation is no longer held (`HoldsBuilder`, the withhold view, G3's held set); the site is tried again at the next review that offers a builder. `CapitalReserveRetaskingAMarchingBuilderReleasesTheTask` runs the real `Plan` (a believed enemy near the capital gives it a floor), the real `Allocate` and `Sync` over three reviews.
   **One task, one builder (verify r4 F11).** The plan keeps listing a depot task until the depot stands, so while a wish is a pending placeholder (or rising) `AssignDepotBuilders` would send a second formation to the same site. `HeldBuilder` now carries the task's site (`SiteX`, `SiteZ`); the allocator skips a plan task within `SiteMatchRadius` of a pinned Wished builder's site (a pin it ignored, the map-group player's, does not count). `PlanTaskDrawsNoSecondBuilderWhileOneIsPinned`.
8. **Railroad Prefix.** As the plan, with: `RailLineSteering.ShouldSteer` (the stand-down set as a pure rule: flags, vanilla's 16054 inputs, the W&L commander-in-chief's alliance, the live job-machine pause, a fresh board), `RolledLines` (the k-th start answers the k-th rolled line, for `[RailroadSteer] alliance=<a> rolled=<j> started=<k> score=<s>`), an assertion (left on) that a success on an eligible line always finds an eligible line left, and the order: gate, eligibility (`StartConstruction(checkonly: true)` only), chance, scores (all before any side effect; any failure returns true and vanilla rolls), then one `UnityEngine.Random.Range(0f, 1f)` per line, then the starts, and from the first draw the Prefix always skips vanilla. `StartConstruction` draws no random number (its body 77833-77845 and what it calls were read: `UpdateCampaignIcon(forcehiding: true)` 188262-188359, which returns at its first statement for a railroad's symbol, and `HidePanel` 207853-207891), so draw-all-first leaves vanilla's stream and starts unchanged. First-fire `campaign.patch.railroad-steering` fires on every alliance tick (not situational). The ledger's suppression and a stale board only change which line starts, never how many.
9. **Fort preference.** A site is preferred when it lies within `rangefortconstructionsite` of the MainEffort axis (the segment from the mean position of the planned mass, the capital when none of it is in the snapshot, to the main sector's target) or stands in a Hold or Screen sector (the plan's "sector boundary" is read as the sector the site is in). The patch puts only preferred, in-range, governor-allowed sites through vanilla's own per-site tests (`GetClosestBuilding` x0.6 for a capital defender, `CheckIfPositionIsCloseToFrontline`, `IsUnitAbleToConstructFort`, and the defender's distance rule), asks `FortSitePreference.Choose` for the one highest-ranked eligible site and hides every other in-range site for the call; with none eligible nothing extra is hidden; out-of-range sites stay (vanilla rejects them itself). `Enable Fort Steering` is independent of `Fort Construction Governor Enabled` (either runs without the other). One bounded row `[ConstructionTelemetry] alliance=<a> reason=planner-axis-preference site=<i>` (its own set of 16); the governor's rows are unchanged.
10. **Flags** (all default true, section "Campaign Planner", each read by its consumer, each needing `Enable Campaign Planner` and `Enable Campaign Planner Executors`): `Enable Supply Depot Steering`, `Enable Fort Steering`, `Enable Railroad Steering`. Not edited, by the plan's rule: `docs/patch-catalog.md`, `docs/handoff.md` (the integrator assigns ordinals at G7).

**Census (to add at G7).** `DepotTaskLedger` records (`DepotTaskRecord`: site, sector, builder unit key, state, state day, end day, wish position) | G5c executor; G6 sidecar v3 `planner.depotTasks` (the ledger is in memory until then; `Restore` rebinds by unit key at the next Sync and keeps the end day, so the retry cooldown survives a reload); `ConstructionOutput.SiteCandidates`, `ConstructionCandidate.SiteIndex`, `ConstructionSuppression.SiteIndex` | `ConstructionSteeringBoard.Build`; `ConstructionBoard` | `RailroadLineSteeringPatch`, `FortConstructionGovernorPatch`; `LogisticsGate.Supplies` (internal) | `RailLineScorer`; `DepotTaskLedgers` | `CampaignWithholdView`, `DepotSteeringRuntime`.

**G5c smoke items (with G7's smoke):** (a) `[DepotTask]` rows for one task in order `task-started` (Marching) -> `wish-placed` (Wished) -> `built`; read the days between `wish-placed` and `built` against `TimeoutDays` 20: a depot that needs longer than 20 days is abandoned and its builder released before it stands, which is a finding for the constant; `wish-refused`, `builder-lost`, `plan-dropped` and `timeout` rows are findings to read; (b) `[first-fire] campaign.patch.railroad-steering` in every session, `[RailroadSteer]` rows only for AI alliances and never for the player's alliance outside `ai_vs_ai`, with `rolled != started` on some rows (the choice is working) and the number of rows per year near vanilla's chance; (c) `[ConstructionTelemetry] ... reason=planner-axis-preference` rows with a fort order following on the chosen site, and #27's `[ConstructionTelemetry] ... reason=hard-cap|saturated-low-threat` rows unchanged in shape; (d) rollback: each of the three flags false gives vanilla's depot job only / vanilla's list order / vanilla's roll; (e) a W&L career: no builder is the player's command, and the CIC player's alliance gets no `[RailroadSteer]` row under `ai_vs_ai`; (g) repeated `task-started` rows for one site with no `wish-placed` between them (a builder vanilla's job 19 or another vanilla job keeps walking off before the wish: the `Ahead` and `Adopt` moments and the fort hold are meant to prevent it; `vanilla-depot-adopted` rows show job 19 raising the depot itself); (i) a Wished builder that is moved or re-tasked before `built` or `wish-lost` (its `[Allocation]` row losing a `depot` count while the task is Wished) is a finding against the pin; (h) a `wish-lost` row: a plain check that the depot is gone, not a tuning question. Read it against the `wish-placed` or `vanilla-depot-adopted` row before it: the wish was cancelled with no site found (`cancelling building placement` in the game log) or the depot was removed unattended (`removed ... as no friendly unit was close`); a `wish-lost` row with a depot visibly standing within the placement radius of where the builder stood at the wish would be a finding; (f) a `builder-lost` or `plan-dropped` row right after a `wish-placed` row is the signature of the window between the wish and `WorkDownConstructionWishes` placing the real building, in which the builder is not yet flagged `VanillaDepotBuilder` and a plan that drops the task lets the allocator reassign it.
