## G5d — Reconnaissance executor (after G5a merges)

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/ReconExecutorRuntime.cs` (runtime), consume `Strategic/Planner/ReconOrders.cs` (built in G1b's pure half with its tests, see G1b as built; pure `internal static class`, because its methods take F's internal `WlChainClass`: `CavalryOrderFor(ReconKind kind, ReconState state, float cavalryShare, WlChainClass chain)` returning the `ChangeCampaignOrders` code 121 (scouting) or 120 (no cavalry order), or −1 for no call; `MayAct(WlChainClass chain)`); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` (executor step after G5a's mover), `Strategic/OperationalProbeRuntime.cs` (transitional: `Run` returns at once while the recon executor is active, so the old probe never moves a formation beside the new probe missions; G6 deletes the file); test additions in `ReconPlannerTests.cs` for the pure helpers it adds (`ReconOrders.CavalryOrderFor`, `ReconOrders.MayAct`).

**Vanilla to read:** `MoveUnitTo` 14479-14540; `ChangeCampaignOrders` 90720-90770 (it returns at once when `cavalryorders` already equals the new order, 90738, so a repeat call is free) and the queued apply 125409-125412; `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel` 92572-92620 (level −1 reaches the group and every descendant three levels down); `SetCavalryOrders` 122074-122082; `UpdateUnitStatusCampaign` 119914-119935; reinforcement delay 67742-67744; readiness reset 113960; W&L 8271, 8366, 14381-14456; stage F's dispatcher and `MoveUnitTo` Postfix.

**Executor (inside `ReviewExecute`, `Enable Campaign Planner Executors` and `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance` on):**
- Moves go through G5a's `MoveTracker` (same confirm/retry/give-up rules, P8) with a separate budget `MaxReconMovesPerDay 3` so recon never starves the main effort; target validation as G5a (`CheckIfFinalWaypointIsPassable` semantics).
- A cavalry-majority screen gets cavalry order 1 (Scouting) — something vanilla's AI never does — through the player's own order path, `bunits.ChangeCampaignOrders(reg, 121)` (90720-90770: immediate `PerformActionForGroupForAllUnitsOnLevel(…, -1, "SetCavalryOrders", 1)` at 90758 without order delays, else queued and applied at 125409-125412), and `120` when recalled or cleared (`ReconOrders.CavalryOrderFor`). **Never on a group that carries the W&L player:** level −1 sends `SetCavalryOrders` to the group and every descendant (92572-92620), so a `CarryingGroup` screen would put order 1 on the player's own brigade or regiments, which also delays their arrival as battle reinforcements (orders 1 and 3, 67742-67744). Setting the order on individual non-player regiments instead would not work: the scouting intel term tests the **group's** own `cavalryorders` (120424-120426) and the "Scouting" status is the group's (119923). So `CavalryOrderFor` returns −1 (no call) for `CarryingGroup`: that screen moves and views like any other, without the order. Effects are vanilla's: "Scouting" status (119923), intel → readiness (120424-120426, 115017/115306), scouting perk experience (115368-115371), later arrival as a battle reinforcement (67742-67744). It does not widen sight; position does. Pickets and advance guards get no cavalry order.
- Recalled missions move back to the hold point the ledger gives; contact is judged from beliefs and `inbattle` only (never from `closestenemyunitfarreg` or enemy destinations, which vanilla's raid abort uses, 9099). Moves are plain `MoveUnitTo(…, true)` (no forced march; screens halt at night like any group, 133958-133961).
- W&L: never the player's own command (`ReconOrders.MayAct` refuses the `PlayerCommand` chain class, re-read live with `ChainClassOf` before acting); a `CarryingGroup` used as a screen is moved normally, F's `MoveUnitTo` Postfix dispatches, and it gets no cavalry order; a `PlayerMapGroup` formation is never a recon pick (G4 verify r3), so the executor issues it no recon order; works for the player's AI side under `ai_vs_ai` (its detection is `OwnSideWithinFog`).
- **#15 stand-down (from G1b as built, item 15; the old probe and package layers are already guarded by the G1b runtime step, F6):** a formation on a recon mission gets directive Screen, which `FormationDirectiveRuntime.ShouldAllowAreaMovement` still allows, so #15 (`ArmyAreaTheaterPatch`, deleted in G6) could move a screen off its post. Until G6, a formation with an active recon mission is refused #15's area moves (the P10 stand-down: `ShouldAllowAreaMovement` returns false for `FormationDirective.Screen`, or #15 checks `ReconMissionLedger.IsRecon`); test `ReconFormationStandsDownFromAreaMoves`. G6 removes the branch with #15.
- Telemetry `ReconMission` per transition: `[ReconMission] alliance=<a> unit=<name> kind=<Screen|Picket|AdvanceGuard|Probe> state=<Moving|Holding|Recalled|Cleared> reason=<…>` (signature-deduped).

- **From the G1b runtime step (as built):** the executor reads the per-alliance `ReconMissionLedger` (`CampaignPlannerRuntime.Recon[alliance]`, private) and the day's tasks; G5d adds the one internal accessor it needs (G1b added none: no reader existed). The Execute job reads the working copy, as the Plan and Formation jobs do; the moment a **patch** reads `IsRecon` (the #15 stand-down) the ledger must join `PrepareDay` (`PublishedDay<T>`) and `ReconLedgerHasNoReaderOutsideTheReview` (which fails on any `Patches/` file naming the ledger, the filter or `ReconStep`) is edited in the same commit. Prefer the stand-down inside `FormationDirectiveRuntime.ShouldAllowAreaMovement` (it already reads the directive ledger, which is published atomically) over a patch reading the ledger. G5d also updates the `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance` description (Plugin.cs), which says missions are planned and marked only until this executor ships, and the `ReconMission.ObservedEnemyStrength` field belongs to the sidecar DTO (G6).

**Steps:**
- [x] Tests first: `ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens`, `RecallClearsScoutingOrder`, `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand` (`CavalryOrderFor` is −1 for `CarryingGroup`, `PlayerMapGroup` and `PlayerCommand` in every kind and state), `NoScoutingOrderOnGroupContainingPlayerCommand` (the executor issues no `ChangeCampaignOrders` call for a `CarryingGroup` screen and still moves it), `ReconBudgetSeparateFromMainEffort` (MoveTracker with two budgets), `PlayerCommandRefusedLive` (pure decision `ReconOrders.MayAct(chain)`), `ReconFormationStandsDownFromAreaMoves` (#15 stand-down until G6). (`ScoutingOrderOnlyForCavalryScreens`, `RecallClearsScoutingOrder`, `ScoutingOrderNeverReachesPlayerCommand` and `PlayerCommandRefusedLive` were written with `ReconOrders` in G1b's pure half.)
- [x] Implement; scope `campaign.planner.recon-execute` (2.0); gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): reconnaissance executor [G5d]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness; in-game at G7 (smoke items 1.10-1.12 and Session B/C recon items).

### G5D as built (2026-10-16)

Branch `feat/campaign-planner-g5d` (worktree `.worktrees/cp-g5d`) from `feat/campaign-planner` `7d2a2cd`. The full record is `scratchpad/loop/stageG/build-g5d.md`. Commits `c3f72d7` (MoveIssue, second budget), `1e3cf19` (VanillaMoveHost), `cd645cf` (the executor), then the test and documentation commits. Harness 3196 PASS (3133 base + 63 new), `./build.sh` and the test build 0 warnings, config-audit 0 (156 entries), orphan-sweep 0, `--include-test-only` 34 -> 31 (`ReconOrders`, `CavalryOrderFor`, `MayAct` gain their reader; nothing is added), `test_smoke_check.py` OK, the allocation and transpiler-label tests pass. No new patch, no new flag, no new first-fire id, no Prefix-skip or Transpiler. Mutation round: 63 plants at the first build, 25 more for verify r1's revise and 17 for verify r2's (109 in all; 2 retired as superseded, 1 equivalent (T04, item 13), the other 106 killed on the final tree; 13 first survivors fixed by tests), `evidence/g5d/mutation-results*.txt`. Verify r1 (REVISE on `9adf9a4`: F1-F3, N1-N4) is answered in items 9-12 and verify r2 (REVISE on `86ff965`: R1, R2, N3) in item 13; the items they change are marked (revised).

**Where the plan was silent, wrong or unimplementable (each decided here):**
1. **Three files the owned list did not name.** The mandated tests (`NoScoutingOrderOnGroupContainingPlayerCommand`: "the executor issues no call and still moves it") cannot run against a runtime file, so G5a's split holds: the decisions are `Planner/ReconExecutor.cs` (pure, over an `IReconHost`), `ReconExecutorRuntime.cs` implements the cavalry-order and live-chain half over vanilla. The move half was private to `CampaignMoverRuntime`; rather than copy it, `Planner/MoveIssue.cs` (pure: restricted target, transport modes, queued, refused, recorded, behind `IMoveHost`) and `Planner/VanillaMoveHost.cs` (runtime base class: own group by id, `IsTerrainPositionRestricted`, the package lock, `MoveUnitTo`, the AI transport default) are shared, and `CampaignMover`/`CampaignMoverRuntime` were refactored onto them with no behaviour change (the G5a harness passes unmoved; its source pins read `VanillaMoveHost.cs` for the lines that moved).
2. **One tracker, two budgets.** "MoveTracker with two budgets": `MoveBudget { Planner, Recon }`, `MaxReconMovesPerDay 3` counted apart from `MaxMovesPerDay 6` (both default-argument additions, so every G5a caller is unchanged; `Clear` resets both). One tracker means `CollectGaveUp` already reports recon give-ups to G3/G4 (`ReviewPlan` is untouched) and `Records` carries recon records to G6's sidecar. Two consequences, accepted: the mover's `JudgeLastReview` judges every in-transit record, so `[CampaignMover] confirmed/failed/gaveUp` count recon moves too (the executor only issues); and an in-transit recon formation is withheld from vanilla's defence for that one review by `CampaignWithholdView.InTransit`, as any planner move is. (revised, F1) `CollectGaveUp` and `Records` list planner-budget entries only (`MoveTracker.Entry.Budget`): G4 matches a give-up by target point (`ForceAllocator.GaveUpAt`) and a recon recall point can be the capital, a CapitalGuard's post, so a recon give-up must not turn that guard into a Hold; G3's set is keyed on objective ids, which a recon record (-1) never matches. A scout's give-up is kept by the executor's own `ShouldIssue` (the post stays shut for `GaveUpExpiryDays`) and recon records are not persisted (a load restarts the scouts).
3. **No ledger accessor.** The plan has G5d add "the one internal accessor"; `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReviewExecute` passes `Recon[alliance]` to `ReconExecutorRuntime.Execute`, so the ledger stays private and `ReconLedgerHasNoReaderOutsideTheReview` needs no edit (no `Patches/` file names it). The #15 stand-down is inside `FormationDirectiveRuntime.ShouldAllowAreaMovement` as the plan prefers, through the pure `FormationDirectiveLedger.AllowsAreaMovement(assignment, reconExecutorActive)` (the old three conditions moved there unchanged, plus `Screen` while the executor is active; `Screen` is produced only by a recon mission, G1b item 15), so the ledger does not join `PrepareDay`.
4. **The executor is not gated on `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance`** (revised, F2). With the flag off the Plan job's `SyncRecon` calls `ReconMissionLedger.ReleaseAll` (reason `recon-off`): every mission ends, a Recalled one included (`Sync` alone clears only active missions and left a Recalled one to be moved to its recall point while #15 and the old probe resumed on the same formation); the pass then takes the Scouting order off their screens (120) and moves nothing, and a recalled formation is vanilla's again where it stands, so a rollback leaves no group scouting for good. What stands down (`ReconExecutorActive()` = both planner flags + the recon flag): the old probe's `OperationalProbeRuntime.Run` (early return, transitional, deleted with the file in G6) and #15's return-to-area moves of a `Screen` formation.
5. **Placement in `ReviewExecute`.** The call follows the final `PublishExecutorContext`, not the mover directly: order is unchanged (mover, then G5d; G5c's runtime, when it merges, sits between), and the textual hunk stays clear of the line G5c inserts after the mover. `CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs` and `Plugin.cs` (the flag's description: the executor has shipped, #15 and the old probe stand down, the flags it needs) are the two shared files touched, on lines G5c does not edit.
6. **Per mission (what `ReconExecutor.Review` does).** Live chain re-read through the host (`WlStrategicOrderBridge.ChainClassOf` from a context captured once per pass), then: `ReconOrders.MayAct` (no `PlayerCommand`, no `CicSide`) and not `OrderOnly` (the map-group player is addressed by neither a move nor a dispatch; G4 never picks him) or the mission is skipped and counted `skippedPlayer`. Cavalry: one `ChangeCampaignOrders` call per formation, the strongest code any of its missions asks for (121 beats 120 beats no call, so a screen re-targeted in place keeps Scouting beside its own cleared mission and one re-tasked to a picket loses it), `-1` for `CarryingGroup`. The host skips the call when `cavalryordersordered` already equals the mode: vanilla's early return tests `cavalryorders` only (90738), so with order delays an order still in the queue would be queued again every review; sending each review is otherwise right because a group in readiness step 1 or less has both reset (113960). The queued edge, handled by `ReconOrders.CallFor(mode, cavalryorders, cavalryordersordered)` (pure, tested): ordered equals the wanted mode -> not sent (applied or already queued); else applied equals the wanted mode while a different one is queued (a 121 waiting when 120 is wanted, or the mirror) -> not sent and not counted, because vanilla cannot replace a queued order: `ChangeCampaignOrders` returns at 90738 on `cavalryorders == num`, before the queue path, and the queue path itself (`AddToOrderQueue`, 124929-124933) replaces only an entry of the SAME order type for the same unit, which 120 and 121 are not, so a wanted mode never overwrites a queued one of the other mode (the queue is applied at 125409-125412). (revised, N1: the first wording said no call removes an entry; `AddToOrderQueue` does replace a same-type one, which does not apply here.) The case self-heals: the queued order applies (`SetCavalryOrders` sets both fields, 122074-122082), the next review's `CallFor` answers send, and the wanted mode is ordered; the host logs `[ReconMission] ... note=cavalry-order-queued wanted=<m> ordered=<m>` once per formation (bounded by the formation count, reset with the campaign). Moves: Assigned, Moving and Recalled missions go to `HoldX/HoldZ` (the ledger rewrites it to the recall point for a recalled one), a recalled formation first; Holding is not moved, a formation whose last waypoint is within `CampaignMover.ArrivalRadius` (10) of the post is left to walk (this also absorbs a recall point that drifts a few units a day, which `MoveTracker.SameTarget` would take for a new target), not Movable or package-locked counts `skippedBusy`, the rest is `MoveIssue` on the recon budget with objective -1. Fog-honest: nothing here reads an enemy destination; contact was the ledger's.
7. **Telemetry.** `[ReconMission]` per mission whose (state, reason) changed (the plan's row; `Assigned` is logged too, so a mission that never starts moving is visible; the memory is pruned of missions the ledger dropped and resets with the campaign) and one counts row per review that did anything: `[ReconMission] alliance=<a> issued=<n> retried=<n> failed=<n> queued=<n> ordered=<n> skippedPlayer=<n> skippedBusy=<n>`. Scope `campaign.planner.recon-execute` (2.0). The event was registered at G0.
8. **Tests changed outside the new file.** `ReconFlagIsBoundAndRead` counts four reads of the recon flag in `CampaignPlannerRuntime` (the new `ReconExecutorActive`); `MoverRuntimeStatementsPinned` and `MoverHostReadsVanillasPublicFacts` read `VanillaMoveHost.cs` as well and name `Alliance` for the renamed field.
9. **One owner per formation per review (verify F1).** A formation G4 gives a role the mover sends (`CampaignMover.MovesRole`: CapitalGuard, MainEffort, Recover, DepotBuilder, Support) belongs to the mover for that review whatever state its mission is in: `ReconMissionLedger.Sync` ends the mission with reason `reassigned` (a Recalled mission included, which `ReconPlanner.IsRecalling` bars from recon picks only), so the executor makes no recon move for it; a screen that had been given 121 still gets its 120. Belt and braces: `ReconExecutor.MoveToPost` also skips a formation the tracker holds in transit (counted `skippedBusy`). (revised, r2 R1: the first wording said the mover's `JudgeLastReview` has settled earlier records by then, so in transit means sent today; that is false for a `Queued` record, which `Judge` leaves Queued. In transit now means: sent today, or a move waiting behind a vanilla order that is still running; item 13 settles the rest.) A second `MoveUnitTo` (refused by vanilla while a `DefensiveMovingOrder` runs, 9933-9945, 14519-14523) would overwrite the mover's record with a recon miss. Pinned through the real `Allocate`, `Sync`, mover `Review` and recon `Review` (the verifier's probe 2: Recalled probe corps given MainEffort; and the low-supply recall that falls below the Recover line); recon give-ups never feed G3/G4 (item 2, revised).
10. **A stand-down the executors owe survives the days they miss (verify F3).** `ReconMission` gains `StandDownDue` (set when the executor ordered Scouting for the active mission, `NoteScouting`; cleared when the 120 is delivered or found already set, `NoteStoodDown`) and `ClearedDay` (the first review that saw the mission Cleared). `OnReview` keeps a Cleared mission with `StandDownDue` instead of dropping it (the executors do not run on a W&L-pause or stale-plan day, so it used to be dropped before any pass saw it and the screen kept order 1, which also delays it as a battle reinforcement, 67742-67744, until a readiness reset, 113960), skips it when re-deriving states, and drops it `MaxMissionDays` after `ClearedDay`; a re-tasked screen's new active mission takes the debt over (`NoteScouting` clears it on the unit's other missions). A 120 that vanilla queues behind another order (`CavalrySend.QueuedOther`) is not delivered and is tried again. Both fields are persisted with the mission: **G6's `ReconMissionDto` carries `StandDownDue` and `ClearedDay`** (`Restore` round-trips the struct, `StandDownDebtRoundTripsThroughRestore`).
11. **Known limit (verify N2).** A screen that was given 121 while its chain was `None` and later became `CarryingGroup` (or any non-`None` class) can never be sent 120: `CavalryOrderFor` answers -1 for a non-`None` chain because the order at level -1 reaches the group and every descendant three levels down (90758, 92572-92620), the player's units included. The executor lets the debt go (`NoteStoodDown`, also when the formation no longer exists) and the group keeps Scouting until a readiness reset (113960, step 1 or less zeroes `cavalryorders` and `cavalryordersordered`).
12. **Mover-path pins (verify N4).** `MoverRefusalListsNothingAndKeepsTheObjective`: a vanilla-refused move is not listed in `unitsinoffensiveoperations` and its record carries the objective (both survived G5a's plants, before and after the refactor). The stale `docs/patch-catalog.md` row stays a G7 item (N3).
13. **A queued record settles by one rule (verify r2 R1, R2, N3).** `Judge` leaves a `Queued` record Queued by design (one order is served at a time, 9966-10052) and nothing else settled it, so a queued move was never re-sent once the order ahead of it had ended, and a formation whose role or mission changed stayed "in transit" (withheld from vanilla's offence and defence by `CampaignWithholdView`) for good. `MoveTracker.SettleQueued(instanceId, budget, wanted, orderRunning)` is the rule, for both budgets: a Queued record settles (is removed) when the order ahead has ended or when its owner no longer wants the move; wanted with the order running leaves it. `SettleExpiredQueued` bounds it: a queued record untouched for `QueuedExpiryDays` (14) review days is dropped (the owner re-records a wanted queued move every review, so only unwanted or unreachable records age out; an issued move is never dropped, the judgement owns it). Call sites: the mover's `JudgeLastReview` (its planner records, wanted = `CampaignMover.MovesRole` of the allocation's role; recon records only for the ended order, since only the recon pass knows the missions) and `ReconExecutor.SettleQueuedMoves` before any move (recon records, wanted = the formation has an active or Recalled mission), so the mission-over case releases the record even while vanilla's order is still running and the recon pass needs no mover pass to settle an ended order; the live mission then re-sends through `MoveIssue`. A queued RETRY that is still wanted settles to Failed with its retry count kept (verify r3 N1: removing it made the re-issue a fresh `Issued` and bought one extra attempt per retry that queued; the verifier's R3-G sequence, `QueuedRetryKeepsItsCountWhenItSettles`), while a first attempt that never failed and any unwanted record are removed. The one mutation of that branch that survives, resetting the kept count to 0, is equivalent: the only reader of the count is the give-up test `Retries < 1`, and the re-issue from Failed adds one. `Judge` and G5a's `QueuedMoveIsNotAFailure` are unchanged. **N3:** the host now tells the campaign `BattleUnits` not being found (`CavalrySend.NoBattleUnits`, transient: the stand-down debt is kept) from the group being gone (`NoUnit`: the debt is let go); both are pinned in the pure contract (`GoneUnitReleasesTheDebtButMissingBattleUnitsKeepsIt`, which also kills the plant that dropped the `NoUnit` release).

**Census.** `ReconOrders.CavalryOrderFor`, `MayAct`: `ReconExecutor`; `ReconOrders.ModeOf`: `ReconExecutorRuntime`'s host; `ReconMissionLedger.Missions`, `ReconMission.*` (`MissionId`, `Kind`, `HoldX/HoldZ`, `State`, `Reason`, `UnitKey`, `InstanceId`, `StandDownDue`, `ClearedDay`): `ReconExecutor`, `ReconMissionLedger` (G6 sidecar: `StandDownDue`, `ClearedDay`); `ReconMissionLedger.ReleaseAll`: `CampaignPlannerRuntime.SyncRecon`; `NoteScouting`/`NoteStoodDown`: `ReconExecutor`; `CampaignMover.MovesRole`: `ReconMissionLedger.Sync`; `MoveBudget`, `MaxReconMovesPerDay`: `ReconExecutor`, `MoveIssue`; `FormationDirectiveLedger.AllowsAreaMovement`: `FormationDirectiveRuntime`; `CampaignPlannerRuntime.ReconExecutorActive`: `FormationDirectiveRuntime`, `OperationalProbeRuntime`. G6 deletes `OperationalProbeRuntime` (its early return goes with it) and `ArmyAreaRuntime`'s caller of `ShouldAllowAreaMovement` (the `Screen` branch of `AllowsAreaMovement` goes with #15). G7 doc item: the `docs/patch-catalog.md` row for `ReconStep`/`ReconMissionLedger` still says "No formation is moved by this step; #15's area moves wait for G5d's stand-down" and lists no executor; add `ReconExecutor`/`ReconExecutorRuntime` (and `MoveIssue`/`VanillaMoveHost` beside the mover) there.

**G5d smoke items (with G7's smoke):** (a) `[ReconMission] alliance=<a> unit=<name> kind=<k> state=Assigned reason=<r>` for each new mission, then `state=Moving` and `state=Holding` rows in the following reviews, `state=Recalled`/`Cleared` with their reasons (`contact`, `low-supply`, `probe-withdraw`, `rejoined`, `expired`, `retreat`, `unassigned`, `missing`, `re-tasked`, `main-moved`); (b) the counts row has `issued + retried + failed + queued` at most 3 in one review and `ordered` > 0 only when a cavalry screen is on its mission; no `[ReconMission]` row for a unit of the W&L player's own command; (c) a cavalry-majority screen shows the "Scouting" status on the map and loses it when recalled or cleared (watch one with the unit panel); a group carrying the player's brigade never shows it, though it moves; (d) no `[OperationalProbe]` write rows and no `[ArmyArea] ... action=skip-return-area reason=formation-directive` oscillation for the same unit day after day while the executor is active (the skip row itself is expected once per unit and day); (e) screens halt at night like any group and arrive within a few days at 20x: a mission that stays `Assigned` for more than two reviews with `issued=0` is a finding (read `failed`, `retried`, `skippedBusy`); (f) rollback: `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance = false` ends every mission at the next review (`[ReconMission] ... state=Cleared reason=recon-off`, Recalled ones too, with no move), takes Scouting off the screens within one review and the old probe resumes; `Enable Campaign Planner Executors = false` stops every move and order, #15 and the old probe resume (a Scouting order already given stays until the flag is switched back or readiness resets it).

## G6 — Removals, migrations, sidecar v3, coordinator rewiring (integrator, sequential)

**Sidecar v3 (`PersistenceDto.cs`, `SidecarDtoCodec.cs`):**
- `SidecarDto.CurrentVersion = 3`; readers accept 1..3. History comment: `3 = campaign planner: plan state, beliefs, task ledgers; CIC plans dropped`.
- `FactionDto` loses `cic`, `probeState`, `probeHysteresis`, `directorMemory.recentOperationReplanDaySerials`; keeps `currentEra`, `directorMemory` (pace/risk/streak), `fiscalMemory`, `frontChangedDay`, `capitalStreakDay`, `modWrittenObjective` (now written by G5a's objective executor); gains `planner` (`PlannerStateDto`: `unreliableDays` (G4's telemetry run: `ForceAllocator.PlayerNonCompliance`, restored with its last review day), `currentMainSector`, `currentMainObjective`, `challengerObjective`, `challengerDays`, `decisionSerial`, `campaignSeed`, `belowOddsDays`, `capitalThreatLatched` — the last two added at G3 verify r1), `beliefs` (`List<EnemyBeliefDto>`: unitKey, unitTyp, x, z, strength, guns, lastSeenDay, confidence, radius, source, traits, lastEmbarkedDay), `appointments` (`List<AppointmentDto>`: commanderId, protectedUntilDay), `depotTasks` (`List<DepotTaskDto>`: x, z, sectorId, builderUnitKey, state, startedDay), `moves` (`List<MoveDto>`: unitKey, tx, tz, issuedDay, retries, objectiveId (G5a: G3 excludes a formation from the mass of the objective it gave up on; -1 for a move toward no objective)), `reconMissions` (`List<ReconMissionDto>`: missionId, kind, unitKey, holdX, holdZ, sectorId, state, issuedDay, lastContactDay). **No DTO stores a Unity instance id** (they change on every load): every unit reference is the `unitKey` (`name:commander`, the convention of `OperationalProbeStateDto.UnitKey` and `FormationDirectiveRuntime.UnitKey`). Rebinding on the first review after a load: `CampaignSnapshot.IndexOfKey(unitKey)` (own formations; for beliefs, the next detection under that key), falling back to the unit name alone when the commander part no longer matches; an unresolved own-unit record (move, mission, depot task, garrison post) is dropped, and a belief keeps decaying under its key until it is seen again or expires.
- `SidecarDto` loses `minorOfficerProfiles` (dead state).
- Chapters decisions (2026-10-15): `PlannerStateDto` also carries `tempoReleasedDecision` (G5a's chapter tempo gate: the decision serial whose main-effort launch has been released, so a reload neither rolls again nor holds that decision's Support moves into enemy-held ground) and `lastFullReviewDay` (G5a's tempo clock: the day serial of the previous executors-allowed Full review; absent in v1/v2 → −1, one review's worth); `currentEra` is the chapter-driven era once `fix/chapters` is merged (decision 7), unchanged in form. Test `V3RoundTripsTempoClock` (both fields round-trip; a v2 file restores `lastFullReviewDay = −1`).
- **Migration from v1/v2:** Newtonsoft ignores the removed members, so a v1/v2 file decodes with no planner state: plans dropped, beliefs empty (rebuilt on the next review), hysteresis fresh, `campaignSeed` computed at the first review after the load (P5). `PlanFromDto`/`MatchesCatalog` are deleted with the catalog; `InferModWritten` keeps working off `modWrittenObjective` and the live `followedcampaignobjective`. Load logs `[Coordinator] sidecar v<n> migrated to v3: plans dropped=<n>` once.
- G5c (verify F2): `PlannerStateDto` also carries `depotTasks` (`DepotTaskLedger.Tasks`: site x/z, sector, `builderUnitKey`, `state`, `startedDay`, `endedDay`, `wishX`/`wishZ`; the instance id is session-local and is not stored), restored through `DepotTaskLedger.Restore`; test `V3RoundTripsDepotTasks` (a restored task rebinds its builder by unit key at the next review, and an abandoned site still cools down).
- Stage E adds need/policy fields to `FactionDto` in parallel; they are additive and version-agnostic — keep them when rebasing.
- Tests (`SidecarMigrationTests` in `CampaignLifecycleFixTests.cs` or a new `PlannerSidecarTests.cs`): `V2SidecarWithActivePlanLoadsWithoutPlan`, `V1SidecarLoads`, `V3RoundTripsPlannerState`, `V3RoundTripsBeliefsAndTasks`, `V3RoundTripsReconMissions`, `V3ReloadReboundsBeliefsByStableKey` (a v3 file restored under new instance ids: beliefs match the next detections by `unitKey`, no duplicate, `StrengthWithin` unchanged), `V3ReloadKeepsReconMissionFormation` (a mission's formation is found again by `IndexOfKey` after the reload; smoke 1.9), `V3DropsUnresolvedOwnUnitRecords`, `NoDtoCarriesInstanceId` (source pin over `PersistenceDto.cs`), `FutureVersionRejected`, `MinorOfficerProfilesNoLongerWritten`.

## G7 — Integration, read census, docs, smoke (integrator, last)

**Owned files:** `tests/WhiskeyRealism.Tests/PlannerReadCensusTests.cs` (new), `docs/*`, `AGENTS.md` (locked choices #3-#6 rewritten per the spec; architecture paragraph; campaign write owners), `MEMORY.md`, `docs/patch-catalog.md` (ordinals for new patches in merge order; removed rows for #1, #15, #38; rewritten rows #2, #3, #4, #6, #27; runtime rows for `CampaignPlannerRuntime`/`CampaignMoverRuntime`/`DepotSteeringRuntime`/`CommanderAppointmentRuntime`; `WlStrategicOrderBridge` row as F leaves it), `docs/handoff.md`, `docs/telemetry.md` (new events), `docs/historical-operation-doctrine.md` (prior semantics), `docs/strategic-recon-commitment.md` (beliefs shipped), `docs/wl-dispatch-objective-bridge.md` + `docs/wl-player-order-doctrine.md` (planner invariants: map-group player = order-only, below-map-level parent movable, CIC stand-down), `docs/bug-fixes/README.md` (BUG-WL-013, BUG-ECO-004/005 status), `tools/smoke-situational.txt`, the test csproj (final reconciliation).

- [ ] Wiring checks: every review runs snapshot → sectors → detect → beliefs → profiles → plan (with logistics and depot tasks) → recon tasks → allocation → recon mission sync → publish, then appointments → executors (G5b, G5a, G5c, G5d) in that order (`CampaignPlannerRuntime` source pin test `PipelineOrderPinned` in `CampaignExecutorTests`); every planner patch reads only `CampaignPlanStore`/`CampaignWithholdView`.
- [ ] **Read census (no written-never-read members; `orphan-sweep` counts a write as a reference, so it cannot prove this).** For every G0 contract member and every `[Campaign Planner]` flag, the census below names the slice and method that **reads** it. G7 proves each row with a read-only grep **scoped to the reader file(s) the row names** (generic names such as `X`, `Z`, `Id`, `Index`, `Name`, `Available` or `Strength` would otherwise hit unrelated types): `rg -n "\.<Member>\b" <reader file>` (e.g. `rg -n "\.Supply01\b" src/WhiskeyRealism/Strategic/Planner/TheaterPlanner.cs`) must show at least one hit in that file that is not an assignment (`\.<Member>\s*=[^=]` excluded), pasted per row into the report; a member with no such hit is deleted before the commit, never kept for a later stage. A test file `PlannerReadCensusTests.cs` (G7) pins the list: it parses `CampaignPlannerContracts.cs`/`CampaignSnapshot.cs` for public members and fails when a member is missing from the census table it carries, so a member added later without a reader row turns the harness red.

| Contract member | Read by (slice: method) |
|---|---|
| `PlayerCommandClass` values | G0 `PlayerCommandClassifier.PlannerStandsDown`/`ExecutorsPaused`; G4 `ForceAllocator.Allocate` (`CommanderInChief` → empty); telemetry `player=` (G0, G4) |
| `FormationFlags.InBattle`, `OnRetreat`, `VanillaDepotBuilder`, `FortBuilder`, `SeaInvasion`, `RaidForce`, `InTransfer`, `Embarked` | G4 busy rule (`ForceAllocator.Allocate`); G1b `ReconPlanner` busy set; `InBattle`, `OnRetreat` also G1b `ReconMissionLedger.OnReview` |
| `FormationFlags.InOffensiveOps`, `InDefensiveOps` | `InOffensiveOps`: G5a Recover relief (removal from vanilla's offensive list); `InDefensiveOps`: G3/G4 `Busy` mask (G5a); both: G1b `ReconPlanner` busy set |
| `FormationFlags.CapitalDefense` | G5b `CapitalFloorPolicy.PickPledge` (already pledged); G1b `ReconPlanner` busy set |
| `FormationFlags.FightingForce` | G4 CapitalGuard and DepotBuilder picks; G1b `ReconPlanner.PickFormation`; G8 `GarrisonPlanner.PickFormation` |
| `FormationFlags.HasPath`, `MoveQueued` | G5a `MoveTracker.ShouldIssue` (no new order over a running or queued one); G1b `ReconMissionLedger.OnReview` (Moving) |
| `FormationRecord.InstanceId`, `UnitKey` | every executor (live lookup); `UnitKey`: G1b `FormationDirectiveLedger` recon check, G6 sidecar rebinding (`IndexOfKey`) |
| `FormationRecord.UnitTyp`, `X`, `Z`, `Strength` | G3 `TheaterPlanner.Plan`, G4 `ForceAllocator.Allocate`, G2 `CommanderAppointmentPlanner.TryPlan` target commands (`UnitTyp`, `Strength` order), G1b, G5b, G8 |
| `FormationRecord.WaypointX/Z` | G5a `MoveTracker.Judge` |
| `FormationRecord.Morale` | G3 Recover posture; G5b `CapitalFloorPolicy.VanillaNeed` (str × morale) |
| `FormationRecord.Supply01` | G3 Recover posture; G1b `ReconMissionLedger.OnReview` |
| `FormationRecord.ReadinessStep` | G1b `ReconPlanner.PickFormation` (movement minima) |
| `FormationRecord.CommandRange` | G3/G0 `LogisticsGate.Evaluate` callers (reach per formation) |
| `FormationRecord.BugleRange` | G1 contact detection; G1b `ReconMissionLedger.OnReview` (contact, arrival), `ReconPlanner` probe contact; G5c `DepotTaskLedger.ShouldWish`; G8 existing strength |
| `FormationRecord.CommanderId` | G3 sector profile lookup (`CommanderProfiles.ForSector`); G2 `CommanderAppointmentPlanner.TryPlan` (sitting commander) |
| `FormationRecord.HasTheater`, `TheaterX/Z` | G5a theater-box writer (write only when it differs) |

### G7 smoke checklist (in order)

`LOG` = `<GTCW>/BepInEx/LogOutput.log`; `SESSION` = newest `<GTCW>/BepInEx/WhiskeyRealism/tuning-logs/<session>/` (campaign rows in `SESSION/campaign*.jsonl`).

0. **Precondition.** Deployed DLL hash = `dist/WhiskeyRealism.dll`; cfg `[Campaign Planner]` flags all `true`.
1. **Session A — normal campaign (player Union, CSA AI), 20x, 3 in-game months, one battle.**
   1. `python3 -I tools/smoke-check.py "$LOG"` exit 0; `[first-fire]` for `campaign.patch.importance-values`, `campaign.patch.planner-availability.offensive`, `campaign.patch.planner-availability.defensive`, `campaign.patch.defensive-ops`, `campaign.patch.transfer-of-units.prefix`, `campaign.patch.commander-replacement`, `campaign.patch.fort-governor.prefix`, and `campaign.patch.railroad-steering`.
   2. `[once:campaign-planner:first-review:1] [CampaignPlanner] first review alliance=1 formations=<n> areas=<n> objectives=<n> supply=<n> rails=<n> commanders=<n> player=NotCareer` (no line for alliance 0 outside `ai_vs_ai`).
   3. `[EnemyBeliefs] alliance=1 mode=FogGrid seen=<n> remembered=<n> expired=<n> contact=<n>` with `seen` below the number of Union campaign groups early in the war, `remembered > 0` later.
   4. `[CampaignPlan] alliance=1 main=<theater>/<objectiveId> …` on the first review, then only on change; no flapping (≤ 1 main-effort change per 3 days unless `switch=capital-threat|accomplished|battle`).
   5. `[Allocation] alliance=1 …` with `main > 0` and `capital > 0`; `[CampaignMover] alliance=1 issued=<n> confirmed=<n> …` with `confirmed/issued ≥ 0.5` over the session and `gaveUp` small.
   6. `[Appointment]` rows at most one per day, and the G2 guard's `[once:replace:blocked]` line (a real job-24 block, G2 smoke item 3); `[DepotTask]` reaching `Built` at least once in 3 months (or `Abandoned` with a reason); `[RailroadSteer]` rows.
   7. Performance (`SESSION/performance*.jsonl`, run with `Logging Profile = FullTuning`: under the default `TacticalTuning` the planner scopes are not timed): every window's `maxMs` per alliance at 20x, including the first review after a load, ≤ 2 for `campaign.planner.snapshot`, ≤ 4 for `campaign.planner.review` (P2: over budget is a defect, there is no deferral), ≤ 1 for `campaign.planner.detect` and `campaign.planner.recon`, and `slowCount` 0 for each; `campaign.daily-review` and `campaign.patch.monthly-tick-hook` `maxMs` not worse than the pre-G0 baseline by more than 3 ms. Means are reported, not gated.
   8. **Absent:** `campaign-planner:snapshot-degraded`, `campaign-planner:*-failed`, any Harmony exception, `[Coordinator] sidecar load rejected`.
   9. Save, reload: `[Coordinator] sidecar loaded`, the next `[CampaignPlan]` keeps the same `main=` (hysteresis state persisted), `[Recon]` keeps its missions on the same formations (rebound by `unitKey`), and `[EnemyBeliefs] remembered=` does not jump on the first review after the load (no double counting).
   10. Recon: `[Recon] alliance=1 screens=<n ≥ 1> pickets=<n> advanceGuards=<n> probes=<n> recalled=<n> unwatchedApproaches=<n> oldestBeliefDays=<n>`; `[ReconMission] alliance=1 unit=<name> kind=Screen state=Moving` then `state=Holding`; a cavalry screen shows "Scouting" in its campaign status (vanilla text, 119923).
   11. Recon effect: across the session `[EnemyBeliefs]` `remembered` stays bounded and `oldestBeliefDays` in `[Recon]` does not grow without bound (screens refresh beliefs); compare against a run with `Enable Campaign Reconnaissance = false`, where `oldestBeliefDays` climbs to the 30-day expiry.
   12. Recall: a screen that meets an enemy logs `[ReconMission] … state=Recalled reason=contact` and moves back, and one whose raw supply drops below 0.5 logs `reason=supply`; no screen sits inside an enemy army's bugle range for more than one review; the main-effort sector never loses its last cavalry screen (`[Allocation]` + `[Recon]`).
   13. Hold: a Hold sector next to a main-effort source keeps its hold need (vanilla's offensive does not gather its withheld Hold formations; `[Allocation] hold=<n>` is stable across the offensive).
   14. Supply: `[Allocation] … recover=<n>` stays small on a normal day (raw metric; a value near the whole army means the halved metric leaked in); a Recover formation leaves `unitsinoffensiveoperations` and walks toward supply (`[CampaignMover]`), and is not picked by vanilla's next offensive.
   15. Reinforcement routing: `[ReplacementFlow] alliance=1 kind=recruit to=<formation>` naming a formation listed short in `[Allocation]`, and `kind=transfer` rows; `[first-fire] campaign.patch.recruit-routing` once vanilla recruits.
   16. Chapter tempo (decision 5): every new main effort into enemy territory logs `[CampaignTempo] alliance=1 main=<id> decision=<n> chapter=<c> gap=<d> p=<tval> … released=false` on its first review (unless it releases at once) and `released=true` before its first `[CampaignMover]` MainEffort move into enemy territory. Read the decision from the rows, not from an expected wait: `gap=1` on every roll (never the days since the previous release, also on the first review after a save/reload or a W&L pause), `p=` is the formula's value for that chapter (CSA chapter 1 outside winter ≈ 0.010 per day plus the idle term: with a fresh mass about three months on average, sooner when the mass has stood idle), and `released=true` follows a roll ≤ `p` or `forced=true`. No MainEffort move into enemy territory precedes the release, no Support move and no W&L player's Support order into enemy-held ground precedes it either, and no member of a deferred mass and no AI Support formation of a deferred decision moves on an offensive (vanilla's offensive cannot pick them; the map-group player's group may still go on vanilla's own roll); a vanilla defensive operation may still move it (the member then shows as busy in the next `[Allocation]`, out of `mass=`). Support moves and the player's Support order within own or neutral ground, other moves in own territory and CapitalGuard/Recover moves are never held.
2. **Session B — W&L brigade career.** `[CampaignPlanner] … player=BelowMapLevel`; `[EnemyBeliefs] alliance=<player alliance> mode=OwnSideWithinFog`; the player's parent corps moves under the planner and a dispatch panel names the superior (F); the player's brigade never moves by itself; `[once:campaign-planner:wl-transfer-fix-first]`. With the player commandless (hierarchy −1): the pause line `[Coordinator] W&L player has no command (hierarchy -1): planner executors and order-issuing ledgers paused for every alliance, as vanilla's job machine` once, no `[CampaignMover]`, `[ReconMission]`, `[Appointment]`, `[DepotTask]`, `[RailroadSteer]` or `[CampaignPlan]` rows for **either** alliance until a new command, while `[EnemyBeliefs]` rows continue (bookkeeping). If the player's carrying cavalry group is used as a screen: it moves, F's dispatch panel appears, it shows no "Scouting" status and the player's own brigade keeps `cavalryorders` 0 (no Whiskey cavalry order reaches the player's units).
3. **Session C — W&L corps career.** `player=MapGroup`; the corps receives orders (dispatch panel), is never moved by Whiskey, and is never the only `capital` holder; after 5 ignored days its role is covered (`[Allocation]` change). A cavalry-corps player is never the only screen of an approach and gets no scouting order from Whiskey.
   In-battle credit (the in-battle holder rule has only a source pin, because `DefenseIntentRuntime` is a runtime adapter outside the test project): while the corps is in a battle (`inbattle`) near an own capital or asset, the next `[DefenseIntent]` row still counts it at strength x morale toward the threat or asset nearest its last waypoint (an AI group in battle is skipped, the map-group player is not), and nothing pledges, packages or moves it.
4. **Session D — W&L CIC career.** No `[CampaignPlanner]`/`[CampaignPlan]` for the player's alliance; the enemy side's rows continue, and the coordinator logs no W&L pause (a CIC has hierarchy 18, not −1).
5. **Session E — Grand Campaign 001/A from its start (1 Mar 1861), player Union, CSA AI, 20x, through the war start and 2 months of war (decisions 5, 6, 8).**
   1. Pre-war (chapter 0, before the "CIVIL WAR!" newspaper): `[once:campaign-planner:pre-war-idle]`; `[CampaignPlan]`/`[Allocation]` rows may appear (bookkeeping), and `[Appointment]` rows may too (appointments are not pre-war gated), but no `[CampaignMover]`, `[DepotTask]`, `[RailroadSteer]`, `[ReconMission]` or `[CampaignTempo] … released=true` row; no `[CampaignPace] … pace=TooQuiet` row; no vanilla "no movement pre-war" rolling text caused by a Whiskey move.
   2. `[CampaignPlanner] first review alliance=1 … objectives=<n> mapped=<m ≥ 1>` (G3c).
   3. After the war starts (chapter 1): executor rows begin; a `[CampaignPlan] alliance=1 main=<theater>/<id>` with id in {4, 15, 16} on mapped ground; `[CampaignTempo]` for it as in Session A item 16 (CSA chapter 1, faction 0.5); never `main=` on a ground-less id (0, 1, 5-13, 18-28).
   4. A Union AI run (player CSA): `main=` in {2, 14, 17} before policy 170 and in {3, 14, 17} after it (policy 170 moves the capital: 2 off, 3 on, 178927-178934; in sub-scenario E neither 14 nor 17); after about 7 Jul 1861 with objective 6 open the Union's first offensive logs `forced=true` (14303-14306).
   5. Era (decision 7, once `fix/chapters` is merged): `[Heartbeat] … era=Amateur1861` through 1861 as before. A late-war save (1864, chapter 2) shows `era=` never `TotalWar1864` before the "Lincoln Re-Elected!" newspaper (chapter 3), and the planner's `[CampaignPace]` LateWarPressure only from chapter 3; checked on that save, not in this run.
6. **Rollback spot check.** `Enable Campaign Planner Executors = false`: plan/allocation rows continue, no `[CampaignMover]`, `[Appointment]`, `[DepotTask]`, `[RailroadSteer]`, `[CapitalFloor]`, `[ReconMission]` rows. `Enable Campaign Planner = false`: no planner rows at all; vanilla `PickCampaignObjective` picks again.

## G8 — Garrison manning beyond the capital (after G7)

Why: vanilla defends only the capital. `AssignUnitToDefendCapital` (11668-11869) is the only proactive defense; `CheckOccupationOfLostCities` (job 29, 17392-17485) is reactive — it sends the closest available unit to retake a lost own-state town that no enemy currently holds, if zone supply allows. No `AICampaign` code creates or mans garrisons (no `isgarrison: true` / `garrisonreference =` write in 8775-17933): garrisons exist only from the scenario (81991-81993) and as the dummy a finished fort spawns (`CreateGarrisonDummy` 82081-82093, `basicgarrisonsize[level]`; 96676-96686); `SeparateUnits` keeps every garrison out of `ownunits` (10510-10513), and the capital need's garrison loop is dead (11742-11750). Key towns, rail hubs, ports, depots and forts away from the capital are therefore held only by whatever happens to stand there.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonPlanner.cs` (pure), `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonLedger.cs` (pure, persisted), `Strategic/Planner/GarrisonRuntime.cs` (runtime executor); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`FormationRole.Garrison = 8`, `GarrisonPost` struct), `Strategic/Planner/ForceAllocator.cs` (Garrison step), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignWithholdView.cs` (Garrison withheld from both pools; the allocator sets `Withheld` for the Garrison role), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignSnapshotRuntime.cs` (forts with their garrison dummy strength, own depots), `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerRuntime.cs`, `PersistenceDto.cs` / `SidecarDtoCodec.cs` (`garrisonPosts`, additive in v3), `Plugin.cs` (bind `Enable Garrison Manning`), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs` (`Garrison`, `GarrisonPost`); test `GarrisonPlannerTests.cs`.

**Vanilla to read:** 11668-11869 (capital need formula 11712-11741 reused for post threat), 17392-17485, 81931-81993, 82081-82093, 96676-96686 (`BattleUnits.fort`), 10458-10515, 14080-14158 (availability; the Postfix withholds Garrison), `IsWithinOperationsTheater` 17090-17110 (the post's theater box is set on arrival, as G5a's writer does), W&L 8271/8366/14381-14456.

**Interfaces — Produces:**
```csharp
public enum GarrisonPostKind { ObjectiveTown = 0, RailHub = 1, Port = 2, Depot = 3, Fort = 4 }
public struct GarrisonPost { public int PostId; public GarrisonPostKind Kind; public float X, Z; public float Need; public float ExistingStrength; public int AssignedInstanceId; /* in-session */ public string AssignedUnitKey; /* persisted */ public int QuietDays; }
public static class GarrisonPlanner {
  public const float ThreatShare = 0.5f;      // posted strength ≥ ThreatShare × post threat − existing garrison
  public const int MaxPostsPerAlliance = 6; public const int ReleaseQuietDays = 14;
  // Posts = own objective towns, rail hubs and ports (ObjectiveRole), own depots and own forts that lie in a Hold, Screen or Support sector
  // or within relocation range of a belief; the capital is excluded (the capital floor owns it). Threat = the 11712-11741 formula on beliefs.
  public static void PlanPosts(CampaignSnapshot s, SectorModel sectors, TheaterPlan plan, System.Collections.Generic.List<GarrisonPost> into);
  public static int PickFormation(CampaignSnapshot s, in GarrisonPost post, Allocation a); // smallest fighting formation that covers the need,
  // from Hold / EconomyOfForce / Reserve only; never MainEffort if that drops it below RequiredRatio, never CapitalGuard, Recon or Recover; -1 none
}
public sealed class GarrisonLedger { public void Sync(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<GarrisonPost> posts, Allocation a, int daySerial);
  public bool IsGarrison(int instanceId); public System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList<GarrisonPost> Posts { get; }
  public void Restore(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable<GarrisonPost> posts); }
```
Existing strength = the fort's garrison dummy (`groupstrengthdirect`) plus own formations within bugle range of the post. A post is released after `ReleaseQuietDays` with no belief within relocation range. The executor uses G5a's mover (hold point = the post, within bugle range) and sets the formation's theater box on arrival; vanilla's reactive recapture (job 29) is unchanged and complements it. W&L (F's chain classes): never a `PlayerCommand`; a `PlayerMapGroup` is order-only (`TryIssue`, intent `Redeploy`, type 5) and never the sole garrison of a post; a `CarryingGroup` may be posted (F's dispatch).

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first (`GarrisonPlannerTests`): `PostsOnlyOwnTownsHubsPortsDepotsForts`, `CapitalNeverAGarrisonPost`, `PostOnlyInThreatenedOrHoldSectors`, `NeedSubtractsFortGarrisonDummy`, `NeedFromBeliefsOnly`, `NeverFromMainEffortBelowRequiredRatio`, `NeverTakesCapitalGuardReconOrRecover`, `PrefersSmallestFormationCoveringNeed`, `PostsCappedPerAlliance`, `ReleasedAfterQuietDays`, `NeverPlayerCommandOrSoleMapGroupPlayer`, `GarrisonWithheldFromVanillaOperations`, `GarrisonPostsRoundTripInSidecar` (by `AssignedUnitKey`), `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`.
- [ ] Implement; scope `campaign.planner.garrison` (2.0). Telemetry `[Garrison] alliance=<a> posts=<n> manned=<n> released=<n> uncovered=<n>` on change; `[GarrisonPost] alliance=<a> post=<name> kind=<kind> unit=<name> state=<Moving|Holding|Released> need=<x>` per transition.
- [ ] Gates; docs (`patch-catalog` runtime row, `handoff`, spec component 9 as built); commit `feat(campaign-planner): garrison manning beyond the capital [G8]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness (red first); mutation plants: drop the capital exclusion → `CapitalNeverAGarrisonPost` red; omniscient threat → `NeedFromBeliefsOnly` red. In-game: a 3-month campaign session shows `[Garrison] … manned ≥ 1` in a threatened theater, a posted formation holding at a town or fort (`[GarrisonPost] … state=Holding`), and release after a quiet fortnight; vanilla's capital defense and recapture lines unchanged; no `[GarrisonPost]` naming the W&L player's command.

## G9 — Ordnance: re-arming priority and weapon purchasing (after G8; waits for `fix/arms-bugs`)

User direction 2026-10-15: "Fix all". Source: `research/armament.md` § B, C and recommendation 4; every anchor below re-read 2026-10-15 in the decompile (build 14703111).

**Vanilla, as read.**
- **Re-arming** (`CheckWeaponUpgrades` 16080-16153, from the alliance tick every `updaterhythmalliancedata` = 0.5 game-day, 81813, 81492): skips the player alliance outside `ai_vs_ai` (16082); copies `GameVars.alliance[a].unitlist` (16091; built in scene-hierarchy order, 81296-81306); on the new-campaign pass (`isinitialization`) drops garrisons and **shuffles** the list (16092-16131); drops `dlcw_isundercommander` units in a career (16137-16144); then `UpgradeWeaponForUnit` (16155-16193) per unit in list order: a basic unit takes `GetBestWeapon` (16224-16257), the highest-WSF model of its type with stock for the whole unit (strength + wounded + sick, or `statsguns`) that beats its current WSF, returning its old pieces to stock. So whoever comes first in the list takes the scarce new models. Nothing else in the call reads `unitlist` except a debug string (`unitlist.IndexOf`, 16181, 16187).
- **Purchasing** (`CheckPurchaseWeapons` 16259-16345, alliance tick 81757): skips the player alliance unless `automanageweapons` (in a W&L career: unless the player is CIC, 16263) and on a bad credit rating (`IsRatingOkForWeaponPurchases`); reads every enemy alliance's **true** `averageweaponstrengthguns/rifles` (16266-16282); per lane counts "equippable units" over models better than its own average (16287-16312) and orders when that count is below `aiminnumberofequippableunits` × artillery/other units (16325, 16335); the pick is **random** among researched-or-imported models with no open order that beat the own average (`list2`/`list`), or that beat the enemy average when behind (`list4`/`list3`) (16328, 16338); the amount is fixed (`usedstatsgunsdummy × aiweaponordersmultipleguns`, `unittypesize[0] × aiweaponordersmultiplerifles`). `WeaponList.PlaceWeaponOrder(alliance, weaponid, quantity)` (227136-227166) refuses a second open order or a missing capital, prices it with `GetProductionFigures` (64074-64142: domestic availability from stock, standardization, `productionmultiplier`, project 102; imports × blockade, 64120-64124; relations), takes the delivery time from `GetDeliveryTime(GameVars.playeralliance, …)` (63989-64001; F7 of the research: it reads the player alliance) and charges the full cost at once. Cost and delivery time never enter the choice.
- **Battle value.** Vanilla ranks by WSF (`GetWeaponStrengthFactor` 16214-16222: `range_yd × rpm^0.2 × firepower[0]/2 × accuracy³`), so rate of fire barely counts; in tactical battles reload time is `1 / rpm` (119633), so a repeater fires several times as often as a rifle-musket; autocalc ignores weapon rate of fire (21025-21047).
- **Researched but unproduced models (research F3): a vanilla bug.** Evidence: the player's order screen allows a model only when `AbleToOrder` (63938-63946) — `GetAvailability` (63962-63987) returns "Not produced" or tooltip 1831 for a researched model with `productionmultiplier <= 0` that is not imported; the AI's candidate test is researched-or-imported only (16296, 16313); 16289 holds a discarded `_ = j == 37 || j == 38;`, the remnant of an exclusion of exactly the Gatling (37) and Coffee Mill (38) (record order of `weapons.txt`: names at lines 1964 and 2017, 53 lines per record from record 40 at 2122); those models (and the Williams gun, id 12, line 639) exist as one-shot project stock (projects 10, 11). So the AI orders models the game says are not produced, at the floor availability (0.01, 64000) and the slowest delivery. The bug fix belongs to `fix/arms-bugs`; G9 never picks such a model either way (rule below).

**Decisions.**
1. **Re-arming priority (`RearmPriorityPatch`, Prefix + Finalizer on `AICampaign.CheckWeaponUpgrades(int alliance, bool isinitialization)`, snapshot-restore as #25):** for alliances 0/1 with a published allocation no older than 2 review days, outside the new-campaign pass (`isinitialization` → vanilla untouched) and while `ExecutorsPaused` is false, the Prefix saves `GameVars.alliance[a].unitlist` (the list object and its order) in `__state` and stably reorders it in place by the role of each unit's campaign group (`BattleUnits.GetCampaignGroup`, 82991; the allocation's `Assignment.Role`, a group-to-rank map built once per call from each assigned group's `allattachedunits`): MainEffort, Support, Reserve, Hold, CapitalGuard, DepotBuilder, Recon, (G8) Garrison, unassigned, then Recover and in-transit (`MoveTracker.InTransit` or `FormationFlags.InTransfer`) last; vanilla order is kept inside a rank. The Finalizer writes the saved order back even when vanilla throws. Vanilla's per-unit rule, stock checks, cascade and its W&L skip run unchanged; no unit is re-armed by Whiskey. No skip.
2. **Purchase pick (`OrdnancePurchasePatch`, two classes):** a Prefix + Finalizer on `CheckPurchaseWeapons(int _alliance)` sets/clears a `[ThreadStatic]` scope (alliance 0/1, fresh plan, flag on, executors not paused, not the CIC player's alliance); a Prefix on `WeaponList.PlaceWeaponOrder(int alliance, ref int weaponid, int quantity)` edits the argument while the scope is set for that alliance: `weaponid` becomes `OrdnancePicker.Pick(...)` over **vanilla's own candidate set for that lane** (same `isari` as vanilla's pick; researched or imported; no open order; WSF above the own lane average) **∩ orderable** (`(IsResearched(a) && productionmultiplier > 0) || IsImported(a)`, the player screen's rule computed for the right alliance). Trigger, amount (`quantity` is not touched), rating gate, one-order-per-model, capital check and cost charging stay vanilla's. When the intersection is empty G9 leaves vanilla's pick (and logs it once): without a skip G9 cannot cancel an order, and the unproduced-model fix is `fix/arms-bugs`'s. **No skipping Prefix: nothing needs user approval.**
3. **Score (`OrdnancePicker`, pure, per lane, every term normalized to [0, 1] over the candidates):** `ValuePerCostWeight 0.35 × battleValue / unitCost` + `DeliveryWeight 0.20 × (1 − deliveryYears / 2)` + `BattleValueWeight 0.20 × battleValue` + `StandardizationWeight 0.15 × standardization` + `EnemyEdgeWeight 0.10 × [battleValue beats the believed enemy lane quality]`, where `battleValue = range_yd × rpm_pract × firepower[0]/2 × accuracy³` (vanilla's WSF terms with rate of fire at full power, as tactical reload does, 119633; guns keep vanilla's tube-weight divisor), `unitCost = GetProductionFigures(a, id, quantity).cost / quantity` (read-only: `Economy.ConsumeWeapons(…, consumegoods: false)` is a dry run, 31486; the import share already carries the blockade factor, 64120-64124, so the cost and the delivery time are blockade-aware), `deliveryYears = GetDeliveryTime(GameVars.playeralliance, quantity, id, figures)` (the value vanilla's `PlaceWeaponOrder` itself uses), `standardization = Weapon.standardizationforalliance[a]` (64154-64190: equipped + stock of that model). **Fog:** the believed enemy lane quality is the confidence-weighted mean of `EnemyBelief.RifleQuality/GunQuality` (new fields recorded at sighting time by G1's detection runtime: the strength-weighted WSF of the seen group's armed units, `GetWeaponStrengthFactor(group)` 16195-16212 split by lane; never refreshed while unseen); G9 never reads another alliance's `averageweaponstrength*`. Ties by lower model id (deterministic).
4. **W&L.** The re-arm reorder never changes a unit's weapons, and vanilla already drops the player's units from the loop (16137-16144); in a CIC career the planner stands down for that alliance, so both patches leave it to vanilla. The purchase steer acts only for alliances the planner runs (it is alliance-level buying, never the player's own command's arms); a CIC player's alliance (vanilla's own 16263 gate, plus the stand-down) is never steered.
5. **Dependencies:** G4 (`Allocation`, roles), G5a (`MoveTracker.InTransit`), G8 (Garrison role rank), G1 (beliefs; G9 adds the two quality fields to `EnemyDetection`/`EnemyBelief` and fills them in `EnemyDetectionRuntime`), and `fix/arms-bugs` merged (cross-stage gates). It does not fit earlier: it needs G8's role and G1's belief fields, and both patches read the published allocation.

**Owned files:** create `Strategic/Planner/RearmPriority.cs` (pure: `RankOf(FormationRole?, bool recover, bool inTransit)`, `StableOrder(int[] ranks) → int[]`), `Strategic/Planner/OrdnancePicker.cs` (pure: `Candidate { Id, IsArtillery, Orderable, OpenOrder, Wsf, BattleValue, UnitCost, DeliveryYears, Standardization }`, `Pick(lane candidates, vanillaPick, believedEnemyQuality) → id`, `BattleValue(range, rpm, firepower0, accuracy, isArtillery, tubeWeight)`), `Strategic/Planner/OrdnanceRuntime.cs` (runtime: candidate facts from `GameVars.weapon`, believed enemy quality), `Patches/RearmPriorityPatch.cs`, `Patches/OrdnancePurchasePatch.cs` (two classes); modify `Strategic/Planner/CampaignPlannerContracts.cs` (`EnemyDetection`/`EnemyBelief` quality fields), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyDetectionRuntime.cs` (record them), `Strategic/Planner/EnemyArmyModel.cs` (carry them), `Plugin.cs` (bind `Enable Rearm Priority`, `Enable Ordnance Purchase Steering`, default on), `Telemetry/TelemetryTagPolicy.cs` (`Ordnance` Decision, `OrdnancePurchase` Write), `tools/smoke-situational.txt` (the purchase ids); test `OrdnanceTests.cs`.

**Steps:**
- [ ] Tests first (`OrdnanceTests`, red first): `RearmOrderFollowsAllocationRole`, `RearmOrderStableWithinRole`, `RecoverAndTransitRearmLast`, `UnassignedUnitsAfterAssignedRoles`, `RearmRestoresUnitListOrder` (pure snapshot/restore over a list), `RearmLeavesInitializationPassToVanilla`, `RearmStandsDownWithStaleOrMissingPlan`, `RearmStandsDownWhileJobMachinePaused`, `PurchaseKeepsVanillaLaneAndQuantity`, `PurchasePicksOnlyFromVanillaCandidatesThatAreOrderable`, `ResearchedUnproducedModelNeverPicked` (Gatling 37 / Coffee Mill 38 / Williams with `productionmultiplier` 0), `PurchaseLeavesVanillaPickWhenNoOrderableCandidate`, `PurchasePrefersValuePerCost`, `PurchaseRateOfFireCountsInFull` (a repeater with lower WSF beats a rifle-musket on `battleValue`), `PurchasePenalizesSlowDelivery`, `PurchasePrefersStandardizedModel`, `PurchaseBlockadeShowsInImportCost` (higher blockade → higher unit cost/delivery for an import-only model → lower score), `PurchaseEnemyEdgeFromBeliefsOnly` (source pin: `OrdnanceRuntime.cs`/`OrdnancePurchasePatch.cs` contain no `averageweaponstrength` read; an unseen enemy gives no edge term), `PurchaseNeverForCicPlayersAlliance`, `PurchaseDeterministicOnTies`, `DetectionRecordsSightedWeaponQuality`, `AlliancesTwoToFourIgnored`.
- [ ] Implement pure files, runtime, patches (scopes `campaign.patch.ordnance-rearm.prefix|finalizer`, `campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.scope.prefix|finalizer`, `campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.pick`; `PatchFirstFire.Mark` literals; the two purchase ids in `tools/smoke-situational.txt`: only when vanilla's trigger fires). Telemetry `[Ordnance] alliance=<a> rearm main=<n> support=<n> reserve=<n> hold=<n> capital=<n> last=<n>` on change; `[OrdnancePurchase] alliance=<a> lane=<guns|rifles> vanilla=<id> picked=<id> score=<s> cost=<c> delivery=<y> reason=<value|delivery|std|edge|vanilla-kept>` per order.
- [ ] Docs: `docs/patch-catalog.md` rows (ordinals at G7 merge order), `docs/bug-fixes/README.md` F3 status (owned by `fix/arms-bugs`), `docs/handoff.md`.
- [ ] Gates; commit `feat(campaign-planner): ordnance re-arming priority and scored weapon purchase [G9]`.

PROVE-MODE: harness (red first; mutation plants: rank table reversed → `RearmOrderFollowsAllocationRole` red; Finalizer restore skipped → `RearmRestoresUnitListOrder` red; orderable filter dropped → `ResearchedUnproducedModelNeverPicked` red; rate of fire at vanilla's 0.2 power → `PurchaseRateOfFireCountsInFull` red; enemy edge from `averageweaponstrength` → `PurchaseEnemyEdgeFromBeliefsOnly` red). In game: smoke below.

**Smoke (G9):**
1. `LOG`: `[first-fire] campaign.patch.ordnance-rearm.prefix` and `.finalizer` within the first game-day; `[first-fire] campaign.patch.ordnance-purchase.pick` once vanilla places an AI order (situational).
2. `SESSION`: `[Ordnance] alliance=1 rearm main=<n> …` rows; vanilla's debug log (`GameVars.DebugOwnLog`, when enabled) shows `upgrade weapon for unit …` lines for main-effort units before quiet-front units in the same tick.
3. `[OrdnancePurchase] alliance=1 lane=rifles vanilla=<id> picked=<id> …` with `picked` never a model whose order screen shows "Not produced"; vanilla's `AI:Placing rifle order … weaponid:<vanilla>` followed by `taking weapon order 1 id:<picked> qty:<vanilla's quantity>`.
4. W&L corps career: no `[Ordnance]`/`[OrdnancePurchase]` effect on the player's own command's units (their weapons change only by the player); W&L CIC career: no G9 rows for the player's alliance.
5. Rollback: `Enable Rearm Priority = false` → unit list order unchanged (no `[Ordnance]` rows); `Enable Ordnance Purchase Steering = false` → `picked` absent, vanilla's random pick.
